## DLC_LEVIATHANS_RECOMMENDATION_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADLC_LEVIATHANS_RECOMMENDATION_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§S《星神兽》§!故事包为太空帝国在星系中准备了全新的冒险和挑战。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:6`

### English

§SLeviathans§! is a story pack that fills the galaxy with new adventures and challenges for space-faring empires.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:8`

## DLC_LEVIATHANS_RECOMMENDATION_ROBOT_REC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADLC_LEVIATHANS_RECOMMENDATION_ROBOT_REC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们推荐§S《星神兽》§!，因为该帝国的特质与此 DLC 中以探索太空和对抗大型生物为核心的机制和内容吻合。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:7`

### English

We're recommending §SLeviathans§! as the makeup of this empire suits its mechanics and content - namely, exploring space and facing up to large Space Fauna.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:9`

## SHIP_GRAPHICS_SPACE_AMOEBA_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASHIP_GRAPHICS_SPACE_AMOEBA_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

一种缓慢地搏动着光芒韵律的凝胶状生漂浮命体。它的动作显示出一种怪异而复杂的智慧，但其行为目的仍是个谜。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:44`

### English

A drifting, gelatinous lifeform that pulses with a slow, luminous rhythm. Its movements suggest a strange, deliberate intelligence, though its purpose remains a mystery.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:48`

## SHIP_GRAPHICS_SPACE_AMOEBA_MOTHER_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASHIP_GRAPHICS_SPACE_AMOEBA_MOTHER_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这只巨大的个体比其同类更加巨大而古老，散发着某种静谧的引力。有人认为它能通过某种直觉或者更深层次的东西来引导较低等级的同类。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:45`

### English

Larger and older than others of its kind, this huge entity exudes a quiet gravity. Some believe it guides the lesser forms through instinct, or something deeper.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:49`

## SHIP_GRAPHICS_SPACE_WHALE_1_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASHIP_GRAPHICS_SPACE_WHALE_1_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这种宇生动物优雅地滑翔着，身上的生物发光追溯着远古星座的排列。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:46`

### English

This variant of the voidborne fauna glides with measured grace, its bioluminescent patterns tracing ancient constellations as it moves.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:50`

## SHIP_GRAPHICS_SPACE_WHALE_2_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASHIP_GRAPHICS_SPACE_WHALE_2_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这种生物巨大而迟缓，身上满是岁月的痕迹；外壳上四处可见结痂、伤痕，还有搭便车的附着生物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:47`

### English

Massive and slow-moving, this creature bears the marks of age; encrustations, scars, and hitchhiking organisms clinging to its vast hide.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:51`

## SHIP_GRAPHICS_SPACE_WHALE_3_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASHIP_GRAPHICS_SPACE_WHALE_3_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这个光滑的标本透着斑斓的光谱，它独自穿越离子风暴和星云。有人认为发光的航迹是它的一种交流方式。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:48`

### English

Sleek and spectral, this specimen travels alone through ion storms and nebulae. Some say its glowing trails are a form of communication.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:52`

## SHIP_GRAPHICS_SPACE_WHALE_4_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASHIP_GRAPHICS_SPACE_WHALE_4_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这头航宇巨兽的身后拖着银丝一般的长长卷须，在星光中闪闪发亮。它似乎受到高能区域的吸引，原因不明。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:49`

### English

Long tendrils drift behind this spacefaring beast, shimmering like gossamer in starlight. It seems drawn to high-energy regions, for reasons unknown.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:53`

## SHIP_GRAPHICS_SPACE_WHALE_5_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASHIP_GRAPHICS_SPACE_WHALE_5_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这种类型罕见且难以捉摸，它发出低频脉冲，与当地物质产生奇怪的共鸣。许多观察到它的人都产生了一种莫名的敬畏……或恐惧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:50`

### English

Rare and elusive, this type emits low-frequency pulses that resonate strangely with local matter. Many who observe it report inexplicable feelings of awe... or dread.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:54`

## SHIP_GRAPHICS_VOIDWORMS_SMALL_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASHIP_GRAPHICS_VOIDWORMS_SMALL_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这些实体如同失落语言的回声一般地在空间中迅速地蜿蜒穿梭。它们的形态似乎只有一部分固定在物质位面上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:51`

### English

Sinuous and rapid, these entities dart through space like echoes of a forgotten language. Their form seems only partially anchored to the material plane.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:55`

## SHIP_GRAPHICS_VOIDWORMS_MEDIUM_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASHIP_GRAPHICS_VOIDWORMS_MEDIUM_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

更大的虚空虫在宇宙中以令人不安的优雅扭动着。它的表面闪烁着无法解释的图案，蕴含着一种令人不适的美。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:52`

### English

Larger voidworms twist through the cosmos with unsettling grace. Their surfaces shimmer with patterns that defy explanation, evoking both beauty and unease.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:56`

## SHIP_GRAPHICS_VOIDWORMS_LARGE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASHIP_GRAPHICS_VOIDWORMS_LARGE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这条虚空虫巨大得超出理解，它的运动如同冰川一般冷峻。观测报告说称，距离靠近时会有时间失调和幻视的感觉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:53`

### English

Vast beyond reckoning, this voidworm moves with glacial certainty. Observers report temporal dissonance and fleeting visions when in close proximity.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:57`

## SHIP_GRAPHICS_CUTHOLOIDS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASHIP_GRAPHICS_CUTHOLOIDS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这些长有触角的古老生物沉默地漂流着，用看不见的眼睛注视着。它们的存在会导致仪器浮动、j精神烦躁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:54`

### English

These ancient and tentacled beings drift in silence, watching with unseen eyes. Their very presence causes instruments to falter and thoughts to fray at the edges.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:58`

## SHIP_GRAPHICS_CUTHOLOIDS_HATCHLING_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASHIP_GRAPHICS_CUTHOLOIDS_HATCHLING_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

一个年轻的样本，还在向其令人费解的形态发育的过程中。尽管尺寸很大，但却散发出一种令人不安的神秘宁静。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:55`

### English

A young specimen, still growing into its incomprehensible form. Despite its size, it radiates an uncanny stillness that unsettles the mind.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:59`

## SHIP_GRAPHICS_CUTHOLOIDS_JUVENILE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASHIP_GRAPHICS_CUTHOLOIDS_JUVENILE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这个个体既不是幼体，但也还没成熟，它诡异地行动，并散发着飘忽不定的光。有些人认为它会以一种我们不能理解的频率，将梦境释出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:56`

### English

No longer a hatchling, but not yet mature, this entity exudes erratic patterns of movement and light. Some suggest it dreams aloud, in frequencies we cannot understand.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:60`

## SHIP_GRAPHICS_VOIDWORMS_TITAN_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASHIP_GRAPHICS_VOIDWORMS_TITAN_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这些虚空虫相互缠绕，进行着摄人心魄的交配之舞，它们如同一个整体般移动与战斗，只为了替刚孵化的幼崽寻觅新的繁衍之地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:57`

### English

Locked in a mesmerizing mating dance, these Voidworms move and fight as one, in search of new breeding grounds for their hatchlings.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:61`

## SHIP_GRAPHICS_CRYSTAL_SHIP_SMALL_BLUE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASHIP_GRAPHICS_CRYSTAL_SHIP_SMALL_BLUE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

碎晶子体从母巢分离，凭借有限的推进手段穿越太空，寻找可供嵌入的星体以发展其晶体结构。多数子体将永远消失在虚空之中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:58`

### English

Shardlings Break off from their Nidus and travel through space with limited propulsion means to find stellar bodies that they can embed into to develop their crystalline structures. Most are lost to the void forever.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:62`

## SHIP_GRAPHICS_CRYSTAL_SHIP_MEDIUM_BLUE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASHIP_GRAPHICS_CRYSTAL_SHIP_MEDIUM_BLUE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

晶体碎片源自那些幸运觅食时撞击到足够大小小行星的水晶碎片生物。如今这些巨大的晶体飞船已接近觉醒，它们终将踏上旅程，寻找新的星体继续生长。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:59`

### English

Crystal shards come from shardlings fortunate enough to strike large enough asteroids when looking for sustenance. Now approaching sentience, these large crystalline ships will eventually set off in search of planetoids to grow once more.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:63`

## SHIP_GRAPHICS_CRYSTAL_SHIP_LARGE_BLUE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASHIP_GRAPHICS_CRYSTAL_SHIP_LARGE_BLUE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

水晶精髓已完全成熟，其表面能捕捉并折射光线，展现出令人目眩神迷的反光效果。尽管这些实体并非真正意义上的群居生物，但仍能观察到它们利用自身质量引导碎片幼体朝小行星方向移动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:60`

### English

Crystalline Quintessence are fully grown, and their surface catches and refracts light in a dazzling display of reflections. While not exactly communal, these entities have still been observed using their mass to steer shardlings towards asteroids.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:64`

## curator_government_desc

## leviathans.102.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.102.name&lang=en)

### 中文

与[From.GetName]的第一次接触

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:136`

### English

First Contact With the [From.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:151`

## leviathans.102a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.102a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

欢迎光临雪拉企业！\n\n我们是在[trader_system.GetName]恒星系对外运营的商业联合企业，主营贸易——我们雪拉企业坚信，贸易是文明的基石。\n\n如果您想进行贸易的话，请联系我们，我们会尽量满足您的一切需要。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:137`

### English

Welcome to XuraCorp!\n\nWe are a business conglomerate operating out of the [trader_system.GetName] system, specializing in trade - the very building blocks of civilization, as we like to say here at XuraCorp.\n\nPlease, do not hesitate to contact us if you're interested in making a good deal.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:152`

## leviathans.102b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.102b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我是里甘商业交易所的代表。我们以[trader_system.GetName]的空间站为基地，负责帮助各个团体建立完善的贸易渠道以达千年。\n\n如果您手头有多余的资源想要出售，找我们就对了，我们提供的价格保证让您满意。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:138`

### English

I speak on behalf of the Riggan Commerce Exchange. We have facilitated trade negotiations between interested parties on our station in the [trader_system.GetName] system for more than a thousand of your years.\n\nIf you happen to possess more resources than you need, we are more than willing to take it off your hands. You will be duly compensated, of course.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:153`

## leviathans.102c.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.102c.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

很高兴见到你们，朋友们！我代表穆塔根商人公会欢迎你们来到[trader_system.GetName]恒星系。我们公会由数百位独立的穆塔根商船舰长组成，他们勇敢地在太空中探索，寻找商机。\n\n您可以在我们的机构中出售多余的资源。如果您有意出售，请随时联系我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:139`

### English

Well met, friends! I represent the Muutagan Merchant Guild, and let me be the first to welcome you to the [trader_system.GetName] system. Our organization consists of hundreds of independent Muutagan merchant captains who brave the spaceways in search of the next good deal.\n\nAt our facilities, you will be able to trade away some of your excess resources. Please contact us if you are interested in such an arrangement.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:154`

## leviathans.100.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.100.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们希望能达成一个交易。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:140`

### English

We wish to make a trade.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:156`

## leviathans.100.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.100.b&lang=en)

### 中文

关闭显示器。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:141`

### English

Viewscreen off.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:157`

## leviathans.100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

雪拉企业

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:142`

### English

XuraCorp

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:159`

## leviathans.100a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.100a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

雪拉企业不会对任何客户抱有偏见。毕竟我们何德何能去对您的道德准则品头论足呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:143`

### English

We do not discriminate against prospective buyers here at XuraCorp. Who are we to judge the morals of your star nation?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:160`

## leviathans.100b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.100b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

您可能需要矿物来建造战舰，或者用它们来建造孤儿院！在雪拉企业，我们绝不会过问您打算如何使用我们的商品！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:144`

### English

Perhaps you need minerals for building warships. Perhaps you need them for building orphanages! Here at XuraCorp, we make it our business not to care!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:161`

## leviathans.100c.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.100c.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们绝不过问任何事情——我们只提供品质精良的物资，保证让您十分满意，时刻准备着为您的工业建设输送新鲜血液!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:145`

### English

No questions asked - just quality resources, ready to be pumped right into the arteries of your industry!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:162`

## leviathans.100d.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.100d.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

恕不退款，十分抱歉。这是公司的政策……这超出了我们的[xuracorp_country.species.GetHandNamePlural]掌控的范围。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:146`

### English

No refunds, I'm afraid. Company policy... It's out of my [xuracorp_country.species.GetHandNamePlural].

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:163`

## leviathans.100e.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.100e.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

客官您对本公司的非品牌蒸馏装备感兴趣吗？并不？那么微粒检测器呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:147`

### English

Can we interest you in some off-brand distillation equipment? No? How about particulate detectors?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:164`

## leviathans.100f.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.100f.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

欢迎联系雪拉企业，请问您需要什么服务呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:148`

### English

You have reached XuraCorp. How may we help you?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:165`

## leviathans.103.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.103.name&lang=en)

### 中文

里甘商业交易所

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:149`

### English

Riggan Commerce Exchange

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:167`

## leviathans.103a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.103a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的所有客户都是匿名交易的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:150`

### English

All of our clients remain anonymous.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:168`

## leviathans.103b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.103b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

您想知道我们的资源是从哪里来的？很抱歉……出于对所有客户——包括[Root.GetName]——的利益考虑，我们的货源必须要保密……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:151`

### English

Where do our resources come from? Trust me... It's best for all involved parties - including the [Root.GetName] - if we keep that little bit of information to ourselves...

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:169`

## leviathans.103c.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.103c.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不从事奴隶交易，绝不开先例。很抱歉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:152`

### English

We do not deal in slaves. Sorry, no exceptions.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:170`

## leviathans.103d.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.103d.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

里甘商业交易所欢迎您的到来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:153`

### English

The Riggan Commerce Exchange greets you.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:171`

## leviathans.103e.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.103e.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

千年老字号，诚信又可靠。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:154`

### English

A thousand years and we are still in business. If that doesn't speak to our credibility, I don't know what will.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:172`

## leviathans.103f.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.103f.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

数个驻扎在我们空间站的势力希望与[Root.GetName]贸易磋商，阁下的意见是？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:155`

### English

Several parties on our starbase have expressed interest in dealing with the [Root.GetName]. Shall we negotiate a deal?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:173`

## leviathans.104.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.104.name&lang=en)

### 中文

穆塔根商人公会

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:156`

### English

Muutagan Merchant Guild

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:175`

## leviathans.104a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.104a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

千年之前，穆塔根帝国从浩瀚星系之中汲取惊人财富，然而现在我们这个小小的财阀成了帝国的唯一遗产。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:157`

### English

Thousands of years ago, the Muutagan Empire spanned a good portion of the galaxy. We are all that remain.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:176`

## leviathans.104b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.104b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有传言穆塔根人可以敏锐地嗅到十二个秒差距以外的商机，这种说法实在是有些夸张了……说真的，最远只有八个秒差距而已。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:158`

### English

It is said that a Muutagan can smell a good deal from a distance of twelve parsecs. This is an exaggeration... In truth, the distance is only eight parsecs.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:177`

## leviathans.104c.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.104c.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

穆塔根商人公会欢迎您，打算来一发交易吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:159`

### English

The Muutagan Merchant Guild welcomes you. Would you like to make a deal?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:178`

## leviathans.104d.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.104d.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个穆塔根人和一个雷西达伦人一起走进一间酒吧……算了，当我没说，这么交流实在欠妥，请您原谅。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:160`

### English

A Muutagan and a Rixidaran walk into a bar... Never mind, this does not make for appropriate conversation. I beg your forgiveness.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:179`

## leviathans.104e.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.104e.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

您好，[Root.GetSpeciesName]。您是来这里找我们交易的吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:161`

### English

Greetings, [Root.GetSpeciesName]. You are here to trade with us, yes?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:180`

## leviathans.104f.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.104f.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

\"折扣\"这个词对于我们的人民来说是一种亵渎。在和我们交流的时候请谨记这一点。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:162`

### English

The word "discount" is considered a profanity among my people. Please keep this in mind when engaging in conversation with us.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:181`

## leviathans.125.desc.z1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.125.desc.z1&lang=en)

### 中文

我们有一位才华横溢的执行经理随时待命，准备前往[Root.GetName]，解决您任何一片星域的困境。$r_trade$的事就交给专家来解决吧！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:207`

### English

We have a talented executive standing by, ready to migrate to the [Root.GetName] and get any of your sectors back out of the red. Entrust your $r_trade$ to an expert!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:231`

## leviathans.125.desc.z1.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.125.desc.z1.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们有一位才华横溢的高管随时待命，准备……嗯？你说什么？\n\n“买方须知”很好，但是我还是有义务通知您，我们的顾问真的只专注于$r_trade$。我不确定这是否适合你，毕竟……您与这一概念似乎有些格格不入。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:208`

### English

We have a talented executive standing by, ready to... Hm. Are you sure?\n\n'Buyer beware' is all well and good, but still: I feel obliged to inform you that our consultants really only specialize in $r_trade$. I'm not sure they would be a good fit for you, given your... unusual relationship with that concept.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:232`

## leviathans.125.z1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.125.z1&lang=en)

## leviathans.131.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.131.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这种§Y易爆微粒§!是里甘的珍贵调味料。它以独一无二的味道在宇宙中声名远扬——它独一无二的爆炸性也同样声名远扬。我们只卖给最宝贵的贸易伙伴，[Root.GetName]便是其一。\n\n只要你接受这合理的价格，我们愿意在十个标准年里向你出口易爆微粒。 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:240`

### English

The §YVolatile Mote§! is a prized Riggan condiment. It is renowned throughout the galaxy for its distinct flavor, which is unlike any other - and its explosiveness, which is also unlike any other. We sell it only to our most valued trading partners, among which we count the [Root.GetName].\n\nIf you can meet our modest price, we are willing to export Volatile Motes to you for a period of ten standard years.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:274`

## leviathans.131.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.131.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

你不会后悔的，除非您吃了太多香料……那样的话您会有很小的几率发生自燃，不过也只有很小的几率，我很肯定。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:241`

### English

You will not be sorry. Unless you consume too much spice, of course... Then there is a small chance that you will spontaneously combust. A very small chance, to be sure.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:275`

## leviathans.130.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.130.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这是我们雪拉企业引以为傲的产品。我们的医疗部门最近开始精炼一种性质独特的气体，可以使适应外星环境的艰难过程变得轻松不少。不过它很可能无法与外星生理机能相容！\n\n我们愿意在十个标准年里向你出口§Y异星天然气§!，之后我们就必须重新签订协议。 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:242`

### English

This is a product that we here at XuraCorp are particularly proud of. Our Medical Division has recently begun refining a gas with unique properties that will significantly ease the often difficult process of adapting to a foreign planetary environment. It is however unlikely to be compatible with alien physiology!\n\nWe are willing to export our §YExotic Gas§! to you for a period of ten standard years, after which the contract will have to be renegotiated.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:277`

## leviathans.130.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.130.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

雪拉企业就该这样做生意啊！我们的货船会马上开始发货。\n\n§L如果您购买了这个产品，也就等同于默认雪拉企业将不对使用这个产品所产生的任何副作用负责，这包括但不限于皮肤刺激，精神病发作，眼球变色，侵略性的突变，殖民地居民自杀率增加，预知恍惚，生物圈资源耗尽，高危奇异物质产生，微型黑洞生成，谷物升降机爆炸，社会崩溃，大规模灭绝事件，意外的超新星爆炸，局部或全面的星际帝国崩溃、突发的伽玛射线暴，时间旅行悖论，真空亚稳态事件，通用熵，时空连续性的崩溃……§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:243`

### English

That's the XuraCorp way! Our freighters will begin making deliveries immediately.\n\n§LBy purchasing this product, you agree that XuraCorp cannot be held responsible for any adverse effects that may result from its use, including but not limited to: skin irritation, psychotic episodes, ocular discoloration, aggressive mutation, increased colonist suicide rates, precognitive trances, biosphere depletion, dangerous strangelet production, micro black hole generation, grain elevator explosions, societal collapse, mass extinction events, unexpected supernova detonations, the complete or partial destruction of your star nation, sudden gamma-ray bursts, time travel paradoxes, vacuum metastability events, universal entropy, the collapse of the space-time continuum...§!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:278`

## leviathans.132.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.132.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

史拉卡-杜——也被我族之外的人称为§Y稀有水晶§!——已经成了我们整个文明的象征。当这些“歌唱”水晶震动时，会散发一种独特的旋律，可对大多数智慧生物起到镇静作用。\n\n尽管我们通常不会与外人分享史拉卡杜的神奇，但可以为你开个特例。如果你付得起，我们愿意在十个标准年里向你出口我们的水晶。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:244`

### English

Shi'rakkat-Du - or §YRare Crystals§! as they are known to those who are not of our species - have come to symbolize our entire civilization. When these "singing" crystals vibrate, they emit a unique musical tone that has a calming effect on most sapients.\n\nAlthough we typically do not share the wonders of the Shi'rakkat-Du with outsiders, we are willing to make an exception in your case. If you can afford it, we are willing to export our crystals to you for a period of ten years.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:280`

## leviathans.132.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.132.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

很好。我们的商人将立即向你的殖民地运送水晶。愿史拉卡杜对你歌唱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:245`

### English

Excellent. Our merchants will begin shipping the crystals to your colonies at once. May the Shi'rakkat-Du sing to you.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:281`

## leviathans.141

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.141&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]被摧毁

### English

[From.GetName] Destroyed

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:299`

## leviathans.141.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.141.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的舰队摧毁了[From.GetName]的总部。从他们的太空基地的残骸中尽可能回收有价值物资的行动也已如火如荼地展开。我们已经获得了大量资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:262`

### English

Our fleet assets have destroyed the [From.GetName] headquarters. Efforts are well under way to recover as many valuables as possible from the shattered remnants of their starbase. We have gained a large amount of resources.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:300`

## leviathans.142.desc.first

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.142.desc.first&lang=en)

### 中文

位于[trader_system.GetName]星系的§Y雪拉企业§!的总部最近被摧毁了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:267`

### English

The corporate headquarters of §YXuraCorp§! in the [trader_system.GetName] system was recently destroyed.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:306`

## leviathans.142.desc.known

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.142.desc.known&lang=en)

### 中文

有报告认为袭击者来自[trader_destroyer.GetName]！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:268`

### English

Reports suggest the attack force belongs to the [trader_destroyer.GetName]!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:307`

## leviathans.142.desc.unknown

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.142.desc.unknown&lang=en)

### 中文

根据我们的情报，这次行动是由我们尚未知晓的势力进行的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:269`

### English

According to our intel, this act was perpetrated by forces as yet unknown to us.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:308`

## leviathans.142.desc.second

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.142.desc.second&lang=en)

### 中文

少数幸存的董事会成员已宣布破产，该公司的剩余资产正在进行清算。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:270`

### English

The few surviving board members have declared bankruptcy, and the corporation's remaining assets are being liquidated.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:309`

## leviathans.142.desc.trade

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.142.desc.trade&lang=en)

### 中文

不幸的是，雪拉集团总部的异星天然气生产设施被摧毁，我们失去了这一战略资源的获取渠道。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:271`

### English

Unfortunately, their production facilities for the Exotic Gas were destroyed along with the XuraCorp HQ, and we will no longer have access to this strategic resource channel.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:310`

## leviathans.142.a

## leviathans.143.desc.first

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.143.desc.first&lang=en)

### 中文

属于§Y里甘商业交易所§!的恒星基地在[trader_system.GetName]星系被毁。这个自由前哨已经为星际文明之间的贸易提供便利超过一个千年，但如今已不复存在。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:278`

### English

The starbase belonging to the §YRiggan Commerce Exchange§! has been destroyed in the [trader_system.GetName] system. This free outpost had facilitated trade between interstellar civilizations for over a millennium, but no longer.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:318`

## leviathans.143.desc.known

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.143.desc.known&lang=en)

### English

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:319`

### 中文

有报告说位于[trader_system.GetName]星系的§Y穆塔根商人公会§!的总部被摧毁了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:288`

Reports are coming in that the headquarters of the §YMuutagan Merchant Guild§! in the [trader_system.GetName] system has been pulverized.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:329`

## leviathans.144.desc.known

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.144.desc.known&lang=en)

### 中文

看来这次袭击的背后是隶属于[trader_destroyer.GetName]的势力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:289`

### English

Forces belonging to the [trader_destroyer.GetName] appear to be behind this attack.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:330`

## leviathans.144.desc.unknown

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.144.desc.unknown&lang=en)

### 中文

这些袭击者似乎来自于一个我们尚未知晓的帝国。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:290`

### English

The attackers appear to hail from an empire which is, as yet, unknown to us.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:331`

## leviathans.144.desc.second

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.144.desc.second&lang=en)

### 中文

存活下来的少数穆塔根商队舰长四散于全星系中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:291`

### English

The few surviving Muutagan merchant captains have scattered across the galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:332`

## leviathans.144.desc.trade

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.144.desc.trade&lang=en)

### 中文

不幸的是，这意味着他们已经无法继续生产珍贵的稀有水晶了，我们失去了这一战略资源的获取渠道。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:292`

### English

Unfortunately, this means an end to their production of the prized Rare Crystals. We will no longer have access to this strategic resource channel.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:333`

## leviathans.144.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.144.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真是严重的贸易事故。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:293`

### English

A significant trade disruption.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:334`

## leviathans.144.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.144.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的水晶啊！这件事情绝不能就这么算了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:294`

### English

Our crystals! This must not go unanswered!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:335`

## leviathans.150.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.150.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

亲爱的客户，我们很遗憾的通知你，我们同[Root.GetName]的异星天然气协定已经到期。如果你有多余的能量币，我们当然愿意再延长十年的交易…

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:295`

### English

Dearest customer, we regret to inform you that the trade deal we negotiated for the export of Exotic Gas to the worlds of the [Root.GetName] has run its course. We are of course more than willing to extend the deal for another ten years if you have the energy credits to spare...

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:337`

## leviathans.150.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.150.a&lang=en)

### 中文

很好。我们希望延长这笔交易。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:296`

### English

Very well. We wish to extend the deal.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:338`

## leviathans.150.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.150.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们现在用不着你们的气体了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:297`

### English

We can do without your gas for now.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:339`

## leviathans.150.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.150.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的资金无法延续这个交易。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:298`

### English

We can't afford to continue this deal.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:340`

## leviathans.151.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.151.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的协议已经到期，[Root.GetSpeciesName]。我们在过去的十年里向你运送了易爆微粒。请问你想续约亦或者结束这一安排？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:299`

### English

We have reached the end of our agreement, [Root.GetSpeciesName]. We have shipped Volatile Motes to your worlds for ten years. Should we continue for another ten, or do you wish to end this arrangement?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:341`

## leviathans.151.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.151.a&lang=en)

### 中文

让我们继续，这些微粒应当保持流通。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:300`

### English

Let us continue. The motes must flow.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:342`

## leviathans.151.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.151.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们现在不需要你的香料了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:301`

### English

We have no need of your spice for now.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:343`

## leviathans.152.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.152.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

时光易逝，不是吗，[Root.GetSpeciesName]？我们之间的稀有水晶贸易已经十年了。你希望再签署一个十年的贸易协定么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:302`

### English

The years just fly by, don't they, [Root.GetSpeciesName]? It has now been ten years since we arranged our trade deal for Rare Crystals. Do you wish to extend the deal for another decade?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:344`

## leviathans.152.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.152.a&lang=en)

### 中文

是的。让我们继续这个交易。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:303`

### English

Yes. Let us continue this deal.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:345`

## leviathans.152.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.152.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们现在不需要你们的水晶了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:304`

### English

We will pass on your crystals for now.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:346`

## leviathans.156.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.156.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你们鲁莽的袭击将雪拉企业的财产和雇员置于危险当中，我们别无选择，只能终止我们之间的贸易协定，而且我们不会退款。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:305`

### English

Your reckless attacks have put XuraCorp property and employees at risk. We have no choice but to terminate our current trade agreement. There will be no refunds.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:348`

## leviathans.156.desc.notrade

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.156.desc.notrade&lang=en)

### 中文

你们鲁莽的袭击威胁到了雪拉企业的财产和雇员的安全。最好记住，良好的客户关系是双向的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:306`

### English

Your reckless attack endangers XuraCorp property and employees alike. You would do well to remember that good customer relations go both ways.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:349`

## leviathans.156.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.156.a&lang=en)

### 中文

终止通讯。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:307`

### English

End communications.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:350`

## leviathans.157a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.157a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

很抱歉，根据公司规定，我们不能和海盗进行贸易。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:308`

### English

I'm afraid it's against company policy to do business with pirates.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:351`

## leviathans.157b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.157b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不能接受你们这样的客户。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:309`

### English

You are not an acceptable customer.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:352`

## leviathans.157c.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.157c.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们和你们没什么好谈的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:310`

### English

We have nothing to discuss with you.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:353`

## leviathans.159.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.159.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你们竟然对我们的太空站发动袭击，不可原谅！我们不会再给你们运送货物了，明白吗？不会再给你们运送辛香料了！我们之间的贸易协定就此作废。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:311`

### English

Your attacks against our station are intolerable! You are cut off, do you understand? You may consider our trade agreement null and void.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:354`

## leviathans.159.desc.notrade

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.159.desc.notrade&lang=en)

### 中文

这次攻击无缘无故，毫无理由！我们绝不会容忍这样的行径！请你自行挂断吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:312`

### English

This attack is unprovoked and uncalled for! We will not stand for it. Consider yourselves cut off.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:355`

## leviathans.160a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.160a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你们的人全是强盗和恶徒，我们将和你们彻底撇清关系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:313`

### English

You are a nation of bandits and cutthroats. We will have nothing to do with you.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:356`

## leviathans.160b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.160b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

海盗鼠辈。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:314`

### English

Pirate scum.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:357`

## leviathans.160c.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.160c.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你们的进攻行为是不可原谅的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:315`

### English

Your attacks are unforgivable.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:358`

## leviathans.162.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.162.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

啊，海盗终于展现出本来面目了！你们别想再从我们这里得到水晶了！任何一个穆塔根人都不会再和[Root.GetName]做交易了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:316`

### English

Ah, so the pirates shows their true colors! You can forget about our trade deals! No Muutagan will do business with the [Root.GetName] again.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:359`

## leviathans.162.desc.notrade

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.162.desc.notrade&lang=en)

### 中文

你竟敢攻击我们？搞清楚点，你这海盗：我们最瞧不起的就是这样的野蛮行径。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:317`

### English

You dare to attack us?! Know this, pirates: we do not stand for such barbarism.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:360`

## leviathans.163a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.163a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你们这群破坏分子！我们是不会忘记你们的残酷行径的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:318`

### English

You are terrorists! Do not think we will forget your aggression against our people.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:361`

## leviathans.163b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.163b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

穆塔根人可是很记仇的，[Root.GetSpeciesName]。我们绝不会忘记你们的恶行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:319`

### English

You will find that Muutagans have very long memories, [Root.GetSpeciesName]. We never forget a slight.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:362`

## leviathans.163c.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.163c.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们再也不会信任[Root.GetName]了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:320`

### English

We will never trust the [Root.GetName] again.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:363`

## ENCLAVE_DIPLOMACY_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AENCLAVE_DIPLOMACY_TITLE&lang=en)

### 中文

城邦外交

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:324`

### English

Enclave Diplomacy

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:369`

## leviathans.295.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.295.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有客人来了！这真是令人欢欣鼓舞！欧，请原谅我的失礼。我想是该自我介绍一下了。我们是艺术家联盟。\n\n我们的成员都全身心地将自己的生命投身于艺术、音乐、文化，以及其他任何让智慧生命体的存在有所意义的事业。如果您愿意分享我们创造的奇迹，请不要犹豫，赶紧联系我们！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:325`

### English

A visitor! This is a joyous occasion indeed! Oh, forgive my manners. I believe introductions are in order. We are the Artisan Troupe.\n\nOur members have dedicated their lives to the pursuit of art, music, culture and all the other things which makes existence bearable to a sapient being. Please, if you would like to share in the wonder of our creations, do not hesitate to contact us!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:371`

## leviathans.300.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.300.name&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y太空城邦：§!艺术家联盟

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:326`

### English

§YEnclave:§! The Artisan Troupe

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:373`

## leviathans.300.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.300.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你好！我是艺术家联盟的首席大师，衷心祝愿你今天也是才思如泉涌。\n\n我们能为您做什么？为您打造一个用于欣赏的艺术作品？或者您希望赞助我们城邦的充满异域之美的创作？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:327`

### English

Greetings! I, the headmaster of the Artisan Troupe, wish you a creative day.\n\nWhat can we do you for? Perhaps produce a work of art for you to enjoy? Or maybe you wish to sponsor our enclave in its creation of otherworldly beauty?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:374`

## leviathans.300a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.300a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

嗯？噢，请原谅，我不小心陷入了冥想状态。有时候我的这种天赋有点……太强势了。我们还有什么能够为你做的吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:328`

### English

Hm? Oh, please excuse my reverie. Sometimes this muse of mine is simply... overpowering. Is there anything else we can do for you?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:375`

## leviathans.300b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.300b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你是否一度为极度的壮丽而驻足惊叹？我们如此，而你们亦然。这是何等的缘分！灵感来了，我得为这写一首歌。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:329`

### English

Have you ever stopped to wonder at the sheer majesty of it all? Here we are, and there you are. What fantastic odds! I feel a ballad coming on.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:376`

## leviathans.300c.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.300c.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

喂？你好？啊，是你！请原谅——我正好卡在了一个特别难以推敲的诗节上面。有什么事吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:330`

### English

Yes? Hello? Ah, it's you! Forgive me; I was stuck chasing a particularly elusive stanza. How can I help?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:377`

## leviathans.300.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.300.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们想委托创作一件§Y艺术品§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:331`

### English

We want to commission an §Yart piece§!.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:378`

## leviathans.300.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.300.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

很遗憾，我们不能再提供更多艺术品了，否则就相当于承认您之前买的那些并不够好。我们也要考虑自己的诚意，敬请谅解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:332`

### English

Sadly we cannot offer any more, lest we come to believe that you didn't appreciate the ones you already purchased. We have our integrity to think about, you see.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:379`

## leviathans.300.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.300.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们想要成为你们的§Y赞助者§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:333`

### English

We wish to become your §Ypatron§!.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:380`

## leviathans.300.b.stop

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.300.b.stop&lang=en)

### 中文

我们想要§Y取消对你们的赞助§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:334`

### English

We wish to §Ycancel our patronage§!.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:381`

## leviathans.300.b.stop.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.300.b.stop.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§R停止§!每月的£opinion£评价收益\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:335`

### English

§RStops§! the monthly £opinion£ Opinion gain\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:382`

## leviathans.300.b.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.300.b.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

我明白了，真是很遗憾，[root.GetSpeciesAdj]，但我们还是要感谢你们一开始的援助。\n\n再见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:336`

### English

Oh, I see. That is unfortunate, [root.GetSpeciesAdj], but we thank you for your initial support.\n\nGoodbye.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:383`

## leviathans.300.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.300.c&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们所有的星球上组织一场§Y节日盛典§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:337`

### English

Organize a §Yfestival§! across our worlds.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:384`

## leviathans.300.c.allow

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.300.c.allow&lang=en)

### 中文

$requires_enclave_opinion$§G+50§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:338`

### English

$requires_enclave_opinion$ §G+50§!.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:385`

## leviathans.300.c.outofstock

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.300.c.outofstock&lang=en)

### 中文

我们每20年才能主办一次§Y星球盛典§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:339`

### English

We may only host one §YFestival of Worlds§! every 20 years.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:386`

## leviathans.300.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.300.d&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y告诉我更多§!有关于你们艺术家联盟的信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:340`

### English

§YTell us more§! about your troupe.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:387`

## leviathans.300.d.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.300.d.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

我们是艺术家联盟。我们联盟包括好几个空间站，里面居住着来自不同世界的艺术天才。他们渴望在这片安宁的避风港创造出真正的美。他们带来了全星系的不同思想和方法论，汇聚一堂我们或许可以发现真我的最终形态。\n\n我们为真正的艺术爱好者提供各类服务。如果你帮助赞助我们的工作，我们甚至可能为你的人民组织更盛大的活动！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:341`

### English

We are the Artisan Troupe. The troupe consists of several stations inhabited by creatives from many worlds, all unified in the desire to create new beauty in these safe havens. They bring their ideas and methods from all around the galaxy, and together we uncover the ultimate expressions of the self!\n\nWe provide many services to those who wish to enjoy art. If you help sponsor our work we might even be willing to organize larger events for your people!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:388`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.300.e&lang=en)

你们还有什么能耐？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:342`

### English

What else can you do?

## leviathans.301.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.301.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

艺术家联盟拒绝回应我们的招呼。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:346`

### English

The Artisan Troupe won't respond to our hails.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:394`

## leviathans.301.desc.unknown

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.301.desc.unknown&lang=en)

### 中文

别说翻译了，只要我们发给[From.GetName]讯息，他们立马关闭所有通讯频道，拒绝与我们交流。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:347`

### English

Translations aside, once we exchanged our information with [From.GetName] they closed down all channels and refused to speak to us.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:395`

## leviathans.301.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.301.a&lang=en)

### 中文

他们好大的胆子啊？！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:348`

### English

How dare they?!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:396`

## leviathans.304a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.304a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

非常感谢你！噢，这样高尚的行为，势必将鼓舞更多的艺术作品现身这个宇宙。我们坚信你会十分满意的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:349`

### English

Thank you so very much! Ah, such a noble act it is to help bring more art into the universe. We are confident you will be satisfied.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:398`

## leviathans.304b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.304b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

因为你今日的所作所为，星辰神龛今天闪耀得更加明亮了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:350`

### English

The stars shine that bit more brightly by your actions here today!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:399`

## leviathans.304c.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.304c.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们知道你们[root.GetSpeciesAdj]存在与众不同之处。讲真，你们一族真是太可口了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:351`

### English

We knew there was something special about you [root.GetSpeciesAdj]. Truly, you are beings of refined taste.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:400`

## leviathans.305.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.305.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

好极了！我们很乐意将我们的一件创作送给你们，这肯定会给你的帝国的人民带去欢乐的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:352`

### English

Fabulous! We'd be glad to entrust you with one of our creations. It is sure to bring joy to the people of your empire.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:402`

## leviathans.305.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.305.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

解锁一项可在殖民地实施的§S£decision£$decision_art_monument$§!§Y决议§!，并带来如下效果：\n£amenities£ $MOD_PLANET_AMENITIES_MULT$ §G+15%§!\n$MOD_PLANET_IMMIGRATION_PULL_MULT$：§G+50%§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:353`

### English

Gain access to an §S£decision£ $decision_art_monument$§! §YDecision§! that can be enacted on a colony with the following effects:\n£amenities£ $MOD_PLANET_AMENITIES_MULT$ §G+15%§!\n$MOD_PLANET_IMMIGRATION_PULL_MULT$: §G+50%§!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:403`

## leviathans.305.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.305.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

太好了！这份杰作将快马加鞭地送达你的存储设施。只需要让你的行星管理部门在任何你希望的行星上颁布§Y决议§!。\n\n我们衷心感谢您的惠顾。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:354`

### English

Excellent! The piece will be sent to your storage facility post-haste. Simply ask your planetary administration to enact the §YDecision§! on any planet you wish.\n\nWe humbly thank you for your patronage.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:404`

## leviathans.330.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.330.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

举办这等规模的星球盛典，那需要我们所有的艺术家都使出浑身解数。不是一件容易的工作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:355`

### English

Organizing a festival of that scale would require all of our artists to bring their talent to bear. Not an easy job, nor a cheap one.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:406`

## leviathans.330.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.330.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

很好！我们很乐意为你举办这伟大的星球盛典！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:356`

### English

Excellent! We will be delighted to create this magnificent Festival of Worlds!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:407`

## leviathans.310.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.310.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这将对我们大有裨益，我们可以尝试新的想法，还能在创作的过程中承担更多的风险。我们有一个历时§Y十年§!的项目，但是需要一次性事前支付。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:357`

### English

That would be very helpful, allowing us to try new ideas and take more risks in our creation process. We have a §Yten year§! program with a one-time up-front cost.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:409`

## leviathans.310.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.310.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

每月§Y艺术家联盟§!获得§G£opinion£评价+1§!\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:358`

### English

Gain §G£opinion£ Opinion +1§! with the §YArtisan Troupe§! each month\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:410`

## leviathans.310.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.310.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

非常感谢你们！有了你们慷慨的捐助，我们就能更好的扩展艺术的概念了！当然，我们也会给所有的赞助者们按时发放进度通讯。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:359`

### English

Thank you very much! Now, with your generous contribution, we will be able to expand on the concept of art itself! We will of course also send out a regular newsletter to all patrons.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:411`

## leviathans.307.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.307.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的经济不再能维持一个艺术家联盟的赞助人身份了。他们将不再收到来自我们帝国的资金。这将会恶化双边关系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:360`

### English

Our economy cannot support being a patron of the Artisan Troupe anymore. They will no longer receive funding from our empire. This will surely sour our relationship with the enclave.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:412`

## leviathans.307.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.307.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§R移除§!帝国修正§Y$enclave_artist_patron$§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:361`

### English

§RRemoves§! the empire modifier §Y$enclave_artist_patron$§!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:413`

## leviathans.335.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.335.name&lang=en)

### 中文

乐极生悲

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:362`

### English

The Outrage

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:415`

## leviathans.335.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.335.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

艺术家联盟主办的§Y星球盛典§!，曾经一度，给[Root.GetName]的人们带去了欢乐。哎呀，这样的事情注定不会长久。\n\n在[this_planet.GetName]上，节日活动引发了人群的大规模踩踏事件，致使数千名公民死亡。目前尚不清楚究竟是怎样的情况导致了这一出家破人亡的惨剧，但是人民对于我们的政府和盛典主办方的怨恨与日俱增。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:363`

### English

The §YFestival of Worlds§! organized by the Artisan Troupe had, for a time, brought joy to the people of the [Root.GetName]. Alas, it was not meant to last.\n\nOn [this_planet.GetName], festival activities have resulted in a huge crowd stampeding and killing thousands of citizens. It is not clear what circumstances led to this tragic loss of life, but there is now growing resentment towards our administration and the festival's organizers alike.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:416`

## leviathans.335.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.335.b&lang=en)

### 中文

转移媒体的注意力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:364`

### English

Divert the media's attention.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:417`

## leviathans.336.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.336.name&lang=en)

### 中文

交织的喜悦

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:365`

### English

The Joy

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:418`

## leviathans.336.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.336.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于§Y星球盛典§!所带来的浓厚的快乐氛围，一些盛典的举办者们爱上了我们的生活方式。他们声称希望节日过后能够留下来；在此定居，成为我们帝国的一员。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:366`

### English

Due to the overabundance of happiness being spread by the §YFestival of Worlds§!, some of the event's organizers have begun to fall in love with our way of life. They claim they wish to remain here; to settle down and become a part of our empire.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:419`

## leviathans.336.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.336.a&lang=en)

### 中文

让他们留下。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:367`

### English

Let them stay.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:420`

## leviathans.336.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.336.b&lang=en)

### 中文

他们必须离开。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:368`

### English

They should leave.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:421`

## leviathans.337.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.337.name&lang=en)

### 中文

背叛

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:369`

### English

The Betrayal

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:422`

## leviathans.337.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.337.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y星球盛典§!突然停止了。艺术家联盟已经收拾好行李离开了，一句解释都没有。\n\n我们彻底搜索他们的营地和演出舞台后发现，大量存放的资源已经消失。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:370`

### English

The §YFestival of Worlds§! has abruptly stopped. The Artisan Troupe has packed their bags and left, without saying a word about why.\n\nThorough searches of their campsites and arenas have revealed only that a substantial quantity of resources has disappeared.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:423`

## leviathans.337.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.337.a&lang=en)

### 中文

一群小偷！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:371`

### English

Thieves!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:424`

### English

The Artisan Troupe will refuse any communique for 10 years.

这倒也是出好戏。

All part of the fun.

原谅艺术家联盟的违约行为。

The Artisan Troupe are forgiven for their transgressions.

Tent Heist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acultural_clemency&lang=en)

文化仁慈

Cultural Clemency

## leviathans.315.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.315.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尊敬的[Root.GetRulerName][Root.GetRulerTitle]：十年前[Root.GetName]承诺赞助艺术家联盟，并已如约履行。\n\n上一期的赞助已经接近尾声，谨慎起见请允许我们再度询问：您是否愿意续订全星系最精美的艺术和工艺品？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:378`

### English

Esteemed [Root.GetRulerTitle] [Root.GetRulerName]: a decade ago the [Root.GetName] pledged to support the Artisan Troupe, and so you have.\n\nThe time of your patronage is coming to an end, but it would be remiss of us not to ask: would you care to renew your subscription to the finest arts and crafts in the galaxy?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:431`

## leviathans.321.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.321.name&lang=en)

### 中文

赞助者简报

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:379`

### English

Patron Newsletter

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:433`

## leviathans.321.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.321.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这里是我们为诸位赞助者提供的最新简讯：\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:380`

### English

Here follows the latest newsletter for all our patrons:\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:434`

## leviathans.321.desc.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.321.desc.a&lang=en)

### 中文

很不幸城邦中最近没能产出什么作品。大部分的艺术家都深陷忧郁之中，皆因难以迸发出创作的灵感。这种情绪十分棘手，但也有益处。毕竟创作还是要花一定时间的。\n\n任何为了帮助这些艺术家重燃创作之火而进行的赞助，都能大大改进我们目前的创作窘境。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:381`

### English

Sadly nothing much has been produced in the enclave. Most of the artists have fallen into a deep melancholy from where no inspiration can be drawn. The mood is dire, but hopeful. Creation takes time, after all.\n\nAny donations that might help the group rekindle their interest in the material plane would be of tremendous help.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:435`

## leviathans.321.desc.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.321.desc.b&lang=en)

### 中文

整个城邦最近几个月里甚是忙碌。我们的厅堂因为居民发明的新技术而议论纷纷，这项技术能够向使用者传播嗅觉。这个非凡的器械注定能改变一切。\n\n为了进一步发展这个想法我们需要更多的捐助。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:382`

### English

The enclave has been busy the last couple of months. Our halls are abuzz with a new kind of technology invented by a resident, capable of transmitting sensations of smell to a user. This remarkable piece of machinery will surely change everything.\n\nIn order to further develop this idea we will require more donations.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:436`

## leviathans.321.desc.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.321.desc.c&lang=en)

### 中文

协会最近一直处于欢脱的氛围之中。嗅媒感知设备的发明使得一个又一个艺术家将其重心转移到为其创造全新体验上。到目前为止，他们的工作所取得的最佳成果是一首通过嗅觉信号传递的管弦乐曲。\n\n我们的工作还在继续，同时十分乐意接受任何形式的捐助。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:383`

### English

The enclave is in a frisky mood. The creation of the Scentilus Rift has caused many an artist to shift their primary focus to creating new experiences for it. So far the most noteworthy to come out of this endeavor is an orchestral piece conveyed via scent.\n\nOur work continues and any donations would be gladly accepted.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:437`

## leviathans.321.desc.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.321.desc.d&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经放弃继续创建以气味为中心的媒介了。这是个拙劣的想法，除了浪费时间并让室内布满臭气之外，没有任何意义。\n\n为了能重新回到正确的创作之路，我们需要你们的赞助，我们感谢你们的继续支持！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:384`

### English

We have abandoned the attempts to create scent-centered media. It was a foolish idea and has brought nothing but wasted time and unpleasant odors.\n\nIn order to find our way back again we will need donations from our trusted patrons. We thank you for your continued support!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:438`

## leviathans.321.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.321.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们乐意提供资助。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:385`

### English

We would like to donate.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:439`

## leviathans.321.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.321.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们现在担负不起这个价格。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:387`

### English

We cannot afford to donate now.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:441`

## leviathans.321.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.321.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们希望取消简讯订阅。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:388`

### English

We wish to unsubscribe from this newsletter.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:442`

## leviathans.322.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.322.name&lang=en)

### 中文

赞助的回报

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:389`

### English

Patrons with Benefits

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:443`

## leviathans.322.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.322.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你好，赞助人[Root.GetRulerName]！我们构想出了一种华丽的新型建筑，专门用来促进人们之间的互相理解——§Y文化中心§!。它的功能得益于融合了大众对彼此的潜意识偏见和艺术的偏好，然后粉碎了这些并以一种新的统一形式呈现出来。\n\n你们想不想购买这个建筑？这一定会对你们帝国的紧密连接起到作用。如果要我自己来说的话，这可是优惠价！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:390`

### English

Greetings, patron [Root.GetRulerName]! We have come up with a gorgeous new type of architecture, specifically tuned to improve crowds' understanding of one another - a §YMinistry of Culture§!. It functions thanks to a mix of using the populace's subconscious bias against each other and art, then smashing it all and presenting a new form of unity.\n\nWould you like to purchase this building? It will surely prove useful to binding your empire together. And it's a bargain, if I may say so myself!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:444`

## leviathans.322.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.322.a.tooltip&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:391`

### English

Gain access to a §S£building£ ['building:building_artist_patron']§!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:445`
