## leviathans.323.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.323.name&lang=en)

### 中文

乏善可陈的更新

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:394`

### English

Lackluster Update

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:448`

## leviathans.323.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.323.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们十分抱歉，来自[Root.GetRulerName]的赞助人：我们很不幸陷入了创作的瓶颈期。整个城邦目前还处于紊乱的状态，尚不能提供任何成果。一如既往，我们仍然十分感谢您的赞助。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:395`

### English

We apologize, patron [Root.GetRulerName]: we have sadly reached something of a creative roadblock. The enclave is in disarray and we have nothing to offer at this time. We thank you for your patronage, as always.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:449`

## leviathans.323.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.323.a&lang=en)

### 中文

希望状况能有所改善。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:396`

### English

We hope the situation improves.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:450`

## leviathans.323.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.323.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们要求退款！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:398`

### English

We demand a refund!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:452`

## leviathans.324.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.324.name&lang=en)

### 中文

破碎的艺术

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:400`

### English

Broken Artistry

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:454`

## leviathans.324.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.324.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

您好，来自[Root.GetRulerName]的赞助人。由于一个重大故障，我们最近不得不报废掉大多数的技术设备。您是否能用得上其中所产生的零件或原料呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:401`

### English

Greetings, patron [Root.GetRulerName]. We have recently been forced to scrap most of our technical equipment due to a major malfunction. Would the materials and scrap be of any use to you, by any chance?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:455`

## leviathans.324.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.324.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会回收原料的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:402`

### English

We will salvage the materials.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:456`

## leviathans.324.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.324.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会帮助你们修理的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:404`

### English

We will repair it for you.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:458`

## leviathans.325.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.325.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

赞助者，我们听到这个消息真是出离地抱歉！没有时事通讯，我们就无法向你传达我们最近的成果……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:406`

### English

We are exceptionally sorry to hear that, o patron! Without the newsletter we won't be able to keep you up-to-date on our latest accomplishments...

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:460`

## leviathans.345.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.345.name&lang=en)

### 中文

文化中心

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:407`

### English

The Ministry of Culture

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:462`

## leviathans.345.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.345.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

啊，§Y文化中心§!……它的任务就是挑战与常理背道而驰的观念，提升你帝国内艺术的凝聚力。让我来说，完全就是一个杰作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:408`

### English

Ah, the §YMinistry of Culture§!... It was designed to challenge divergent ideas and promote greater unity throughout your empire through the medium of art. A true masterpiece, if I may say so myself.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:463`

## leviathans.345.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.345.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

很好！这件杰作很快就会被送到您的贮藏设施中。只需要让你的工人们建到任何你自己的行星即可。\n我们由衷感谢您的赞助。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:409`

### English

Excellent! The piece will be sent to your storage facility immediately. Simply ask your builders to erect it on any planet you wish.\nWe humbly thank you for your patronage.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:464`

## leviathans.352.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.352.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们从艺术家联盟收到了一条信息。他们的一座空间站被摧毁了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:410`

### English

We have received a transmission from the Artisan Troupe. One of their stations has been destroyed!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:466`

## leviathans.353.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.353.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们收到了一条来自艺术家联盟首席的绝望的求救信息。他们的最后一个空间站与他们的文化都被毁灭殆尽。几乎没有幸存者留下来，艺术家联盟已经不复存在了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:411`

### English

We received a distress signal from the headmaster of the Artisan Troupe. Their last station and cultural haven has been destroyed. There were few survivors, and the troupe is essentially no more.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:467`

## leviathans.354.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.354.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在摧毁艺术家联盟的空间站后，我们发现残骸中有大量可供回收的资源。舱室中储存的捐赠物、礼品与作品现在漂浮在太空之中，可供我们随意回收取用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:412`

### English

After having destroyed the Artisan Troupe's station we found many resources to salvage from the wreckage. Donations, gifts and creations stacked in its halls now drift in space, ready for the taking.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:468`

## leviathans.354.desc.unknown

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.354.desc.unknown&lang=en)

### 中文

在摧毁了那个漂浮在[station_system.GetName]星系的空间站之后，我们在遗骸中发现了大量的珍贵资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:413`

### English

After destroying the station facility floating in the [station_system.GetName] system, we have found plenty of valuable resources amongst its remains.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:469`

## leviathans.500.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.500.name&lang=en)

### 中文

策展人

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:417`

### English

Curators

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:475`

## leviathans.500a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.500a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

也许你正在疑惑我身后壁画上图案的意义。这是个好问题，答案也会十分有趣。只需要1.5亿亿能量币你便可以得到这个问题的解答。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:418`

### English

Perhaps you are wondering about the significance of the shapes you see on the mural behind me. That is a good question with a very interesting answer! The fee for this information is 15 quadrillion energy credits.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:476`

## leviathans.500b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.500b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们是策展人，欢迎你们，请问你们需要什么服务？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:419`

### English

You have reached the Curators. How may we assist you?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:477`

## leviathans.500c.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.500c.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你们知不知道这次对话正在被七个来自不同领域的亚空间实体所密切地监听着？唉，想和他们交流是不可能的，因为你们的文明远不具备能与他们交流的通讯技术。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:420`

### English

Did you know that this conversation is being closely monitored by entities from seven different subspace realms? Alas, I fear that the technology to communicate with them is too far beyond the present capabilities of your civilization to do you any good.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:478`

## leviathans.500d.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.500d.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

不用担心，我们已经更新了档案系统，[Root.GetName]的信息已经被记入了档案以供后辈们查阅。即使在你的帝国经历不可避免的消亡后，你们仍将在档案中被铭记。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:421`

### English

Fear not. We have updated our archives with information about the [Root.GetName] for future generations. You will be remembered long after the inevitable collapse of your star nation.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:479`

## leviathans.500e.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.500e.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我想告诉你的东西有很多，但我不能白白免费地告诉你。这个空间站需要很多能源来维持运转…我们必须考虑收支的平衡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:422`

### English

There is so much I wish I could tell you, but I simply can't do it for free. This space station requires a lot of energy to operate... we must consider our overhead.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:480`

## leviathans.500f.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.500f.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

谨代表策展人协会向您问候，请问您有什么需要的吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:423`

### English

Greetings on behalf of the Curator order. Was there something you wanted?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:481`

## leviathans.500g.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.500g.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们一向很高兴能和你们的政府进行贸易，我们这里还有一些科技资源，你们有兴趣吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:424`

### English

As always, it is a pleasure doing business with your government. Can we interest you in some technology?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:482`

## leviathans.500h.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.500h.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

很高兴见到你。我很肯定今天我们可以达成一些互利互惠的协定。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:425`

### English

It is good to see you. I am sure we can come to some sort of mutually beneficial agreement before this day is over.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:483`

## leviathans.500i.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.500i.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

知识一旦落入了恶毒之徒的手中，就会变得相当危险。但我们能怎么办呢？毕竟我们需要能源才能运营下去……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:426`

### English

Knowledge in the wrong gripping appendages can be dangerous, but what can we do? We need energy to keep the lights on...

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:484`

## leviathans.500j.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.500j.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesName]，你们不知道的事情还有很多。求求你……让我们教授你们更多有关这个宇宙的知识吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:427`

### English

There is much that you do not yet know, [Root.GetSpeciesName]. Please... let us educate you about the universe.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:485`

## leviathans.500k.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.500k.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们持有整个星系的详细星图，这是从上百种不同的物种处收集来的信息的汇总。也许这能提起你们的兴趣？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:428`

### English

We possess detailed star charts of the entire galaxy, gathered by explorers from a hundred different races. Perhaps they would be of interest to you?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:486`

## leviathans.500l.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.500l.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

知识就是一切，它们必须被守护。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:429`

### English

Knowledge is everything. It must be preserved.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:487`

## leviathans.502.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.502.name&lang=en)

### 中文

与[curator_enclave_country.GetName]的第一次接触

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:430`

### English

First Contact with the [curator_enclave_country.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:489`

## leviathans.502a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.502a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

您好，星际旅行者，我们是策展人。\n\n我们遵从的古训自万古之前便由星系中的支配力量所制定。我们献身于保存世间所有的知识，来避免星系再次陷入荒蛮与死寂的黑暗时期。\n\n但我们失败了。\n\n我们只留下了很少的幸存者，但相比我们的前辈，我们完成自己神圣使命的决心丝毫没有动摇。我们有意与你分享一些我们的知识……当然，这不是无偿的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:431`

### English

Greetings, star travelers. We are the Curators.\n\nOur ancient order was established eons ago by the various galactic powers in ascendancy at the time. We were dedicated towards the preservation of all knowledge, in an effort to safeguard the galaxy from descending into yet another dark age of mass extinctions and barbarism.\n\nWe failed.\n\nFew of us remain today, but we are no less committed to our sacred mission than our predecessors were. We are willing to share some of our knowledge with you... for a price.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:490`

## leviathans.502b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.502b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

幸会，宇宙中的探险家。与你进行通话的是[curator_enclave_country.GetName]。也许你已经知晓我们古训中的信条。和其他城邦中的策展人同行们一样，只要你们能满足我们的要求，我们便愿意同你们交易。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:432`

### English

Well met, explorers. You are speaking to the [curator_enclave_country.GetName]. Perhaps you are already familiar with the tenets of our ancient order. Like our esteemed colleagues in the other Curator enclaves, we are willing to trade provided that you can meet our rates.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:491`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.502.a&lang=en)

### English

## leviathans.500.k.reply.reward_unavailable

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.500.k.reply.reward_unavailable&lang=en)

### 中文

我们很抱歉，但我们目前没有什么可以提供的。试着去收集、展示更多的$specimens$吧。也许我们到时候就能分享更多专业知识了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:450`

### English

We apologize, but we have nothing to offer you at this time. Try collecting and displaying more $specimens$. Perhaps we can then share our expertise.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:510`

## leviathans.500.k.reply.over

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.500.k.reply.over&lang=en)

### 中文

我们很抱歉，但我们已经没什么可以提供给你们了！你们的收藏真是蔚为壮观。很荣幸这个星系里能有你们这样的合作者。\n\n你们就是知识保护的无上财富。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:451`

### English

We apologize, but we have nothing left to offer you! Your collection is truly magnificent. We are honored to have you as a partner in our galaxy.\n\nYou are an asset to the preservation of knowledge.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:511`

## leviathans.504a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.504a.desc&lang=en)

### English

Our order thanks you, esteemed customer.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:515`

## leviathans.504b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.504b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[root.GetSpeciesAdj]，感谢你。愿明晰与好奇心常伴汝身。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:454`

### English

Thank you, [root.GetSpeciesAdj]. Clarity and curiosity be with you.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:516`

## leviathans.504c.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.504c.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

很高兴和你做生意！我们敏锐的见解和无价的记录能如何为你服务？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:455`

### English

A pleasure doing business with you. How else might our keen insights and inestimable records be of service to you?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:517`

## leviathans.506.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.506.d&lang=en)

### 中文

你们还有什么好东西？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:461`

### English

What else did you have to offer?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:524`

## leviathans.507.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.507.name&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y太空城邦：§!策展人科研协助

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:463`

### English

§YEnclave:§! The Curators Research Aid

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:527`

## leviathans.507.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.507.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当然。我们准备将我们庞大的数据库对你们进行部分开放，为期十标准年。这应该会对你们行星§H研究实验室§!的科学研究起到巨大的推动作用。\n\n自不用说，我们要为这样的服务收取一定的报酬。不过我认为你一定会感觉我们的价格十分实惠。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:464`

### English

Of course. We are prepared to offer you limited access to our extensive databanks for a period of ten standard years. This should provide a significant boost to the research efforts of your planetside §HResearch Labs§!.\n\nWe will require compensation for this service, naturally, but I think you'll find that our prices are very reasonable.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:528`

## leviathans.507.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.507.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们乐意购买这项服务。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:465`

### English

We would like to purchase this service.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:529`

## leviathans.507.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.507.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

明智的选择，[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]的后人们会称赞你的选择的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:466`

### English

A wise choice, that future generations of [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] will no doubt laud you for.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:530`

## leviathans.508.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.508.name&lang=en)

### 中文

未解之谜

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:467`

### English

Mysteries

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:532`

## leviathans.508.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.508.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们星系中的一些特定的恒星系里，潜藏着极其强大的存在。耗费了巨大的人力物力，策展人的调查人员煞费苦心地收集了他们的信息，有了这些信息，你在下次遇见它们时应该可以更容易地对付他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:468`

### English

There are exceedingly powerful entities lurking within certain star systems in our galaxy. At great cost in both lives and resources, Curator agents have painstakingly gathered knowledge that should make these beings easier to deal with the next time you encounter them.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:533`

给我们讲讲§Y$NAME_Dimensional_Horror$§!的事。

## leviathans.506.f

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.506.f&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的舰队面对它该如何作战？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:484`

### English

How would our current fleet fare against it in battle?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:550`

## leviathans.5106.f1.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.5106.f1.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

你的舰队差劲，太差劲。\n\n等等，难不成你想要去……我的天哪，就用你的这丁点舰队？那，那会变成一边倒的屠杀。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:485`

### English

Poorly. Very poorly.\n\nWait a second, you're not actually thinking of...? My goodness, with the ships you have? It... it would be a slaughter!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:551`

## leviathans.5106.f2.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.5106.f2.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

我强烈不建议你进行作战。你的攻击与其说是威胁它，更像是无谓的吵闹。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:486`

### English

I would strongly advise against it. Your attacks would likely be more of an annoyance than a threat.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:552`

## leviathans.5106.f3.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.5106.f3.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

嗯……没错……没错，我想事实上你还是有很大的机会的。如果你联合你的整个舰队的话……我提醒你这场胜利不会轻松，但是起码你有胜利的可能性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:487`

### English

Hmmm, yes... Yes, I suppose you might actually stand a chance. If you combined your entire fleet... It would not be an easy victory, mind you, but one that is at least within the realm of possibility.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:553`

## leviathans.5106.f4.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.5106.f4.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

你的舰队是一支强大的舰队，甚至比得上一些被我们策展人编年史记载着的最伟大的无敌舰队。胜利掌握在你的手中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:488`

### English

Yours is a mighty fleet, comparable to some of the greatest armadas recorded in the annals of the Curator Order. Victory is certainly within your reach.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:554`

## leviathans.500.f

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.500.f&lang=en)

### 中文

我们愿意建造一个§Y共享智库§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:489`

### English

We would like to establish a mutual §YThink Tank§!.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:556`

## leviathans.511.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.511.name&lang=en)

### 中文

智库

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:490`

### English

Think Tank

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:557`

## leviathans.511.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.511.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

是的……是的，这样一个设施将会有益于全星系的科学发展。你可以在一个与我们共享星系的恒星基地里修建这个智库。我们的策展人将与你的科学家共处。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:491`

### English

Yes... yes, such a facility would benefit the advancement of Galactic Science. You may build this Think Tank at your starbase in the system you share with us. Our Curators will come to mingle with your scientists.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:558`

## leviathans.511.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.511.a&lang=en)

### 中文

棒极了。你们还有什么好东西？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:492`

### English

Excellent. What else do you have to offer?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:559`

## leviathans.511.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.511.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

我们现在可以在§Y[curator_system.GetName]§!星系建造§H策展人智库§!这一恒星基地建筑了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:493`

### English

We can now build the §HCurator Think Tank§! Starbase Building in the §Y[curator_system.GetName]§! system.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:560`

## leviathans.511.a.tooltip_nomads

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.511.a.tooltip_nomads&lang=en)

### 中文

我们现在可以在§Y[curator_system.GetName]§!星系建造§H策展人智库§!驿站模块了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:494`

### English

We can now build the §HCurator Think Tank§! Waystation Module in the §Y[curator_system.GetName]§! system.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:561`

## leviathans.550.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.550.name&lang=en)

### 中文

虚空孳孽

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:495`

### English

Voidspawn

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:563`

## leviathans.550.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.550.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

如果我们没猜错的话，虚空孳孽来自很远的地方。我们认为它是某种巨大无比的星系际捕食者的后代，他们会在虚空中寻找猎物。尽管我们从未遭遇过活的个体，我们听说过虚空孳孽的成年体所过之处，皆是破坏与混乱的故事。据说星球会在它们的阴影下溃烂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:496`

### English

If our suspicions hold true, the Voidspawn is far away from home. We believe it is the progeny of an extragalactic predator of unthinkable proportions: one that stalks the void of space in search of its prey. Though we have never before encountered a live specimen, we have heard tales of the destruction and chaos grown Voidspawn leave in their wake. It is said that worlds fester under their shadow.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:564`

## leviathans.550.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.550.a&lang=en)

### 中文

$leviathans.510.a$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:497`

### English

$leviathans.510.a$

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:565`

## leviathans.550.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.550.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

据我们所知，母体会在恒星的轨道上产下星球大小的蛋，并花费数千年来孵化，有时甚至久到足以在蛋壳上发展出大气层与有机生命。\n\n它们以活体物质为食，而成年虚空孳孽据说会对所有活物造成一种奇怪的影响。幸运的是，这只幼体似乎被其父母抛弃了。\n\n或许我们最好该趁它尚未成熟之时将它消灭？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:498`

### English

From what we can tell, the mother lays its planet-sized eggs in orbit of a star, where they then incubate for thousands of years, sometimes even long enough to develop an atmosphere and organic life.\n\nThey feed off of living matter, and adult Voidspawn are said to exert a strange influence on all living things. Fortunately this spawn seems to have been abandoned by its parents.\n\nPerhaps it would be best to stifle the creature while it is still young and vulnerable?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:566`

## leviathans.550.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.550.b&lang=en)

### 中文

该怎么击败它？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:499`

### English

How is it defeated?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:567`

## leviathans.550.b.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.550.b.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

我们从未听说有谁曾击败过成年虚空孳孽，但这只才刚刚出生。虚空孳孽会喷出腐蚀性的黏液和尖刺，连最厚的装甲都可以烧穿，但涂上一层抗酸涂层可以减缓这一过程。我们会为你们提供帮助。虚空孳孽对我们所有人都是个威胁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:500`

### English

We have never heard of a grown Voidspawn being defeated in battle, but this one is newly hatched. The Voidspawn projects corrosive spittle and spikes that can burn through the thickest of armor, but applying a protective acid-proof coating may slow its progress. We will help you make it. The Voidspawn is a threat to us all.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:568`

## leviathans.555.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.555.name&lang=en)

### 中文

拾荒者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:501`

### English

Scavenger

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:570`

## leviathans.555.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.555.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

啊，废品机器人。拾荒者是一个失落时代的奇特造物。它无止尽地在残骸场中搜寻，将从无数残骸中搜获的枪炮，引擎，材料焊至自己的外壳上。其体量与火力数百年以来一直以这种方式提升，而且毫不减缓。\n\n如果有足够的时间与废品，没有人知道它到底能将自己造得多大。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:502`

### English

Ah, the scrap bot. The Scavenger is a curious construct from a lost age. Endlessly it searches through the debris fields of the galaxy, welding the guns, engines, and materials scavenged from countless derelicts onto its own hull. Its mass and firepower have been increasing in such a fashion for centuries, with no sign of relenting.\n\nNo-one knows if there is an upper limit to how massive it could become, given enough time and scrap to work with.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:571`

## leviathans.555.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.555.a&lang=en)

### 中文

当初是谁造了它？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:503`

### English

Who built it, initially?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:572`

## leviathans.555.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.555.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

不幸的是，关于拾荒者的起源并没有准确的数据。有些科学家认为它是一个失落帝国的废品回收机器人，但出了故障，其原本用途早已被硬件劣化埋没了。也有人认为它原本是一个微小的纳米机器人，在其程序驱动下，利用其周围环境来不断地使自己变大变强。还有人认为它当初是机器人舰长，是数千年前一场星际大战的唯一幸存者。因其文明突然且彻底的毁灭，导致其逻辑系统无法挽回地受损了。\n\n当然，这些都只是猜测。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:504`

### English

Unfortunately, no definitive data regarding the Scavenger's origin is known to exist. Some researchers believe it to be a malfunctioning scrap collection service machine from a lost empire, its purpose long since muddled by hardware degradation. Others believe it to have originated as a single microscopic nanobot, programmed to endlessly build itself larger and stronger by exploiting its environment. Still others reckon it may have started out as a robotic ship's captain; the sole survivor of an intergalactic battle waged millennia ago. Perhaps its reasoning systems were irrevocably damaged by the sudden destruction of its entire civilization.\n\nOf course, this is all just speculation.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:573`

## leviathans.555.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.555.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以与它沟通吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:505`

### English

Can we communicate with it?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:574`

## leviathans.555.b.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.555.b.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

多年来许多人都试图与拾荒机器人沟通，或者转而尝试更改其程序。不幸的是，这些远征队大多数都变成了拾荒者机器人身处的宇宙垃圾场的一部分。只有那些跑得比它还快的才活了下来。\n\n它身上焊接的重炮火力已经足以单枪匹马消灭整个帝国的舰队——而且这样的情况可能已经发生过不止一次了。当然，这种毁灭性的遭遇只会进一步增强拾荒者，为其提供更多可利用的舰船部件。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:506`

### English

Over the years there have been many attempts to communicate with it, or else to alter the programming of the Scavenger. Unfortunately, most of those expeditions ended up part of the cosmic scrap yards where the Scavenger Bot dwells. Only those who were able to outrun it survived.\n\nThe Scavenger has welded enough heavy artillery onto its patchwork frame to single-handedly destroy an entire nation's fleet - an event that may already have occurred on more than one occasion. Of course, any such destructive encounters would only have served to further strengthen the Scavenger, providing it with more ship components to repurpose.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:575`

## leviathans.555.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.555.c&lang=en)

### 中文

$leviathans.510.b$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:507`

### English

$leviathans.510.b$

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:576`

## leviathans.555.c.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.555.c.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

你可能已经推测到了：拾荒者并非按照任何明确的现成图纸而造。因此，它的某些部件并不太……合身，导致了一些不连续的结构弱点。当然，外行人一眼看不出这些弱点，但我们的先进传感器已经设法确定了它们的位置。\n\n让你的舰队集中火力攻击我们在拾荒者上标明的这些部位，应该可以大幅降低击溃它所需要倾斜的火力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:508`

### English

As you may have surmised: the Scavenger was not built according to any definitive, pre-existing schematics. As a result, some of its parts fit together... less than optimally, leading to a discrete number of structural weaknesses. Of course, to the uninitiated, most of these are obscured from view - but our advanced sensors have managed to isolate them.\n\nTell your fleets to focus fire on the indicated sections of the Scavenger's hull, and it should substantially reduce the amount of damage the construct is able to take before collapsing.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:577`

## leviathans.555.aa

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.555.aa&lang=en)

### 中文

再给我们讲讲它的起源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:509`

### English

Remind us about its origins.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:578`

## leviathans.555.bb

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.555.bb&lang=en)

### 中文

再给我们讲讲与它沟通的尝试。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:510`

### English

Remind us about attempts at communicating with it.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:579`

## leviathans.556.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.556.name&lang=en)

### 中文

缇扬奇族母

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:511`

### English

Tiyanki Matriarch

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:581`

## leviathans.556.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.556.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

真壮观，不是吗？一只消瘦的老族母，她的外皮在漫漫岁月中变白变硬，战列舰在她的触手中就像牡蛎壳一般脆弱。几十年前，我们的科学家听说有一位舰长，发誓要狩猎族母至星系尽头。不过，考虑到族母仍然存活，我们可以肯定他未能成功。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:512`

### English

Magnificent, is she not? A wizened old Matriarch, her hide bleached and hardened by the eons, her tentacles capable of snapping the hulls of battleships like oyster shells. Decades ago, our researchers heard rumors of a captain sworn to hunt the Matriarch to the ends of the galaxy. However, considering her continued existence, we cannot imagine he met with success.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:582`

## leviathans.556.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.556.a&lang=en)

### 中文

为什么族母比其它缇扬奇要大许多？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:513`

### English

Why is the Matriarch so much larger than other Tiyanki?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:583`

## leviathans.556.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.556.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

她惊人的巨大身躯实属异常。我们认为族母是来自另一星系的漂流者，那里的缇扬奇与我们星系中的缇扬奇进化产生了分歧，变得更大更强。\n\n当然，另一种可能性是族母只是得了一种巨大畸形症——不过这无法解释她的长寿。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:514`

### English

Her incredible size is an anomaly, to be sure. We believe the Matriarch is a wanderer from another galaxy in deep space, where a species of Tiyanki far greater in size and strength diverged evolutionarily from the Tiyanki found in our own.\n\nOf course, it is also possible the Matriarch is simply suffering from an advanced form of gigantism - though this would fail to explain her extended lifespan.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:584`

## leviathans.556.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.556.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你对那位狩猎族母的舰长有何了解？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:515`

### English

What do you know about the captain hunting the Matriarch?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:585`

## leviathans.556.b.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.556.b.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

很少。那位舰长很可能从另一个星系追着族母来到了这里。不过，为何有人会蠢到挑战可以单枪匹马消灭整支舰队的生物仍是个谜。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:516`

### English

Very little. It is quite possible the captain chased the Matriarch here from another galaxy. Though why one would be foolish enough to challenge a creature capable of destroying entire fleets with just one vessel is beyond us.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:586`

## leviathans.556.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.556.c&lang=en)

### 中文

$leviathans.510.b$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:517`

### English

$leviathans.510.b$

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:587`

## leviathans.556.c.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.556.c.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

族母非常强大，但并非坚不可摧。尽管她的装甲外壳可以抵御多种投射物，她仍有一个可观的弱点。\n\n在她的背上插着一枚巨型质子鱼雷，这是最近一场战斗中留下的仍未愈合的伤口。尽管那颗鱼雷似乎故障了，我们的扫描显示它仍可以引爆，只要用一道特定的能源脉冲诱发即可。\n\n我们现在将详情发送给你。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:518`

### English

The Matriarch is formidable, but not indestructible. While her armored hide protects her from a wide range of projectiles, she carries with her a remarkable weakness.\n\nThere is a massive proton torpedo lodged in her back: a wound from a recent battle that has healed poorly. While seemingly defective, our scans of the torpedo suggest it can still be detonated, if agitated with a specific energy pulse.\n\nWe will transmit the details to you now.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:588`

## leviathans.556.aa

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.556.aa&lang=en)

### 中文

再给我们讲讲为何族母比其它缇扬奇要大许多。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:519`

### English

Remind us why the Matriarch is larger than other Tiyanki.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:589`

## leviathans.556.bb

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.556.bb&lang=en)

### 中文

再给我们讲讲那位狩猎族母的舰长。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:520`

### English

Remind us about the captain hunting the Matriarch.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:590`

## leviathans.558.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.558.name&lang=en)

### 中文

毒圣

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:521`

### English

The Toxic God

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:592`

## leviathans.558.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.558.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

毒性神祗是一个捉摸不透的存在。几千年来它在星系内来回游荡，偶尔会选择某个星球喷洒毒液——我们只能猜测这是其生命循环的某个自然阶段。我们听说上次它被目击到是在§Y[toxic_god_system.GetName]§!星系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:522`

### English

The Toxic Entity is an elusive being. It has roamed the galaxy for millennia, occasionally choosing to spurt venom at worlds in what we can only imagine is a natural stage of its life cycle. We have heard that it has been tracked to the §Y[toxic_god_system.GetName]§! system.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:593`

## leviathans.558.desc.knights

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.558.desc.knights&lang=en)

### 中文

毒性神祗是一个捉摸不透的存在。几千年来它在星系内来回游荡，偶尔会选择某个星球喷洒毒液——我们只能猜测这是其生命循环的某个自然阶段。当然，你肯定早就知道了！\n\n我们很抱歉，但是我们没法帮你找到它现在所在的位置——我们的全体线人都有好一阵子没看到它了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:523`

### English

The Toxic Entity is an elusive being. It has roamed the galaxy for millennia, occasionally choosing to spurt venom at worlds in what we can only imagine is a natural stage of its life cycle. But you already knew this, of course!\n\nWe are sorry, but we cannot help you find its present whereabouts - it has not been spotted by any of our contacts for quite some time.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:594`

## leviathans.558.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.558.A&lang=en)

### 中文

$leviathans.510.a$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:524`

### English

$leviathans.510.a$

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:595`

## leviathans.558.AA

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.558.AA&lang=en)

### 中文

重新给我们讲讲你对它有什么了解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:525`

### English

Remind us what you know of it.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:596`

## leviathans.558.A.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.558.A.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

没有文献记载毒性神祗究竟从何而来。第一次目击是在数千年之前，自那以后每过几个世纪就有人目击过。某些人视其为神明，但具体如何得由你自己判断。我们能够告诉你的是，它是一个极度令人畏惧的有机生物，有着节肢动物的特征。有报告表明，它的体型相当巨大，上面甚至发展出了一整个生态系统。当然，获取其生物学样本的难度可以预想是有多么困难了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:526`

### English

It is not documented where the Toxic Entity came from. The first sighting was several thousand years ago, and since then it has been seen every few centuries. Some have called it a deity, but that is for you to decide. What we can tell you is that it is an extremely formidable organic being with arthropoid characteristics. Reports suggest that it is sufficiently large that an entire ecosystem has developed on it, though acquiring samples of its biology has proved understandably challenging.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:597`

## leviathans.558.B

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.558.B&lang=en)

### 中文

该怎么击败它？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:527`

### English

How is it defeated?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:598`

## leviathans.558.BB

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.558.BB&lang=en)

### 中文

重新给我们讲讲应该如何同它战斗。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:528`

### English

Remind us how we can fight it.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:599`

## leviathans.558.B.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.558.B.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

过去撞见它的人中有少数几个幸存者。根据报告，它带有腐蚀性的毒液能够烧蚀任何形态的装甲。护盾相对来说更加有用一丁点。此外，在它的背上生活的那些生物在剧毒实体受到威胁的时候，会像蜂群一样发动攻击。我们只能建议你带着压倒性的军力去接近它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:529`

### English

There have been few survivors to its past encounters. According to reports, its corrosive poisons are able to burn through all forms of armor. Shields are only slightly more useful. Furthermore, the creatures living on its back will supposedly act as an attack swarm when it is threatened. We can only suggest you approach it with overwhelming force.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:600`

## leviathans.559.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.559.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$NAME_Opus_Sentinel$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:530`

### English

$NAME_Opus_Sentinel$

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:602`

## leviathans.559.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.559.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

那个颇为神经质的自动化机械体来到这个星系的时间并不长，但不要被这种新奇所迷惑。$NAME_Opus_Sentinel$的创造者们在被一台专横的机器同化之前，就已经是他们所在领域中最杰出的工程师之一。我们不知道自那时起，那个机器意识遭遇了怎样严酷的命运，但从哨兵那痛苦的扭动来看：情况似乎相当不稳定。如果你决定满足自己的好奇心，务必小心行事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:531`

### English

That rather twitchy automaton is a relative newcomer to the galaxy, but don't be fooled by such novelty. The $NAME_Opus_Sentinel$'s creators were counted among the finest engineers in their sector even before they were assimilated by a tyrannical machine. We do not know what harsh fate has befallen the machine consciousness since then, but if the Sentinel's agonized writhing is anything to go by: the whole situation appears quite volatile. Tread carefully, should you decide to slake your curiosity.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:603`

## leviathans.559.desc.unplugged

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.559.desc.unplugged&lang=en)

### 中文

恕我冒昧，但我以为您会比我们更了解此事！与我们所研究的对象相比，那个颇为不安的自动化机械体是个新面孔，但作为对手同样令人畏惧。\n\n我们知道，即便在被同化之前，$NAME_Opus_Sentinel$背后的工程师们也被认为是该领域最优秀的人才。因此，那艘战舰不容小觑。我们不太清楚的是，这艘船是如何与[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]如此疏远的。从“哨兵”痛苦的扭动来看，我们推测情况仍然相当不稳定。您是否同意这一评估，[root.GetSpeciesName]？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:532`

### English

Forgive my impertinence, but I assumed you would know more about it than we do! That rather twitchy automaton is a newcomer compared to what we usually study here, but is no less fearsome an opponent.\n\nWe know that even before they were assimilated, the engineers behind the $NAME_Opus_Sentinel$ were counted among the finest in their sector. That warship is therefore not to be underestimated. What is less clear to us is how the vessel became so detached from the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]. We gather from the Sentinel's agonized writhing that the whole situation remains quite volatile. Would you agree with assessment, [root.GetSpeciesName]?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:604`

## leviathans.559.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.559.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你是说它在守护着什么吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:533`

### English

You say it is guarding something?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:605`

## leviathans.559.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.559.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

正是，尽管我们并不确切知道是什么。我们的一些联系人认为，哨兵正在保卫分裂的[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]所剩下的任何东西；另一些人则暗示，这颗星球本身蕴藏着某种宝藏。我们所知道的是，这艘战舰的建造比其[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj]同类坚固得多，甚至高出一个数量级。无论它在保护什么，$NAME_Opus_Sentinel$都在坚持，尽管有明显的痛苦迹象，甚至可能是极度的煎熬。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:534`

### English

Indeed, though we do not know precisely what. Some of our contacts believe the Sentinel is defending whatever remains of the splintered [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]; others suggest the planet itself holds some treasure. What we do know is that this warship was built more robustly than any of its [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj] peers by an order of magnitude. Whatever it is protecting out there, the $NAME_Opus_Sentinel$ perseveres despite clear signs of distress, or perhaps even agony.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:606`

## leviathans.559.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.559.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们宁愿不谈这件事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:535`

### English

We prefer not to talk about it.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:607`

## leviathans.559.b.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.559.b.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

完全可以理解。我们只是出于好奇。\n\n还有其他我可以帮您的吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:536`

### English

Quite understandable. We are merely curious.\n\nIs there anything else I can help you with?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:608`

## leviathans.559.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.559.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们能在哪里找到这个实体？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:537`

### English

Where can we find this entity?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:609`

## leviathans.559.c.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.559.c.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

鉴于您是我们较为信任的客户之一……我可以告知您，$NAME_Opus_Sentinel$最后一次被目击到是在守护§Y[origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core.GetName]星系§!\n\n考虑到我们对这艘饱经战火、问题重重的战舰仍然知之甚少，请您放心：我们将以极大的兴趣观察您与它的遭遇细节！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:538`

### English

Seeing as how you are one of our more trustworthy clients... I can tell you the $NAME_Opus_Sentinel$ was last seen guarding the §Y[origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core.GetName] system§!.\n\nGiven how much we still do not know about this ferociously troubled warship, rest assured: we shall observe the particulars of your encounter with keen interest!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:610`

## unplugged_curator_poi_opus_sentinel_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged_curator_poi_opus_sentinel_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们从策展人那里购买了这些坐标，他们报告称$NAME_Opus_Sentinel$最后一次出现在该区域。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:539`

### English

We bought these coordinates from the Curators, who report the $NAME_Opus_Sentinel$ was last seen in this area.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:611`

## leviathans.590.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.590.name&lang=en)

### 中文

雇佣科学官

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:540`

### English

Hire Scientist

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:614`

## leviathans.590.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.590.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们愿意提供我们之中最杰出的科学家来换取你们的资助。你们要知道这种提议可不是谁都能轻易获得的。\n\n当然，在做出人员安排之前，你必须准备好合适数量的……资金赞助。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:541`

### English

We are willing to pledge the services of one of our most brilliant scientists to your cause. This is not an offer we make to just anyone, you understand!\n\nA suitable... donation will of course be necessary, before traveling arrangements can be made.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:615`

## leviathans.590.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.590.a&lang=en)

### 中文

当然，我们会给你们资助的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:542`

### English

Of course. You shall have your donation.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:616`

## leviathans.590.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.590.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

很好，你们现在可以随意的使用我们这个时代最杰出的头脑了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:543`

### English

Excellent. You will now have unfettered access to one of the greatest minds of our time!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:617`

## leviathans.590.tt_scientist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.590.tt_scientist&lang=en)

### 中文

我们每次只能雇佣§Y一名§!§H策展人科学家§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:544`

### English

We may only employ §Yone§! §HCurator scientist§! at a time.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:618`

## leviathans.598.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.598.name&lang=en)

### 中文

共享原理

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:545`

### English

Shared Principles

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:620`

## leviathans.598.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.598.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们愿意向你们的集体灌输知识的基本原理。这个流程非常严格，而且只有在共同意识的情况下才能进行。\n\n即使对我们来说，这也不是一件容易的事情。成本并非微不足道。但在所有商品中，知识仍是最宝贵的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:546`

### English

We are willing to infuse your collective with the fundamental principles of knowledge. A serious procedure, and one only available to a shared consciousness.\n\nEven for us, it is no easy thing to achieve. The cost is not insignificant. But of all commodities, knowledge remains the most precious.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:621`

## leviathans.598.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.598.a&lang=en)

### 中文

$leviathans.590.a$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:547`

### English

$leviathans.590.a$

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:622`

## leviathans.598.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.598.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

这是一笔划算的投资。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:548`

### English

A sound investment.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:623`

## leviathans.598.tt_principles

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.598.tt_principles&lang=en)

### 中文

策展人的基本原理已经分享给我们了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:549`

### English

The fundamental principles of the Curators have already been shared with us.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:624`

## leviathans.599.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.599.name&lang=en)

### 中文

购买情报

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:550`

### English

Buy Intelligence

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:626`

## leviathans.599.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.599.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你们属于高信用的顾客，所以我们可以向你们透露某些我们的……低优先级顾客的消息。当然，该如何使用这些消息完全取决于你们自己。\n\n你想要了解什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:551`

### English

Since you are one of our trusted customers, we are able to divulge to you certain information on our... less favored clients. What you do with this information is entirely up to you, of course.\n\nWho do you want to know about?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:627`

## leviathans.599.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.599.a&lang=en)

### 中文

告诉我们[curator_customer_1.GetName]的情报吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:552`

### English

Tell us about the [curator_customer_1.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:628`

## leviathans.599.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.599.b&lang=en)

### 中文

[curator_customer_2.GetName]的情报你们都知道些什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:553`

### English

What do you know of the [curator_customer_2.GetName]?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:629`

## leviathans.599.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.599.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们希望了解关于[curator_customer_3.GetName]的情报。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:554`

### English

We wish to know about the [curator_customer_3.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:630`

## leviathans.599.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.599.d&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对[curator_customer_4.GetName]的情报感兴趣。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:555`

### English

The [curator_customer_4.GetName] is of interest to us.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:631`

## leviathans.599.yes.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.599.yes.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

很好。情报很快就会通过星系最严密的星际数据链传输给你们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:556`

### English

Excellent. The information will be transferred to you immediately via the most secure interstellar datalink in the galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:632`

## leviathans.600.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.600.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$clues$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:557`

### English

$clues$

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:634`

## leviathans.600.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.600.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$lgates$？是的，正如其它传送门一样，它们的历史比最古老的$gov_stagnant_ascendancy$还要久，但你们肯定已经知道这一点了。\n\n它们为何不同？它们通往哪里？我们决定不要对这些谜团太过深究——“一朝被蛇咬，零点一七年怕井绳”这道理你懂的——但我们愿意分享一些可能与其有关的近期发现。\n\n当然，不是免费的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:558`

### English

The $lgates$? Yes, like the other Gateways they predate even the oldest $gov_stagnant_ascendancy$, but we are sure you have figured that out already.\n\nWhy are they different? Where do they lead? We have opted not to delve too deeply into those particular mysteries - "thrice bitten, zero-point-seventeen shy" you know how it is - but we are willing to share some recent revelations that just might be related.\n\nFor a price.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:635`

## leviathans.600.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.600.a&lang=en)

### 中文

请说吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:559`

### English

Please do.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:636`

## leviathans.596.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.596.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

看来我们真是高估你们了，你们的人根本不具备文明处事的能力。真是奇怪……我们通常在刚见面的时候就可以准确识别出某个物种是否具备好斗倾向。为什么对你们看走眼了呢。\n\n我们已经剥夺了你们对我们数据库的访问权限。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:560`

### English

It would appear that we grossly overestimated your capacity to act as a civilized nation. Strange... We are usually able to identify the violent ones at an early stage.\n\nWe have severed your access to our databanks, of course.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:638`

## leviathans.597a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.597a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你们这群笨蛋！袭击我们就等于放弃了知识，你们的后辈们将活在低科技水平的蛮荒之中！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:561`

### English

You fool! By attacking us, you are condemning future generations to lives of low-tech misery!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:639`

## leviathans.597b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.597b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你们受本能驱使而袭击我们，而现在你们要为此付出代价。我们将永远不再与[Root.GetName]分享我们的知识。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:562`

### English

Your base instincts compelled you to attack us, and now you must pay the price. We will never share our knowledge with the [Root.GetName] again.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:640`

## leviathans.597c.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.597c.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]，我们慷慨相助，你们却不识好人心。一群野蛮的渣滓！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:563`

### English

We had so much to offer you [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural], and yet you would throw it all away. Neo-barbarian scum!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:641`

## leviathans.597d.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.597d.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你们已经成为整个策展人组织的敌人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:564`

### English

You have been declared an enemy of the entire Curator Order.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:642`

## leviathans.510.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.510.name&lang=en)

### 中文

以太巨龙

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:565`

### English

Ether Drake

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:644`

## leviathans.510.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.510.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

以太龙族这个物种并不适合在我们的宇宙中生存，最后的这只龙虽然很强，但也不是无敌的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:566`

### English

The Ether Drake species is ill-suited to life in our universe. The last dragon is strong, but not invincible.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:645`

## leviathans.510.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.510.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这东西到底是什么玩意？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:567`

### English

What is it, really?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:646`

## leviathans.510.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.510.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

传说中，以太巨龙的出现先于任何一个宇宙文明。它们曾经数量众多，但随着时代更迭他们的族群也在逐渐衰落。\n\n可以说，此时此地并不适合它们的生存。它们似乎不大适应宇宙的物理法则，就好像它们是从其他的现实中进化而来一般。而这，正是事实所在。我们认为它们是来自一个我们宇宙开始之前就已终结的宇宙，也就是某次\"大坍缩\"中唯一的生还者。\n\n离这里不远的那条龙恐怕是这个星系，乃至这个宇宙中最后的以太巨龙了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:568`

### English

By all accounts, the Ether Drakes predate all galactic civilization. There once were multitudes, but their race has been wasting away for eons.\n\nThey are ill-suited to life, here and now. Something about the physical laws of the universe does not agree with them, like they evolved in another reality. They did, in fact. We believe that they are but the last dregs of a universe that ended before ours began, the sole survivors of some "big crunch."\n\nThe dragon not far from here is the last of its kind in the galaxy, perhaps the universe.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:647`

## leviathans.510.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.510.b&lang=en)

### 中文

告诉我们如何才能击败它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:569`

### English

Tell us how to defeat it.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:648`

## leviathans.510.b.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.510.b.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

龙身上的鳞片对敲击节律有反应，更精确地说，特定的频率对它的皮下组织有着放松的作用，而那里控制着它们的用以移动与防护冲击的鳞片。这个条件反射曾是以太巨龙之间社交行为的关键环节，现在则成为了它的死穴。\n\n调整舰船火力平台的攻击节奏到一个特定的频率，可以有效地增加以太巨龙所受到的伤害。它并不是主动想生存在这个世界之中——一劳永逸地结束它的痛苦吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:570`

### English

Its scales respond to percussive rhythms. To be more precise, certain frequencies have a relaxing effect on the sub-dermal musculature controlling the positioning and shock absorption of their scale plates. A reflex that once played a key role in Ether Drake social interactions, it is now a clear and obvious weakness.\n\nAdjusting the firing pattern of your vessels according to a particular rhythm will greatly increase the damage you can inflict on the last dragon. It was never meant for our age - put it out of its misery.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:649`

## leviathans.515.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.515.name&lang=en)

### 中文

神秘堡垒

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:571`

### English

Enigmatic Fortress

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:651`

## leviathans.515.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.515.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这是一种已经没人会再建造的，异想天开，体型巨大且能自我维持的建筑。也许不建是明智的选择。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:572`

### English

It is the kind of absurd, massive and self-perpetuating construction that you don't see built anymore. Maybe that is for the best.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:652`

## leviathans.515.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.515.a&lang=en)

### 中文

任何造物都能被毁坏。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:573`

### English

Any structure can be destroyed.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:653`

## leviathans.515.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.515.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

这座堡垒曾经被击毁过，但它总能恢复原状。一些古老的帝国曾经因未能揭开它更深层次的秘密，抑或是未能永久地毁灭它而陷入癫狂。这座堡垒对于维持自身是如此尽心尽力，令人不得不怀疑它是否还有其他的正经用途。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:574`

### English

The Fortress has been defeated before, but it always recovers. Some of the ancient empires of this galaxy were driven to madness by their vain efforts to unlock its deeper secrets, or else to permanently destroy it. It appears so focused on maintaining itself that we doubt it has any other serious purpose.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:654`

## leviathans.515.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.515.b&lang=en)

### 中文

堡垒可以进入吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:575`

### English

Can the fortress be entered?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:655`

## leviathans.515.b.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.515.b.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

可以，而且已经有人进入过了，尽管那项尝试的壮举已经在时间的流逝中几乎被忘却。这座堡垒在关闭状态时仅能维持部分供能，不过它会尝试从附近摄取能量以迅速重启系统。请务必小心。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:576`

### English

It can and it has been, although that attempt is now nearly forgotten. The fortress maintains only partial power while in shutdown, however it will attempt to draw power from nearby sources in order to jump-start its systems again. Proceed with caution.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:656`

## leviathans.515.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.515.c&lang=en)

### 中文

是谁建造了这个堡垒？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:577`

### English

Who built the fortress?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:657`

## leviathans.515.c.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.515.c.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

不管是谁，他们一定对于谜题与解密十分着迷。人们过去只从堡垒里面回收了一些形状奇怪的金属物件：这些古怪装置的零件，似乎设计上可以通过重新排列组成更加自然的形态。当时的回收人员并未能解开谜题，但所幸从堡垒中死里逃生。绝大多数人就没能活着出来了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:578`

### English

Whoever they were, they must have been fond of puzzles and enigmas. The only items to have been recovered from the fortress are strange metal shapes: components of perplexing contraptions which seem designed to be rearranged into more satisfying forms. Those who brought them back did so because they failed, and they barely escaped with their lives. Many others didn't.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:658`

## leviathans.515.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.515.d&lang=en)

### 中文

它是如何驱动自己的？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:579`

### English

How is the fortress powered?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:659`

## leviathans.515.d.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.515.d.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

动力系统一定是在建造最初舱体与安装防御系统之间的某个时候建造而成的。我们尚不清楚它的恢复方式，但我们发现堡垒中保存着巨量的暗物质。了解那些物质与以及其与黑洞的关系，是弄清堡垒长久以来运作方式的关键。\n\n没人曾经成功找到它的动力源，但这动力的来源似乎十分不稳定。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:580`

### English

The power system must have been built at a time between initial construction of the hull, and installation of its defense systems. The system's recovery method is unknown to us, but we have discovered that the fortress contains large quantities of dark matter. That substance and its relationship to black holes are key to understanding how the fortress has lasted so long.\n\nNo one has ever found the source of its power, but it is likely to be extremely volatile.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:660`

### English

Remind us about entering it.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:661`

### English

Remind us who built it.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:662`
