## leviathans.520.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.520.name&lang=en)

### 中文

噬星者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:584`

### English

Stellar Devourer

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:665`

## leviathans.520.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.520.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

就像你的帝国一样，噬星者是星系狩猎场中的一种新的寄生虫。它们从恐惧和核聚变中诞生，平时在星际间游荡。它们会潜进恒星内部，从内部汲取养分。另外，它们也很不想被打扰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:585`

### English

The Stellar Devourer is a new parasite on the galactic hunting ground, not unlike your empire in many ways. It was born out of fear and nuclear fusion. It roams the galaxy, submerging itself into stars, feasting on them from within. And it certainly does not like being disturbed.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:666`

## leviathans.520.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.520.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

这种恒星吞噬者是一种很罕见的生物，我们并不知道这种怪物的来源。我们手上现有的情报非常有限，只有极少数的情报提供源获得了与它们接触的第一手资料。\n\n但是我们现有的资料都指向了一个事实：这是一种极其可怕的生物，它只会带来毁灭。\n\n噬星者能够以聚变为食。我们得出了一个§Y非常§!有说服力的推断：它是暗物质和恒星物质在外部维度层面上融合的产物。它们潜进恒星内部，吞噬其中的物质来为其内部聚变反应堆提供燃料。它所到的恒星系，无一逃得过灭亡的命运。在它的盛宴之后，行星们要么像冰一样粉碎，要么暴露在它胃中的第二太阳之下被烧尽。\n\n它贪得无厌，永远不会停下吞噬的脚步。当你注视着它的时候，你会从中看到为了生存而透露出的无以言表的狂怒。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:586`

### English

The star-feaster is a rare breed. A new type of beast of unknown origin. The accounts we have collected are few and sparse with intel. Few sources even have firsthand experience with the thing.\n\nAll our data points to the same fact, however: it is a terror that brings nothing but ruin.\n\nThe Stellar Devourer is capable of feeding off of fusion itself. Our §Yvery§! educated guess is that its biology is the result of some outer-dimensional fusing of dark matter and solar matter. It dives into stars, feasting on their innards in order to fuel its own, internal fusion reactor. It brings death to all systems it reaches. Either they shatter like ice after its banquet, or they burn from being exposed to a second sun from within the being's belly.\n\nIt wants to feed, and it will stop at nothing to get its share. When you gaze at it, what you see is a survivor's raw fury.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:667`

## leviathans.520.b.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.520.b.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

我们目前唯一确定的事实是，噬星者最重要的部分是它的集成核心。它极度需要维持其内部的反应炉运转，避免让寒冷的虚空接触到它内部使其自行崩塌。没有那些恒星物质，它就没办法存活。\n\n这是一个你们可以利用的缺陷。你们可以将武器按照我们提供的标准来进行校准，这样你们就能穿破保护其消化系统的外层组织。打伤它，你就能看到它的内部物质喷射出来，扩散到整个星系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:587`

### English

The only thing we know for sure is that the collected core is the most important aspect of the Stellar Devourer. The creature desperately needs to keep its furnace burning, lest the cold void of space reaches its vital organs deeper within and makes it collapse in on itself. Without that stellar matter, it is nothing.\n\nYou can use this to your advantage. Calibrate your weapons to our specifications and you can then home in on the very membranes that hold the Devourer's digestive system together. Wound it and you will see its innards gush out across the solar system.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:668`

## leviathans.525.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.525.name&lang=en)

### 中文

位面之魇

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:588`

### English

Dimensional Horror

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:670`

## leviathans.525.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.525.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的设备发现有一物体冲入我们的世界，同时还检测到了巨大的能量涌动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:589`

### English

Our instruments monitored the immense energy surge when this entity pierced its way into our realm of existence.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:671`

## leviathans.525.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.525.a&lang=en)

### 中文

$leviathans.510.a$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:590`

### English

$leviathans.510.a$

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:672`

## leviathans.525.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.525.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

这种完全是由怨恨组成的生物，诞生于另外一个位面。那里非常像是许多太空文明所信仰的宗教里描述的地狱。目前我们看到的还只是它的冰山一角——这只怪物的全貌应该比现在的样子还大上几倍。\n\n所幸，它没能完全转移至我们的宇宙。如若不然，它可能早已经将所有的生命赶尽杀绝了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:591`

### English

It is a creature of pure malice, born in a dimension that bears more than a striking resemblance to the realm of Hell as described by many galactic religions. What we are seeing is but a small part of a greater whole - the creature in its entirety is multitudes larger.\n\nIt is very fortunate that it was unable to complete its transition into our universe. Had it been successful, the creature would likely have consumed all life in the galaxy by now.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:673`

## leviathans.525.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.525.b&lang=en)

### 中文

$leviathans.510.b$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:592`

### English

$leviathans.510.b$

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:674`

## leviathans.525.b.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.525.b.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

位面之魇最大的弱点是它通往我们宇宙的位面传送门——没有传送门，它就不能在我们的宇宙中存活下去。我们已经确认了传送门能量矩阵中几个薄弱的位置，用传统的武器就能对其造成伤害。现在开始传送目标坐标点。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:593`

### English

The dimensional portal that it spawned from is its greatest weakness - without it, it cannot exist in our universe. We have identified several weak points in the portal's energy matrix that could be exploited by conventional weapons. Transmitting targeting coordinates now.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:675`

## leviathans.530.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.530.name&lang=en)

### 中文

小行星蜂群

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:594`

### English

Asteroid Hiver

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:677`

## leviathans.530.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.530.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些令人感到烦躁的虫子曾经比现在更多，它们的足迹遍布整个星系。那个时候，当你航行通过任意一个小行星带时，你都会发现有小虫子围绕着你的船只。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:595`

### English

These annoying pests were once far more prevalent in our galaxy. There was a time when you couldn't cruise through a random asteroid belt without the little beasts swarming around your ship!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:678`

## leviathans.530.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.530.a&lang=en)

### 中文

$leviathans.510.a$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:596`

### English

$leviathans.510.a$

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:679`

## leviathans.530.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.530.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

它们是一种具有侵略性的硅基生物。尽管它们一度对太空文明构成巨大威胁，但过去的50万年来，它们遭到大量的捕杀，剩下的都隐遁了起来。\n\n尽管流传着许多谣言，目前没有任何证据表明它们具备任何智慧生物的特征。它们喜欢在小行星里筑巢，并且攻击所有它们认为是有威胁的东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:597`

### English

They are an aggressively territorial silicon-based life form. Although they once posed a significant threat to spacefaring civilizations, their numbers have been severely culled over the last 500,000 years and the surviving members of their species are fairly reclusive.\n\nDespite rumors to the contrary, there is no evidence that they possess anything that could be described as sapient intelligence. They tend to nest inside asteroids, and they will attack anything which is perceived as a threat.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:680`

## leviathans.530.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.530.b&lang=en)

### 中文

$leviathans.510.b$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:598`

### English

$leviathans.510.b$

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:681`

## leviathans.530.b.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.530.b.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

这种生物喜欢用一种未知的物质包裹在他们的蜂巢小行星外面来防御舰载武器的攻击。但是这种物质并不能完全地覆盖在小行星的表面上。\n\n有了这些现在正传输给你的瞄准数据，你的飞船可以集中火力攻击未覆盖装甲的区域，大大提升攻击效果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:599`

### English

The creatures have a tendency to coat their asteroid nests with some kind of unknown substance that acts as a potent armor against ship-based weapons. This substance is not perfectly applied across the entire surface of the asteroid, however.\n\nWith the targeting data we are transmitting to you now, your ships will be able to focus their fire on unprotected surface areas, greatly increasing the damage they inflict.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:682`

## leviathans.535.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.535.name&lang=en)

### 中文

无限神机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:600`

### English

Infinity Machine

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:684`

## leviathans.535.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.535.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

嗯对，这种色彩斑斓的机器出现于远古时代。我们对它的研究可以追溯到我们城邦建立之初。在我们之前已经有许多文明也对它进行了研究。\n\n这种机器已经不只是上古时代的产物了。根据我们现有的资料显示，它一直都存在着。但是，它所做的唯一的事情就是绕着黑洞转。我们对无限神机很感兴趣，但是我们明白这种东西对于你们来说还是太难理解了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:601`

### English

Ah yes, the chromatic being from long ago. We have studied it for as long as our order has existed. As have several civilizations before us.\n\nThis machine is beyond ancient. For all we know, it has always been. And yet, all it does is orbit that black hole. It's fascinating to us, but we understand if it is too much for you to grasp.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:685`

## leviathans.535.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.535.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

你口中笼统称为无限神机的存在，是无法进行回收的，更无法获得它的任何信息。整个星系中没有任何一个骇客或者工程师能透过它闪亮的外壳看到里面的任何东西——我们很清楚它里面藏着许多有价值的信息。我们也束手无策。我们既不知道它的用途，也不知道它接下去的任务是什么。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:602`

### English

The Infinity Machine, as you so bluntly call it, is impervious to extraction. No information comes out from it. No hacker or engineer in the entire galaxy has been able to see through its shiny exterior to gain a glimpse of the knowledge it so obviously withholds from us. We are at a loss. We neither know what it is for, nor what it aims to do once it's done.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:686`

## leviathans.535.b.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.535.b.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

有一种办法大概可以摧毁它。我们虽然无法从它身上获得任何信息，但它的外壳还是可以破坏的。采矿者曾经无数次尝试过从它上面获取资源，但回来时全都狼狈不堪。不过他们发现了一些有趣的情况：无限神机并不像看起来那样坚不可摧。\n\n它是由一种能够调节其自身属性的材料构成。密度、弹性、温度，只有你想不到，没有它变不了的！它能够实时分析伤害，并及时改变外壳特性进行补偿。这样一来，外界的伤害就很难摧毁它了。你要做的，就是改变攻击的频率，调整导弹的热力输出，不管用什么攻击方式，想方设法地进行变化。这样你们才能克服无限神机外壳的适应性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:603`

### English

There is perhaps one thing you could do to destroy it. We have been unable to retrieve information from it, but its hull is breakable. Miners have tried to gather resources from it at numerous times, only to come back with their ships barely holding together. But they did find something interesting: The Infinity Machine is not as rigid as it seems.\n\nIt is made of a material capable of changing its characteristics. Density, flexibility, heat, you name it! It can analyze damage in real time and change its shell to compensate, thus making itself harder to take down. Change your frequencies, adjust the thermal output of your missiles, whatever you people lumber around with, and keep changing them. That way you can get past its adaptive capabilities.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:687`

## leviathans.540.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.540.name&lang=en)

### 中文

自动无畏战舰

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:604`

### English

Automated Dreadnought

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:689`

## leviathans.540.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.540.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这是一个悲伤的故事。自动无畏战舰的主人已经过世百万年之久了，但是这些巨大的舰船还继续在它们帝国的废墟周围警戒着。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:605`

### English

A sad story. Its masters have been dead for millions of years, but this massive warship continues to stand vigil over the broken rubble of their empire.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:690`

## leviathans.540.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.540.a&lang=en)

### 中文

$leviathans.510.a$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:606`

### English

$leviathans.510.a$

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:691`

## leviathans.540.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.540.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

七百万年前，曾经有个伟大的星际帝国在我们的星系中一个不起眼的地方兴盛强大，这是那曾经无比强大的帝国的旗舰之一。历史往往都是相似的，这个帝国在一系列与邻国的惨烈战争中骤然消亡。\n\n你遇到的无畏战舰是他们最后的遗物。尽管船员们都已经去世，电脑系统还在尽职地巡逻。在这段漫长的时光中，无数人都曾想让这艘战舰安息——但没有一次成功。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:607`

### English

It was one of several flagships belonging to an unusually powerful interstellar empire that flourished in a small region of our galaxy some seven million years ago. As is so often the case, that empire was brought to a sudden and violent end at the hands of its neighbors in a series of very destructive wars.\n\nThe dreadnought you have encountered is the last of its kind, and it continues to dutifully patrol its assigned system despite the death of its crew. Many attempts have been made throughout the ages to put the old warship to its final rest - none successful.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:692`

## leviathans.540.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.540.b&lang=en)

### 中文

$leviathans.510.b$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:608`

### English

$leviathans.510.b$

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:693`

## leviathans.540.b.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.540.b.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

设计之初，这艘无畏战舰应该载着大量的船员进行操作，但是现在它仅依靠着自动化系统来工作。这使得它在其中央计算机的中继节点受到干扰时将变得非常脆弱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:609`

### English

The dreadnought was designed to operate with a large crew, but it now relies exclusively on automated systems to function. This makes the ship especially vulnerable to attacks that disrupt the relay nodes used by its central computer.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:694`

## leviathans.545.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.545.name&lang=en)

### 中文

幽魂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:610`

### English

Wraith

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:696`

## leviathans.545.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.545.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

神出鬼没的幽魂是一种令人烦躁的生物，如果它们能被称为生物的话。我们把它们当做是一种罕见但是极具破坏力的自然灾害。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:611`

### English

Spectral Wraiths are disturbing creatures, if they are creatures at all. We consider them a rare and extraordinarily efficient natural disaster.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:697`

## leviathans.545.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.545.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你对这种灾害有什么了解？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:612`

### English

What do you know about these disasters?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:698`

## leviathans.545.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.545.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

它们似乎像是光影形成的幻象，但等你凑近去看，马上就会发现它们与幻象相去甚远。\n\n打倒它们的关键就在于它们的形态，虽然这听起来有些矛盾。可见光谱影响着它们的力量，当它们接近相似频率的光源时，力量就会减弱。时刻注意幽魂的颜色变化。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:613`

### English

They appear to be illusions of light, but when you get close, it quickly grows apparent that they are far from illusory.\n\nParadoxically, in that appearance lies the key to their defeat. Their relationship with the visible spectrum of light is their strength, but it diminishes when they approach a light source of a similar frequency. Pay close attention to the color of the Wraith.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:699`

## leviathans.545.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.545.b&lang=en)

### 中文

红色幽魂是什么样的呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:614`

### English

What about red Wraiths?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:700`

## leviathans.545.b.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.545.b.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

红色幽魂的频率是450THz。它们的光与§YM级恒星§!是一致的。它们免疫传统武器攻击的能力在有红矮星的恒星系里将会被削弱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:615`

### English

Red Wraiths have a frequency of 450THz. Their light is synchronized with that of a §YClass M Star§!. Their ability to avoid conventional weaponry is weakened in systems with red dwarfs.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:701`

## leviathans.545.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.545.c&lang=en)

### 中文

黄色幽魂是什么样的呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:616`

### English

What about yellow Wraiths?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:702`

## leviathans.545.c.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.545.c.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

黄色的幽魂的频率是520THz。它们的光与§YG级恒星§!是一致的。它们免疫传统武器攻击的能力在有黄矮星的恒星系里将会被削弱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:617`

### English

Yellow Wraiths have a frequency of 520THz. Their light is synchronized with that of a §YClass G Star§!. Their ability to avoid conventional weaponry is weakened in systems with yellow dwarfs.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:703`

## leviathans.545.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.545.d&lang=en)

### 中文

蓝色幽魂是什么样的呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:618`

### English

What about blue Wraiths?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:704`

## leviathans.545.d.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.545.d.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

蓝色幽魂的频率是650THz。它们的光与§YB级恒星§!以及§YA级恒星§!是一致的。它们免疫传统武器攻击的能力在有蓝巨星的恒星系里将会被削弱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:619`

### English

Blue Wraiths have a frequency of 650THz. Their light synchronizes with both §YClass A§! and §YClass B Stars§!. Their ability to avoid conventional weaponry is weakened in systems with blue giants.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:705`

## leviathans.593

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.593&lang=en)

### 中文

策展人空间站被摧毁

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:620`

### English

Curator Station Destroyed

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:707`

## leviathans.593.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.593.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[curator_system.GetName]星系摧毁了策展人的空间站之后，我们能干的军队在策展人曾经占据的区域搜寻任何有价值的东西。他们成功恢复了一下策展人数据库的内存碎片，这将能极大地促进我们的研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:621`

### English

After destroying the ancient Curator station in the [curator_system.GetName] system, our capable forces combed the area it once occupied for anything valuable. They managed to recover several memory fragments from the Curator databanks that should be a great boon to our research.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:708`

## leviathans.594.ab.desc.start

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.594.ab.desc.start&lang=en)

### 中文

深空通讯阵列获取了从[curator_system.GetName]星系的策展人城邦发来的简短的自动悲报。通讯在几分钟之后停止，长距探测器确认了远古空间站被摧毁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:624`

### English

Deep space communication arrays picked up a brief automated distress call from the Curator station in the [curator_system.GetName] system. The transmission ceased after just a few minutes, and long-range recon probes have confirmed that the ancient space station has been destroyed.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:711`

## leviathans.594.ab.desc.known

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.594.ab.desc.known&lang=en)

### 中文

残余离子轨迹表明，这次袭击是§Y[curator_destroyer.GetName]§!所为。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:625`

### English

Residual ion trails suggest that forces from the §Y[curator_destroyer.GetName]§! were behind this attack.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:712`

## leviathans.594.ab.desc.unknown

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.594.ab.desc.unknown&lang=en)

### 中文

发动此次袭击的势力尚不明确。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:626`

### English

The forces responsible for this attack remain unidentified.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:713`
