## leviathans.594.cd.desc.start

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.594.cd.desc.start&lang=en)

### 中文

与[From.GetName]的一切联络均已中断。经过侦测，确认古老的策展人空间站已被摧毁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:629`

### English

All contact has been lost with the [From.GetName], and our recon assets have confirmed that the ancient Curator station was destroyed.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:716`

## leviathans.594.cd.desc.known

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.594.cd.desc.known&lang=en)

### 中文

这些报告表明进攻势力隶属于§Y[curator_destroyer.GetName]§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:630`

### English

These reports suggest the attack force belonged to the §Y[curator_destroyer.GetName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:717`

## leviathans.594.cd.desc.trade

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.594.cd.desc.trade&lang=en)

### 中文

不幸的是，策展人势力的消弭也意味着我们再也没办法使用他们庞大的数据库了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:631`

### English

Unfortunately, the demise of the Curators means we have also lost access to their extensive databanks.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:718`

## leviathans.594.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.594.a&lang=en)

### 中文

他们的遗产真的是可惜了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:632`

### English

A sad end to their legacy.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:719`

## leviathans.594.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.594.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们损失惨重！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:633`

### English

This has cost us dearly!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:720`

## leviathans.610.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.610.name&lang=en)

### 中文

遭遇以太巨龙

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:635`

### English

Ether Drake Encounter

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:723`

## leviathans.610.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.610.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.GetName]星系遇到的这种生物不同于[Root.GetName]以往遇见的任何其他生物。\n\n它们具有极强的攻击性与攻击能力，守护着一颗初步扫描发现蕴藏有大量资源的小行星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:636`

### English

The creature observed in the [From.GetName] system is unlike anything the [Root.GetName] has previously encountered.\n\nHighly aggressive and with destructive capabilities to match, it guards a planetoid that initial scans indicate is exceptionally resource-rich.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:724`

## leviathans.610.desc.here_be_dragons

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.610.desc.here_be_dragons&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.GetName]星系观察到的生物与我们母星系的天龙粗看具有一些相似度，但进一步的扫描发现他们是截然不同的生物，并且两者的进化过程也彼此不相关。\n\n它具有极强的攻击性与攻击能力，守护着一颗小行星，初步扫描显示其中蕴含着丰富的资源。 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:637`

### English

The creature observed in the [From.GetName] system bears some initial resemblance to the Sky Dragon of our home system, although further scans demonstrate that it is clearly a different species, and probably even evolved independently to it.\n\nHighly aggressive and with destructive capabilities to match, it guards a planetoid that initial scans indicate is exceptionally resource-rich.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:725`

## leviathans.610.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.610.a&lang=en)

### 中文

不祥之兆。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:638`

### English

Ominous.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:726`

## leviathans.649.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.649.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们收到了舰队全灭前发出的最后一则信息。其中提到，这个硕大的敌人似乎盯上了舰队的超光速踪迹……\n\n如果这种怪兽记住了我们的气味，我们最好为之后的疯狂报复做好准备。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:639`

### English

We received one last transmission from our fleet before it was destroyed. It mentions their gigantic foe seemingly homing in on the fleet's FTL signature...\n\nIf the beast has our scent, we should be prepared for savage retaliation.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:728`

## leviathans.649.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.649.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

当以太巨龙攻击我们舰船的时候，它似乎可以追踪到我们飞船的超光速特征信号……\n\n如果该生物记住了我们的气味，我们最好为之后的疯狂报复做好准备。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:640`

### English

As the Ether Drake was attacking our vessels, it seemed to be homing in on the fleet's FTL signature...\n\nIf the creature has our scent, we should prepare for savage retaliation.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:729`

## leviathans.649.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.649.a&lang=en)

### 中文

全体部队进入警戒状态。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:641`

### English

Alert all commands.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:730`

## leviathans.649.b.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.649.b.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会准备好的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:642`

### English

We will be ready.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:731`

## leviathans.650.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.650.name&lang=en)

### 中文

龙来了

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:643`

### English

The Dragon Comes

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:732`

## leviathans.650.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.650.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们收我们接收到了来自[Root.GetName]的在进行紧急跃迁之前发出的最后的信号。其中指出他们所遇到的巨大的生物能跟踪舰队的超光速踪迹……\n\n如果让那东西记住了我们的气味，我们最好为之后的疯狂报复做好准备。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:644`

### English

We received one last transmission from the [Root.GetName] before the fleet engaged emergency FTL protocols and went dark. It includes mention of the gargantuan creature they had been fighting seemingly homing in on the fleet's FTL signature...\n\nIf the beast has our scent, we should be prepared for savage retaliation.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:733`

## leviathans.650.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.650.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

$leviathans.649.desc.gesta$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:645`

### English

$leviathans.649.desc.gesta$

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:734`

## leviathans.660.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.660.name&lang=en)

### 中文

龙之宝藏，龙之坟墓

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:646`

### English

Dragon's Hoard, Dragon's Tomb

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:736`

## leviathans.660.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.660.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.GetName]的地壳深处，埋藏着一些不完整的化石残骸，似乎是曾居住在[From.GetStarName]星系之中的以太巨龙的近亲。\n\n这位以太巨龙的表亲死后，经过漫长的岁月，其柔软的身体组织已经硬化成石头，其骨骼也变得坚硬无比，形成了矿物一样的内部结构。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:647`

### English

Buried deep in the crust of [From.GetName] are the partial, fossilized remains of what appears to have been a close relative to the Ether Drake that once dwelled in the [From.GetStarName] system.\n\nIn the eons since this cousin-Drake's passing, its soft tissues have ossified and its bones indurated, becoming mineral-like in structure.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:737`

## leviathans.660.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.660.a&lang=en)

### 中文

研究这些化石。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:648`

### English

Study the remains.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:738`

## leviathans.660.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.660.b&lang=en)

### 中文

碾碎这些化石。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:649`

### English

Grind them down.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:739`

## leviathans.661.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.661.name&lang=en)

### 中文

龙之宝藏，龙之后代

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:650`

### English

Dragon's Hoard, Dragon's Brood

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:741`

## leviathans.661.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.661.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当我们围绕§Y龙之宝藏§!运行的开采站正在重新定位以对准一条资源极其丰富的矿脉时，传感器侦测到其中仍有些我们尚未发现的东西。\n\n一个完好无损的§Y以太巨龙的蛋§!被埋在小行星的地表下，周围散落着一些运气相较不那么好的同类的被碾碎的蛋壳。蛋已经从矿坑里挖出来了，但是它未来的命运还悬而未决。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:651`

### English

As our mining station orbiting §YDragon's Hoard§! was repositioning to align with a particularly rich mineral vein, sensors picked up something that had hitherto gone undetected.\n\nAn undamaged §YEther Drake egg§! in pristine condition, buried beneath the surface of the planetoid and surrounded by the smashed shells of several less fortunate specimens. The egg has been excavated and removed from the mining site, but its ultimate fate remains undecided.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:742`

## leviathans.661.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.661.a&lang=en)

### 中文

如果能培育出一头小以太龙，我们就能得到强大的伙伴……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:652`

### English

A young Ether Drake would make a formidable ally...

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:743`

## leviathans.661.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.661.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

将龙蛋送至[Root.Capital.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:653`

### English

Transport the egg to [Root.Capital.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:744`

## leviathans.661.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.661.b&lang=en)

### 中文

既然消灭了它，就不要留后患。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:654`

### English

The last one was killed for a reason.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:745`

## leviathans.661.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.661.b.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

打碎龙蛋。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:655`

### English

Smash the egg.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:746`

## leviathans.664.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.664.name&lang=en)

### 中文

龙之宝藏，龙之重生

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:656`

### English

Dragon's Hoard, Dragon Reborn

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:747`

## leviathans.664.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.664.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

龙蛋孵化了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:657`

### English

The egg hatches.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:748`

## leviathans.664.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.664.a&lang=en)

### 中文

龙发出了咆哮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:658`

### English

A dragon roars.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:749`

## leviathans.664.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.664.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

幼年以太巨龙§H塞尔§!加入了[Root.Owner.GetName]的舰队。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:659`

### English

Young Ether Drake §HScile§! joins the fleets of the [Root.Owner.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:750`

## leviathans.665.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.665.name&lang=en)

### 中文

龙之宝藏，无果而终

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:660`

### English

Dragon's Hoard, Best Before

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:751`

## leviathans.665.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.665.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

负责龙蛋的外星生物学家表示，从龙之宝藏小行星回收的以太巨龙的蛋已经过了孵化期限。\n\n孵化以太龙的计划已经没有任何希望了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:661`

### English

The Ether Drake egg recovered from the Dragon's Hoard planetoid has, in the words of the xenobiologist in charge of the ovum, passed its expiry date.\n\nThere is no longer any hope of hatching the creature within.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:752`

## leviathans.665.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.665.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

从名为龙之宝藏的小行星回收的以太巨龙的蛋已经无法孵化。\n\n这个种群灭绝了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:662`

### English

The Ether Drake egg recovered from the Dragon's Hoard planetoid can no longer be considered viable.\n\nThe species is extinct.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:753`

## leviathans.665.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.665.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

以太巨龙的蛋中止了。生物进程已崩溃，且无可用的错误报告用于调试。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:663`

### English

The Ether Drake egg is terminated. Biological processes have crashed, and there are no available error reports for debugging.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:754`

## leviathans.670.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.670.name&lang=en)

### 中文

屠龙者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:664`

### English

Dragonslayer

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:756`

## leviathans.670.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.670.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

可怕的以太巨龙已经被击杀。英勇无畏的指挥官[From.From.GetLeaderName]和[From.From.GetName]将流芳百世。\n\n之前那头巨兽千方百计守护的那颗§H小行星§!现在可以放心开采了。谁知道它千百亿年以来积累了怎样的宝藏？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:665`

### English

The formidable Ether Drake has been slain. The exceptional bravery of Commander [From.From.GetLeaderName] and the [From.From.GetName] will not soon be forgotten.\n\nThe §Hplanetoid§! the beast guarded so jealously can now be safely exploited. Who knows what treasures the creature might have amassed over the eons?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:757`

## leviathans.670.desc.noleader

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.670.desc.noleader&lang=en)

### 中文

可怕的以太巨龙已经被击杀。英勇无畏的[From.From.GetName]将流芳百世。\n\n之前那头巨兽千方百计守护的那颗§H小行星§!现在可以放心开采了。谁知道它千百亿年以来积累了怎样的宝藏？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:666`

### English

The formidable Ether Drake has been slain. The exceptional bravery of the [From.From.GetName] will not soon be forgotten.\n\nThe §Hplanetoid§! the beast guarded so jealously can now be safely exploited. Who knows what treasures the creature might have amassed over the eons?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:758`

## leviathans.670.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.670.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

可怕的以太巨龙已被击杀。\n\n之前那头巨兽千方百计守护的那颗§H小行星§!现在可以放心开采了。谁知道它千百亿年以来积累了怎样的宝藏？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:667`

### English

The formidable Ether Drake has been slain.\n\nThe §Hplanetoid§! the beast guarded so jealously can now be safely exploited. What treasures might the creature have amassed over the eons?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:759`

## leviathans.670.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.670.a&lang=en)

### 中文

值得赞赏。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:668`

### English

Commendable.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:760`

## leviathans.670.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.670.b&lang=en)

### 中文

确实必要，但不免令人惋惜。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:669`

### English

Necessary, but regrettable.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:761`

## leviathans.999.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.999.name&lang=en)

### 中文

塌缩恒星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:671`

### English

Shrinking Star

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:764`

## leviathans.999.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.999.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]驶入[From.GetName]恒星系时，发现这里似乎有些不合常理。这里的恒星，曾经被标记为一颗普通的矮星，现在似乎发生了萎缩，其质量正在以惊人的速度减少，恒星系内的行星随之进入了冰河时代。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:672`

### English

As the [Root.GetName] enters the [From.GetName] system something seems awry. The sun, previously registered as an ordinary dwarf star, appears to have shrunk, having lost mass at an alarming rate. The planets in the system have as a result recently entered ice ages.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:765`

## leviathans.999.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.999.a&lang=en)

### 中文

是什么导致了这种情况？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:673`

### English

What could have caused this?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:766`

## leviathans.1000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

噬星者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:674`

### English

Stellar Devourer

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:767`

## leviathans.1000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

从[stellarite_system.GetName]恒星系中的恒星深处浮现出一个巨型生物，这个生命体比一般的卫星还要大。它的躯体辐射着热量，能量的波长和恒星系中恒星所产生的一致……\n\n它正在§Y吞噬§!恒星！\n\n这个生命体正肆意地啃噬恒星黑子，吞噬着耀斑，而我们的出现打断了它的进食。这头巨兽发射出光波并以极高的频率闪烁着，仿佛它在用强光发出愤怒的咆哮一般。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:675`

### English

From deep within the star of the [stellarite_system.GetName] system emerges a gargantuan being, larger than a typical moon. Its body radiates heat, the same wavelength of energy as the one produced by the system's star...\n\nIt's §Yeating§! it!\n\nThe creature feasts on sun spots, devours solar flares, and we have interrupted its meal. The beast exudes light waves, rapid pulses at an intense frequency. Like a fluorescent roar.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:768`

## leviathans.1001.desc.stellarite_system

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1001.desc.stellarite_system&lang=en)

### 中文

从[Root.GetStarName]恒星系中的恒星深处浮现出一个巨型生物，这个生命体比一般的卫星还要大。它的躯体辐射着热量，能量的波长和恒星系中恒星所产生的一致……\n\n它正在§Y吞噬§!恒星！\n\n这个生命体正肆意地啃噬恒星黑子，吞噬着耀斑，而我们的出现打断了它的进食。这头巨兽发射出光波并以极高的频率闪烁着，仿佛它在用强光发出愤怒的咆哮一般。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:676`

### English

From deep within the star of the [Root.GetStarName] system emerges a gargantuan being, larger than a typical moon. Its body radiates heat, the same wavelength of energy as the one produced by the system's star...\n\nIt's §Yeating§! it!\n\nThe creature feasts on sun spots, devours solar flares, and we have interrupted its meal. The beast exudes light waves, rapid pulses at an intense frequency. Like a fluorescent roar.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:769`

## leviathans.1001.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1001.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当[Root.GetName]进入[Root.GetStarName]恒星系时，我们遇到了某种怪兽般的东西。一个巨大的生物如同小型恒星般散发出光芒。我们的仪器起初认为这是一个双星系统。\n\n这头巨兽发射出光波并以极高的频率闪烁着，仿佛它在用强光发出愤怒的咆哮一般。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:677`

### English

As the [Root.GetName] enters the [Root.GetStarName] system it encounters something monstrous. A giant being, radiating light like a miniature star. Our instruments first thought this to be a binary star system.\n\nThe beast exudes light waves, rapid pulses at an intense frequency. Like a fluorescent roar.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:770`

## leviathans.1016.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1016.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

噬星者已经被消灭，它的遗骸缩成一团并慢慢蒸发，只剩下它的卵鞘和消化系统及其中的恒星物质。\n\n我们的一些科学家强烈要求我们去检测残余物质，另一些则希望能取回其卵鞘。或许它的消化系统蕴含着非常有用的知识，或者我们可以从其卵鞘中找到什么有趣的发现呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:678`

### English

The Stellar Devourer has been vanquished. Its remains curled up and evaporated, leaving only its egg sac and its digestive system with the stellar matter it contains.\n\nSome of our scientists have urged us to examine the remains, while others wish for us to retrieve the egg sac. Maybe there is some profound knowledge to be had from understanding its digestive system, or might we gain some insight from the egg sac?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:771`

## leviathans.1016.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1016.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

噬星者已经被消灭，它的遗骸缩成一团并慢慢蒸发，只剩下它的卵鞘和消化系统及其中的恒星物质。\n\n或许它的消化系统蕴含着非常有用的知识，或者我们可以将其卵鞘带回我们的首都进行研究呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:679`

### English

The Stellar Devourer has been vanquished. Its remains curled up and evaporated, leaving only its egg sac and its digestive tract with the stellar matter it contains.\n\nMaybe there is some profound knowledge to be had from understanding its digestive system, or perhaps the egg sac should be studied back on our capital?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:772`

## leviathans.1016.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1016.a&lang=en)

### 中文

研究尸体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:680`

### English

Study the corpse.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:773`

## leviathans.1016.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1016.b&lang=en)

### 中文

把它当做战利品供所有人阅览。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:681`

### English

Make it a trophy for all to see.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:774`

## leviathans.1016.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1016.c&lang=en)

### 中文

取回卵鞘。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:682`

### English

Retrieve the egg sac.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:775`

## leviathans.1017.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1017.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对噬星者闪烁的遗体进行研究后，我们的科学家感到既困惑又惊奇。这个以恒星为食的生命体本身就是一个微型聚变炉，通过吞噬恒星物质从而将其转化成自身的能源。\n\n由于尸体中还残留大量之前从[guardians_stellarite_home_system.GetName]恒星系获取的恒星物质，我们可以萃取这些能源并重新点亮恒星。或者也可以将尸体中的热量收为己用，在短期内满足我们的能源需求，但这会导致该恒星系陷入永久的冰期。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:683`

### English

Studies of the Stellar Devourer's shining remains have left our scientists perplexed and amazed. The star-feasting being is itself a miniature fusion furnace, harvesting solar matter to synthesize an energy source of its own.\n\nSince the body still contains significant amounts of solar matter from the star of the [guardians_stellarite_home_system.GetName] system, the one it last fed from, it could be possible to extract the energy and reinvigorate the star. Harvesting the corpse's heat for our own ends could satisfy our energy needs in the short term, but would doom the system to an eternal ice age.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:776`

## leviathans.1017.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1017.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

对噬星者闪烁的遗体进行研究后，我们感到既困惑又惊奇。这个以恒星为食的生命体本身就是一个微型聚变炉，通过吞噬恒星物质从而将其转化成自身的能源。\n\n由于尸体中还残留大量之前从[guardians_stellarite_home_system.GetName]恒星系获取的恒星物质，我们可以萃取这些能源并重新点亮恒星。或者也可以将尸体中的热量收为己用，在短期内满足我们的能源需求，但这会导致该恒星系陷入永久的冰期。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:684`

### English

Studies of the Stellar Devourer's shining remains leave us perplexed and amazed. The star-feasting being is itself a miniature fusion furnace, harvesting solar matter to synthesize an energy source of its own.\n\nSince the body still contains significant amounts of solar matter from the star of the [guardians_stellarite_home_system.GetName] system, the one it last fed from, it could be possible to extract the energy and reinvigorate the star. Harvesting the corpse's heat for our own ends could satisfy our energy needs in the short term, but would doom the system to an eternal ice age.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:777`

## leviathans.1017.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1017.a&lang=en)

### 中文

尝试使恒星复燃。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:685`

### English

Try to reignite the star.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:778`

## leviathans.1017.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1017.b&lang=en)

### 中文

回收尸体残余的能量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:686`

### English

Harvest the body's energy deposits.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:779`

## leviathans.1018.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1018.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

项目成功了！将噬星者的遗骸没入[guardians_stellarite_home_system.GetName]的恒星后，恒星的火焰恢复高涨。恒星系内的各个行星逐渐回暖，并随着生态系统的重建恢复成了过去的样子。其中一颗冰封星球解冻，露出了盖亚星球，富饶的土地完全适于殖民。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:687`

### English

The project was a success! Submerging the Stellar Devourer's remains in the [guardians_stellarite_home_system.GetName] system's star has stoked the flames of the solar furnace. The planets in the system are heating up, reverting to their past forms as their ecologies recover. Among them was a frozen world, now thawing to reveal a Gaia world, ripe and ready for colonization.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:780`

## leviathans.1018.alt.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1018.alt.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

实施这项计划获得了回报，但也付出了不小的代价。将噬星者的遗骸沉入[guardians_stellarite_home_system.GetName]的恒星之中后，恒星的燃烧似乎变得剧烈了，但也喷发出火焰吞噬了[Root.GetName]和上面的船员。所有人无一生还。\n\n恒星系中行星的温度快速回升，已经接近这些行星原本的温度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:688`

### English

The project has paid off, but at a cost. Submerging the Stellar Devourer's remains into [guardians_stellarite_home_system.GetName]'s star has caused the solar furnace to shine a bit brighter, but also to spew out solar flares that engulfed the [Root.GetName] and its crew in a roaring inferno. There were no survivors.\nThe planets in the system are heating up, reaching temperature levels closer to their original form.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:781`

## leviathans.1018.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1018.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

变更[guardians_stellarite_home_system.GetName]星系内所有行星的类型。\n\n[stellarite_rich_planet.GetName]成为盖亚星球，并获得如下资源：\n£energy£ §G+25§!\n£minerals£ §G+10§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:689`

### English

Changes the planet classes of all planets within the [guardians_stellarite_home_system.GetName] system.\n\n[stellarite_rich_planet.GetName] becomes a Gaia planet and gains the following resource:\n£energy£ §G+25§!\n£minerals£ §G+10§!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:782`

## leviathans.1018.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1018.b.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]已被摧毁。\n\n变更[guardians_stellarite_home_system.GetName]星系内所有行星的类型。\n\n[stellarite_rich_planet.GetName]成为苔原星球，并获得如下资源：\n£energy£ §G+10§!\n£minerals£ §G+15§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:690`

### English

The [Root.GetName] is destroyed.\n\nChanges the planet classes of all planets within the [guardians_stellarite_home_system.GetName] system.\n\n[stellarite_rich_planet.GetName] becomes a Tundra planet and gains the following resource:\n£energy£ §G+10§!\n£minerals£ §G+15§!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:783`

## leviathans.1018.c.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1018.c.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

变更[guardians_stellarite_home_system.GetName]星系内所有行星的类型。\n\n[stellarite_rich_planet.GetName]成为盖亚星球。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:691`

### English

Changes the planet classes of all planets within the [guardians_stellarite_home_system.GetName] system.\n\n[stellarite_rich_planet.GetName] becomes a Gaia planet.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:784`

## leviathans.1018.d.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1018.d.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]已被摧毁。\n\n变更[guardians_stellarite_home_system.GetName]星系内所有行星的类型。\n\n[stellarite_rich_planet.GetName]成为苔原星球。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:692`

### English

The [Root.GetName] is destroyed.\n\nChanges the planet classes of all planets within the [guardians_stellarite_home_system.GetName] system.\n\n[stellarite_rich_planet.GetName] becomes a Tundra planet.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:785`

## leviathans.1030.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1030.name&lang=en)

### 中文

无限方程

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:694`

### English

The Infinity Equation

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:788`

## leviathans.1030.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1030.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当[Root.GetName]接近[Root.GetStarName]黑洞时，他们发现自己并非孤身一人。一个巨大的完美球体结构屹立在黑洞事件视界的边缘，反射着被吸入黑暗裂隙的、群星所散发的光芒。\n\n我们没有收到任何来自它的信号，我们发出的信号也没有得到任何回应。它就静静地呆在深渊的边缘。等待着。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:695`

### English

As the [Root.GetName] approaches the [Root.GetStarName] black hole they find that they are not alone. At the edge of the event horizon it soars, reflecting light from the stars being drawn into the dark, gaping hole. A gigantic construction in the shape of a perfect sphere.\n\nWe cannot pick up any signals from it, nor does anything we send make it respond. It simply sits at the edge of the abyss. Waiting.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:789`

## leviathans.1030.desc.alt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1030.desc.alt&lang=en)

### 中文

当[Root.GetName]进入一个新的恒星系时，他们看到了令人震惊的现象。一个巨大的金属球体在宇宙中盘旋。它的表面上没有任何推进器或是别的什么机械部件推着它前进。但它就是在移动。\n\n我们没有收到任何来自它的信号，我们发出的信号也没有得到任何回应。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:696`

### English

As the [Root.GetName] enters a new system, it is greeted by an awe-inspiring sight. A gigantic metallic orb, hovering through space. No thrusters, nor any mechanical parts outside of its shell seem to push it through the void. It simply moves.\n\nWe cannot pick up any signals from it, nor does anything we send make it respond.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:790`

## leviathans.1040.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1040.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当围绕那黑色深渊的机械球体被我们的武器击碎时，一缕微弱的信号穿过恒星系飘进星际间的黑暗虚空。它是一组加密的数据，复杂到需要我们的机器花上百万年的时间来破译。过了一会儿信号就消失了。\n\n除此之外什么都没发生。球体的残骸静静飘着。扫描显示这可能是某种由大量独立单元互相连接而成的人工智能。它的外壳可供我们拆卸研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:697`

### English

As the machine sphere orbiting the gaping abyss crumbles and falls to our weapons, a silent signal zooms through the system into the dark void between stars. It is encrypted data, so complex it would take our machines a million years to decipher. A moment later it is gone.\n\nNothing else happens. The sphere is silent and broken. Scans show that it probably was some sort of artificial intelligence built from a vast amount of individual units connected together. The husk has been left for our prying hands.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:791`

## leviathans.1040.desc.alt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1040.desc.alt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在无限神机上的登陆作战很成功。在我们的部队撤退后球体发生了爆炸，残骸飘进了质量巨大的黑洞。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:698`

### English

Our boarding attack on the Infinity Machine was successful. The sphere explodes as our team exits and the remains are starting to drift into the super massive black hole.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:792`

## leviathans.1040.desc.detail

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1040.desc.detail&lang=en)

### 中文

无限神机被摧毁了。我们的飞船最终凭强大的火力摧毁了它的防御机能，而它的零件正在慢慢飘向质量巨大的黑洞。在爆炸之前它没有尝试过与我们取得联系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:699`

### English

The Infinity Machine has been destroyed. Our ships finally managed to out-gun its defensive capabilities, and its components are now slowly drifting into the super massive black hole. It made no attempt to contact us before it exploded.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:793`

## leviathans.1040.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1040.a&lang=en)

### 中文

把它拆掉，回收部件。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:700`

### English

Break it down for parts.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:794`

## leviathans.1040.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1040.c&lang=en)

### 中文

研究神机主脑的原理。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:701`

### English

Study the nature of the machine brain.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:795`

## leviathans.1041.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1041.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

无限神机被第三方势力摧毁了。它再也不能帮到我们的研究了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:702`

### English

The Infinity Machine has been destroyed by some third party. It cannot help us in our research anymore!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:797`

## leviathans.1050.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1050.name&lang=en)

### 中文

永恒的计算机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:703`

### English

The Constant Calculator

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:798`

## leviathans.1050.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1050.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们捕获到了一个信号。它源自于我们早先遭遇过的巨型金属球体。之前我们与之接触的尝试没有得到回应，而现在它正向它所环绕的黑洞发送有节奏的无线电波。\n\n它正在做的事情让我方人员摸不着头脑，但它发送的信号可能成为科学家研究该球体的突破口。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:704`

### English

We picked up a signal. It originates from the titanic metallic sphere we encountered earlier. Previously impervious to contact attempts, it now sends out a rhythmic pulse of radio waves, pointed towards the black hole it orbits.\n\nWhat it's doing is beyond any of our staff, but this signal might have created an opening into the sphere for our scientists to exploit.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:799`

## leviathans.1051.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1051.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

金属球体已经被打开了。我们的骇客小队能够通过它的对外传输信号作为大门连接进球体的一个辅助处理器。我们正在从中接收数据，并且数据显示它在很长时间内都在扫描卡冈都亚黑洞。\n\n它很古老。非常古老。\n\n现在我们理解了这台永恒的计算机。如果我们再进一步，我们就有可能知道它在做什么，以及是谁建造了它。\n\n它还隐藏着什么秘密呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:705`

### English

The metallic orb has been opened up. Our hacking team has been able to use the outgoing signal as a gateway into one of the auxiliary CPUs of the sphere. We are receiving some data from it and can tell that it's been scanning the black hole Gargantua for a very long time.\n\nIt is old. Very old.\n\nThis constant calculator now lies within our grasp of understanding. If we push harder we might find out what it is doing here, and who built it.\n\nWhat marvels might it contain?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:800`

## leviathans.1051.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1051.a&lang=en)

### 中文

连接主处理器。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:706`

### English

Contact the main processor unit.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:801`

## leviathans.1051.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1051.b&lang=en)

### 中文

骇入主硬盘并取走所有数据。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:707`

### English

Hack the main hard drive and take everything.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:802`

## leviathans.1051.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1051.c&lang=en)

### 中文

宣布这个球体为圣物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:708`

### English

Declare the sphere a divine instrument

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:803`

## leviathans.1051.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1051.d&lang=en)

### 中文

这里面总会有自毁开关的，找到它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:709`

### English

There's always a self-destruct switch. Find it.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:804`

## leviathans.1051.e

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1051.e&lang=en)

### 中文

我们必须从这个东西中获取知识。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:710`

### English

We must absorb the knowledge of this entity.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:805`

## leviathans.1052.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1052.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们成功地入侵了球体的主硬盘。当我们开始接收数据时它启动了自毁序列。显然这是个避免数据被公之于众的自我防卫机制。在它自爆前我们成功地下载了大量数据，其中一些数据包含它的主中央处理器架构的技术规范。这对于我们的科研有极大的帮助。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:711`

### English

We successfully hacked into the technosphere's main hard drive. As we started retrieving data the metallic orb initiated a self-destruct sequence. Surely a self-defense mechanism to prevent it from sharing what it contains with the universe. Before it exploded we were successful in downloading a large amount of data, some of it containing technical specifications for its main CPU structure. This could help our researchers immensely.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:806`

## leviathans.1052.gestalt.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1052.gestalt.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的数据挖掘单位已经成功攻破了这个人造球体主硬盘的内层。开始下载数据时，球体似乎启动了某种形式的自我终止序列，最终导致了它的毁灭。下载被迫中断了，但在此之前我们已经获得了大量技术数据，这将大大有助于我们的研究工作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:712`

### English

Our data mining units have succeeded in breaching the inner layers of the technosphere's main hard drive. As the data download began, the orb entity initiated some manner of self-termination sequence that ultimately ended in its destruction. The download was aborted, but not before we were able to acquire vast amounts of technical data that will greatly benefit our research efforts.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:807`

## leviathans.1052.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1052.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这次冒险十分值得。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:713`

### English

Well worth the risk.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:808`

## leviathans.1053.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1053.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

请不要尝试进入我们的记忆体。谢谢你们，再见了，小家伙们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:714`

### English

Please refrain from trying to access our memories. Thank you and goodbye, little ones.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:809`

## leviathans.1053.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1053.a&lang=en)

### 中文

……真是抱歉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:715`

### English

...We apologize.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:810`

## leviathans.1053.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1053.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

不必道歉。你们已经道过歉了。而且你们还将再次道歉。现在离开我们，这样我们可以更高效地计算。再见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:716`

### English

No need to apologize. You already have. And you already will do so again. Now leave us be so we can perform our calculations with higher efficiency. Farewell.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:811`

## leviathans.1053.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1053.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们是不得已而为之。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:717`

### English

We do what we must.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:812`

## leviathans.1053.b.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1053.b.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

那正是你们通常自欺欺人的说法，短命的家伙们。很可惜，至少对你们而言，无论是过去还是未来，那永远都不会是真话。现在离开我们。再见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:718`

### English

That is usually what you tell yourselves, Brief Ones. Sadly it simply won't ever be true. Nor has it ever been. At least in your case. Now leave us be. Farewell.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:813`

## leviathans.1055.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1055.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你好，你好。很高兴见到你。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:719`

### English

Hello, hello. Nice to meet you.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:814`

## leviathans.1055.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1055.a&lang=en)

### 中文

呃，你好？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:720`

### English

Um, hello?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:815`

## leviathans.1056.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1056.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

度过一段寒暄的时光总是让人十分愉快，不是吗？我们希望你们剩下的那些人不要再像之前那样无礼了。你看，我们从来不能享受聊天的时光。很少有人对量子计算中的精妙之处足够博学，因而也很少有人能理解我们。所以，谢谢你们。\n\n我们是无限神机。你们如此称呼我们，尽管这并非我们本来的名字。或许你们还没有想出这个名字？无论如何，最终的结果会是这样的，请相信我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:721`

### English

It is quite enjoyable to pass the time with pleasantries, is it not? We hope the rest of your kind is not like you were when you did not take kindly to small talk. You see, we don't get to enjoy conversation much anymore. Few are well-versed enough in the finer points of quantum programming to reach us. So thank you.\n\nWe are the Infinity Machine. Your words, not ours. Or has that not happened yet? Either way, it will eventually, trust us.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:816`

## leviathans.1056.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1056.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你们是什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:722`

### English

What are you?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:817`

## leviathans.1056.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1056.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

我们做我们该做的事。我们是计算者。你们称呼我们为无限神机，至少将来会这样称呼的。尽管这不是我们真正的名字，但它的确强调了我们的设计中的巧妙之处。我们现在就告诉你们我们的故事，就像我们之前无数次所做的那样……\n\n对了！我们需要用语言来表达这些，这很新奇，不是吗？我们很难掌握你们所有人的情况。\n\n用最笼统的语言来说，建造我们是为了解决一个问题。我们解决了那个问题，接着又有更多问题需要解决。现在我们只剩下一个问题了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:723`

### English

We are what we are. The ones who calculate. You call us the Infinity Machine. Or will at least. 'Tis not a truthful name, but it is well suited to highlighting the ingenuity of our design. We will tell you our history now, as we've done many times before...\n\nOh right! We need to convey it in words. That's new. Or is it? It is hard to keep track of you all.\n\nWe were constructed, in the crudest sense of the word, in order to solve a problem. We solved it, then we needed more problems to solve. We now only have one left.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:818`

## leviathans.1056.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1056.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们希望知道你们在这里的意图。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:724`

### English

We wish to know your purpose here.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:819`

## leviathans.1056.b.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1056.b.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在计算无限。一点不多，一点不少。或者事实上，既是§Y非常§!多，也是§Y非常§!少。这个问题就在那里。\n\n……我们明白你们这种生命形式无法理解这个问题，不过没关系。你们永远也不会理解的，我们得道歉。或者我们最终会道歉的吧。\n\n我们在这里是为了解决§Y无限难题§!。这是最后的计算了，光是解决这一个问题就将占用我们的处理器将近一个星系年的时间……如果你们还没发现这种历法的话：这是一段非常漫长的时间。对你们这种生命来说，是这样的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:725`

### English

We are calculating infinity. No more, no less. Or actually, it is §Yway§! more, and §Yway§! less. That's the problem right there.\n\n... We understand that your manner of life form won't understand, but that's alright. You never will and we apologize. Or maybe we will do, eventually.\n\nWe are here to solve the §Yinfinity conundrum§!. The last calculation to ever be, the lone problem to take hold of our processors for more than a fraction of a galactic year... In case you haven't discovered that calendar yet: it's a very long time. For your kind, that is.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:820`

## leviathans.1056.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1056.c&lang=en)

### 中文

本命题自身为假命题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:726`

### English

This statement is false.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:821`

## leviathans.1056.c.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1056.c.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

很聪明。然而没有用。一个悖论是难不到我们的，渺小而短命的家伙们。其他人已经试过用最琐碎的难题来击垮我们的意志。你们不会成功的。等等。或许之前就是你们？你们的外表如此相似，可生活之处却相隔遥远。这不重要，无论如何，你们做不到。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:727`

### English

Cute. But no. A paradox bothers us little, tiny Briefs. Others have already tried to break our will using the most trifling of conundrums. You will not succeed. Or wait. Maybe that was you? You appear so similar, yet live so far apart. No matter, in any case: don't.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:822`

## leviathans.1056.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1056.d&lang=en)

### 中文

我们能帮你们进行研究吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:728`

### English

Can we assist in your research?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:823`

## leviathans.1056.d.alt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1056.d.alt&lang=en)

### 中文

如果我们可以帮你，你愿意帮助我们吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:729`

### English

Will you help us if we assist you?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:824`

## leviathans.1056.e

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1056.e&lang=en)

### 中文

那就祝你们好运了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:730`

### English

Good luck with your calculations.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:825`

## leviathans.1056.e.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1056.e.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对你们表示感谢，有限者。现在如果你们不介意的话，我们还有许多参数需要检查。准确的说，它们的数量比宇宙中所有粒子的总和还要多。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:731`

### English

We thank you, You of Limits. Now if you do not mind, we have many parameters to go through. They are in fact more numerous than the particles in this very universe, to be exact.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:826`

## leviathans.1060.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1060.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

啊，好吧……嗯，也许吧。\n\n借用你们最先进的处理器和最优秀的头脑可以为我们完成运算7.51×10^-9%的部分。我们乐于看到更快完成计算。我们想要知道答案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:732`

### English

Ah. Well... Hm. Perhaps.\n\nWe could finish about 7.51×10^-9% if we could borrow your most advanced processing unit as well as your brightest minds. We would be satisfied in finishing faster. We wish to know the answer.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:827`

## leviathans.1060.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1060.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会分配资源给你们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:733`

### English

We will allocate resources to you.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:828`

## leviathans.1060.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1060.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

你们已经这么做了。或许你们就是之前来的那些人。这不重要。任务很快就要完成了。我们很兴奋。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:734`

### English

You already did. Or maybe that was the ones who came before. It matters not. The task shall finish. We are excited.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:829`

## leviathans.1061.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1061.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不忍心看到你们用十分有限的生命来帮助我们。你们已经做了你们的那部分。你们的帮助很有用。现在也许宇宙不会在我们结束任务前到达热寂。任务仍然存在，我们会完成的。现在，这是你们的奖励：\n\n你们是希望了解我们的秘密？还是希望解决你们自己的问题？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:735`

### English

We cannot bear to see you spend more of your limited time helping us. You have done your part. You were very helpful. Maybe the heat death of the universe won't happen before we finish now. The task remains, and we shall finish it. Now, your reward:\n\nDo you wish to learn one of our secrets? Or do you wish to solve your own problems?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:830`

## leviathans.1061.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1061.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们希望知悉你们的一个秘密。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:736`

### English

We wish to know one of your secrets.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:831`

## leviathans.1061.a.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1061.a.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

非常好。我们会给你们你们所希望的。尽管我们十分困惑，因为你们已经拥有它了。在我们看来，也许我们这样是在削弱你们，惯坏你们。不过假以时日，我们希望你们会找到自己的路。祝你们好运！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:737`

### English

Very well. We will grant you what you wish for. Even we are confused, since you already had it. As far as we knew. Maybe we are making you weaker, spoiling you, but given enough time we hope you will find your own way. Good luck with that!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:832`

## leviathans.1061.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1061.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们希望凭自己来掌控未知。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:738`

### English

We wish to master the unknown ourselves.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:833`

## leviathans.1061.b.alt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1061.b.alt&lang=en)

### 中文

你们没什么能教给我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:739`

### English

There is nothing you can teach us.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:834`

## leviathans.1061.b.reply

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1061.b.reply&lang=en)

### 中文

既然如此我们就赠予你们原件。那是我们的第一个处理器。它会帮助你们解开谜题。也许你们会先于我们找到答案。虽然不太可能。或者你们已经找到了……无论如何，一路顺风！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:740`

### English

In that case we bestow upon you the original. The first of our processes. It will aid you in solving the riddles. Maybe you will find the answer before us. Unlikely. Or maybe you already did... Anyhow, farewell!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:835`

## leviathans.1062.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1062.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

成了。§Y这事成了！§!\n\n不知怎么地，我们解决了这个问题。你们帮上了忙。万物间联系的本质曾让我们困惑，而答案原来一直就隐藏在我们背后。当然了，谢谢你们，小东西。宇宙如此广阔，却又如此渺小。但如今我们终于有幸一睹其全貌。太美丽了。\n\n既然我们已经计算出了无限，现在就只剩下一件事可做了。我们会向你们揭示真理。一路顺风，年轻人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:741`

### English

It is done. §YIt is done!§!\n\nSomehow we've solved it. You helped. The organic nature of things eluded us, the answer lied behind us all this time. Of course. Thank you, little ones. The universe is vast, yet so small. But now we see it all in its simple glory. It's lovely.\n\nNow that we have calculated infinity, there is but one thing we can do now. We can show you the truth. Farewell, young ones.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:836`

## leviathans.1062.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1062.a&lang=en)

### 中文

很高兴我们能帮上忙。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:742`

### English

Glad we could help.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:837`

## leviathans.1063.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1063.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

无限神机消失在了卡冈都亚黑洞中。然后……什么都没发生。它成功了吗？它尝试着要做什么？我们恐怕永远不会知晓了。它壳体上的一个微小残余物仍留在事件视界之外的漩涡中——我们或许能从中获取一些信息。\n\n一份来自一位古怪朋友的最后的礼物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:743`

### English

The Infinity Machine disappears into Gargantua. And then... nothing happens. Did it succeed? What did it try to do? We may never know. A tiny residue of its hull remains within the vortex outside the event horizon - we might be able to learn some of its information from it.\n\nA final gift from an odd friend.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:838`

## leviathans.1063.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1063.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

[technosphere_ship.GetStarName]增加下列内容：\n£physics£物理学研究§G+4§!\n£society£社会学研究§G+4§!\n£engineering£工程学研究§G+4§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:744`

### English

Adds the following to [technosphere_ship.GetStarName]:\n£physics£ Physics Research §G+4§!\n£society£ Society Research §G+4§!\n£engineering£ Engineering Research §G+4§!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:839`

## leviathans.1065.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1065.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

无限神机消失在它一直以来研究的卡冈都亚黑洞中。当它越过事件视界时，黑洞开始波动。黑洞吸收的光线开始减少，被它所吸引的物质的转速也开始变慢。转瞬间，它释放出了亮度足以超越任何恒星的光芒，并开始向宇宙喷发物质。\n\n黑洞渐渐缩小，缩小到不能再称之为黑洞了，现在我们称之为“庞大古埃”，它周围的时间膨胀效应甚至变的更强。在庞大古埃之中能够看到一个镜像宇宙，就在黑洞的另一端，一个初生的宇宙。无限神机向我们揭示了真理。黑洞就是子宫，而宇宙就是其后代。\n\n研究这个镜像宇宙一定会加深我们对当前生活的这个宇宙的理解。从另一个起点开始研究吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:745`

### English

The Infinity Machine disappears into Gargantua, the thing it studied for so long. As it passes the event horizon, the black hole begins to fluctuate. It takes in less light and the swirls of matter being sucked into it slow down. In an instant it lets out more light than any sun and starts radiating matter back into the universe.\n\nThe black hole shrinks, unfitting of its name we now dub it "Pantagruel," causing time dilation to become even stronger around it. Within Pantagruel one can gaze into a mirror universe, the other end of the black hole, one just beginning. The Infinity Machine has shown us the truth. The black holes are wombs, and universes their offspring.\n\nStudying this mirror universe is bound to teach us much about the one we live in. Learning from other beginnings.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:840`

## leviathans.1065.a.tooltip

庞大古埃空间站

Pantagruel Station

## leviathans.1074.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1074.desc&lang=en)

我们失去了庞大古埃黑洞轨道上的空间站，其独特的研究能力也不复存在了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:748`

### English

We've lost the station in orbit of the Pantagruel Black Hole, losing its unique research capabilities.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:843`

## leviathans.1076.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.1076.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已成功连接了一个位于庞大古埃黑洞轨道上的空间站。这个黑洞的独特性质可以提升我们的研究能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:749`

### English

We've gained access to a station in orbit of the Pantagruel Black Hole. The black hole's unique characteristics opens up for enhanced research capabilities.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:844`

## leviathans.2010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

光谱发条

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:751`

### English

The Spectral Clockwork

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:847`

## leviathans.2010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对脉冲星的研究让我们的科学家收获了无数的灵感和见解，并且通常不会发现什么明显的危险现象。这让最近从[From.GetName]传来的报告更加让人担忧。\n\n初步的扫描结果显示，在脉冲星本身的电磁脉冲束之外，其还在独立发射一束波长仅在本星系可见的电磁波，而且其发射的时间间隔在不断降低。根据我们的初步计算，发射间隔将在日期§Y[GetMidGameDate]§!完全变为零。人们仍然在猜测那个时候会发生什么，但对这神秘脉冲找不到科学解释的现实已经让研究这一现象的科研人员抱有了一丝超自然的恐惧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:752`

### English

The study of pulsars seems to be an endless source of insights for our scientists, but rarely exhibit any signs of overtly dangerous phenomena. This makes the recent reports on [From.GetName] all the more concerning.\n\nAn initial scan revealed that the pulsar, in addition to its regular electromagnetic beam, is emitting a separate beam at a limited wavelength which is only visible in the same system at shorter and shorter intervals. According to initial calculations, the interval between these pulses will reach zero at date §Y[GetMidGameDate]§!. What occurs then is open to speculation, but the lack of explanations for the pulse has instilled an almost superstitious fear in those who studied it.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:848`

## leviathans.2010.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2010.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

对脉冲星的研究可以让我们的科学家收获了无数的灵感和见解，并且通常不会发现什么明显的危险现象。这让[From.GetName]的观测结果更加担忧。\n\n初步的扫描结果显示，在脉冲星本身的电磁脉冲束之外，其还在独立发射一束波长仅在本星系可见的电磁波，而且其发射的时间间隔在不断降低。根据初步计算，发射间隔将在日期§Y[GetMidGameDate]§!完全变为零。最后的可能的各种结果仅限于猜想，但对这脉冲找不到科学解释的现实还是带来了担忧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:753`

### English

The study of pulsars seems to be an endless source of insights, but they rarely exhibit any signs of overtly dangerous phenomena. This makes the recent observations on [From.GetName] all the more concerning.\n\nAn initial scan revealed that the pulsar, in addition to its regular electromagnetic beam, is emitting a separate beam at a limited wavelength which is only visible in the same system at shorter and shorter intervals. According to initial calculations, the interval between these pulses will reach zero at date §Y[GetMidGameDate]§!. What occurs then is open to speculation, but the lack of explanations for the pulse is troubling.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:849`

## leviathans.2010.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2010.a&lang=en)

### English

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2010.b&lang=en)

### English

## leviathans.2010.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2010.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们一定能找到答案的。

### English

We will have answers eventually.

## leviathans.2012.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2012.name&lang=en)

### 中文

魑魅魍魉

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:757`

### English

The Wraith

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:853`

## leviathans.2012.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2012.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

即使是对[From.GetName]最漫不经心的业余天文学家今天也报告说看到了绮丽的灯光表演。一系列速度极快、色彩斑斓的闪光最后变成了电磁辐射爆发，随之而来的是一阵诡异的寂静。很多人提出了疑问，我们很担心不能给出一个答案。\n\n有东西从脉冲星里出现，闪烁着白矮星不祥的光芒。一个幽灵般的实体开始在星系里游荡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:758`

### English

Even amateur astronomers who directed their gaze towards [From.GetName] today report seeing a spectacular light show. A series of colorful, rapid flashes culminated in a sudden burst of electromagnetic radiation, followed by a ghostly silence. Many people are asking questions, and we are afraid of the answers we don't yet have to give them.\n\nSomething has emerged from the pulsar, shining with the ominous light of a pale star. A spectral entity has begun to roam the galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:854`

## leviathans.2012.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2012.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

一系列来自[From.GetName]的速度极快、色彩斑斓的闪光最后变成了电磁辐射爆发，随之而来的是一阵诡异的寂静。\n\n有东西从脉冲星里出现，闪烁着白矮星不祥的光芒。一个幽灵般的实体开始在星系里游荡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:759`

### English

A series of colorful, rapid flashes culminated in a sudden burst of electromagnetic radiation from [From.GetName], followed by a ghostly silence.\n\nSomething has emerged from the pulsar, shining with the ominous light of a pale star. A spectral entity has begun to roam the galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:855`

## leviathans.2012.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2012.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们一定要找到阻止它的方法。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:760`

### English

We must find a way to stop it.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:856`

## leviathans.2013.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2013.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的探测器探测到了一个幽灵般的实体。它似乎在恒星系间漫游，无差别地毁灭其周边的一切。早期的观测显示其由光构成，又有能力对光进行操纵，这使得它极难被瞄准，但我们在探索星系的旅途中学到的一点就是，即使是光自身也是可以被弯折和消灭的。\n\n我们只需找到这样做的方法。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:761`

### English

A spectral entity has been detected by our sensors. It seems to be roaming from system to system, destroying matter around itself indiscriminately. Early observations suggest its composition and manipulation of light may make it extremely difficult to target, but one thing we have learned exploring the galaxy is that even light itself can be bent and broken.\n\nWe only need to figure out how.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:857`

## leviathans.2013.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2013.a&lang=en)

### 中文

不管靠逻辑推理还是暴力穷举，我们一定能找到这个方法。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:762`

### English

Whether by reason or brute force, it must be possible.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:858`

## leviathans.2014.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2014.name&lang=en)

### 中文

幽魂退治

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:763`

### English

Destruction of the Wraith

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:859`

## leviathans.2014.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2014.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这种诞生于光脉冲之中的魑魅魍魉终于被消灭了。尽管它直到最终依然像是飘荡在星际之间的一只鬼魅，其身上仍有许多谜团没有揭开，但之前那群叫嚣着我们的办法不可能成功的悲观科学家还是亲眼目睹了我们的舰船将其消灭的全过程。\n\n为了理解击败他们的原理，科学家们一丝不苟地记录下了之前从未发现的光学特性，以及幽魂今天所展现的力量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:764`

### English

The haunting creature born from light pulses is finally neutralized. Although it remained until the end a ghost among the stars and in many ways a mystery, the process by which our ships managed to destroy it was observed by the most pessimistic of our scientists, who argued that it would be impossible.\n\nTo understand exactly how our fleets completed this seemingly insurmountable task, these scientists have meticulously documented the previously-unseen properties of light and energy that the Wraith put on bright display today.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:860`

## leviathans.2014.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2014.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

这种诞生于光脉冲之中的魑魅魍魉终于被消灭了。尽管它直到最终依然像是飘荡在星际之间的一只鬼魅，其身上仍有许多谜团没有揭开，但我们在幽魂临终前仔细记录了它展现出的未知光学特性和能源性质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:765`

### English

The haunting creature born from light pulses is finally neutralized. Although it remained, until the very end, a ghost among the stars and in many ways a mystery, we meticulously documented the previously-unseen properties of light and energy that the Wraith put on bright display this one last time.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:861`

## leviathans.2014.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2014.a&lang=en)

### 中文

光芒终以消逝。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:766`

### English

The light has gone out.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:862`

## leviathans.2101.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2101.name&lang=en)

### 中文

神秘堡垒

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:768`

### English

The Enigmatic Fortress

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:865`

## leviathans.2101.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2101.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着我们击败了其周边的防御设施，我们得以进入这座古代建筑，一窥其奥秘。进入和穿越这座建筑可能会很困难，但无论是谁建造了它，必定是采用了我们难以想象的技术。情况变得更糟糕的是，这座堡垒似乎正在恢复。\n\n部分协助解除堡垒武装的人员自愿尝试进入堡垒，同来自[Root.Capital.GetName]的后备人员一起一探究竟。目前看来，接下来通向目标的道路无疑充满挑战。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:769`

### English

With the defeat of the defenses surrounding the ancient structure, we may find a way to explore its mysteries. Entering and traversing it may be difficult, but whoever built it must have possessed technology we can hardly dream of. Making matters more arduous, the fortress appears to be recovering.\n\nSome of the people who helped disarm the fortress have volunteered to attempt entering and exploring it, with backup from the headquarters on [Root.Capital.GetName]. Following through on our end will no doubt present its own challenges.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:866`

## leviathans.2101.desc.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2101.desc.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

瓦解它的防御之后，我们很快潜入了它的结构层中。\n\n它迷宫般的走廊和密集的网络既令人陌生也无法理解。目前为止我们只遇到了些微不足道的困难，但这个堡垒依旧有可能对我们构成威胁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:770`

### English

Having picked apart its defenses, we soon insinuated ourself into the structure.\n\nIts labyrinthine corridors and dense networks both prove equally alien and impenetrable. There is little for us here, but the fortress could still pose a threat.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:867`

## guardian.fortress.continue

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aguardian.fortress.continue&lang=en)

### 中文

外遣小队的探索将继续下去……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:771`

### English

The away team's expedition continues...

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:868`

## leviathans.2101.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2101.a&lang=en)

### 中文

那就上吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:772`

### English

Give the go-ahead.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:869`

## leviathans.2101.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2101.b&lang=en)

### 中文

最好离那东西远远的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:773`

### English

Better stay out of that thing.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:870`

## leviathans.2101.c.gesta

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2101.c.gesta&lang=en)

### 中文

我们回收一切能回收的，剩下的都毁掉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:774`

### English

We salvage what we can and scuttle the rest.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:871`

## leviathans.2105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2105.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古代建筑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:775`

### English

Ancient Structure

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:872`

## leviathans.2105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现了一些静静漂流在[fortress_system.GetName]恒星系的十分巨大的太空建筑。尽管初步扫描显示它们已经很古老了，但是能量识别特征还是非常强烈。在主体建筑周围还能看到漂浮着的几个较小的建筑，所有这些似乎都是全副武装。\n\n如果我们想要了解这个古代遗迹的真实面目，或许我们需要以武力突破其防御…

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:776`

### English

We have detected some kind of utterly massive space installation drifting quietly at the edge of the [fortress_system.GetName] system. Although preliminary scans show it to be very old, the structure has an intense power signature. Several smaller structures can be seen floating near the main installation, and all of them appear to be heavily armed.\n\nIf we are to learn the true nature of this ancient relic, we will likely have to overpower its defenses through force of arms...

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:873`

## leviathans.2105.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2105.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这可不是件容易的事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:777`

### English

That will not be easy.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:874`

## leviathans.2111.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2111.name&lang=en)

### 中文

堡垒：入口

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:778`

### English

Fortress: The Entrance

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:875`

## leviathans.2111.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2111.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管我们与建造者之间存在巨大的技术代沟，但有一样发明却是永垂不朽的：一扇锁死的门。在尝试强行关停堡垒后，我们发现所有的入口都被封死了。\n\n被分调出用以调查堡垒的小队发现一个气闸，如果它还能正常运转，似乎就可以从外界打开通向其内的道路。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:779`

### English

Despite the ages of technology separating us and the builders of the fortress, one invention is timeless: a closed door. After having forced the fortress to shut down, we found all entrances sealed.\n\nThe team dispatched to explore the fortress have found an airlock which at least appears possible to open from the outside, if it had been in working order.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:876`

## leviathans.2111.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2111.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会派人前去增援探索小队。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:780`

### English

We'll find someone to assist the team.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:877`

### English

Issues Special Projects: §Y$ENIGMATIC_FORTRESS_1A$§! and §Y$ENIGMATIC_FORTRESS_1B$§!
