## leviathans.2112.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2112.name&lang=en)

### 中文

堡垒：拆除失控

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:782`

### English

Fortress: Uncontrolled Demolition

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:879`

## leviathans.2112.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2112.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

爆破小队依照计划炸开了气闸，来给我们的探查小队打开一条通道。然而爆炸并不只是点燃了气闸，由于未知的原因，也许是有意留下的陷阱，气闸内含的高气压的爆炸性气体混合着氧气混合，引发了一场更大的爆炸，我们的排险小队试图解决，但无一生还。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:783`

### English

The detonation set to blow the airlock open went off as planned, providing an entrance for our team of explorers. However, the explosives were not the only thing to ignite. For reasons unknown, possibly as a deliberate trap, the airlock contained pressurized explosive gas in addition to the expected oxygen, causing a larger explosion that wiped out the demolitions team sent to set it up.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:880`

## leviathans.2112.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2112.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们只能希望他们的牺牲是值得的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:784`

### English

We can only hope to make their sacrifice worth it.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:881`

## leviathans.2112.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2112.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

探索者进入堡垒。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:785`

### English

The explorers enter the fortress.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:882`

## leviathans.2113.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2113.name&lang=en)

### 中文

堡垒：调整能源供给

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:786`

### English

Fortress: Adjusting Energy Supply

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:883`

## leviathans.2113.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2113.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

稳定了飞船同堡垒动力系统的连接后，我们必须决定有多少能量用于第一次注入。因为堡垒使用了陌生的技术，精确的能量值难以确定。现在有两种估计方案可选。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:787`

### English

Having stabilized the ship's connection to the power system of the fortress, we must decide how much energy to supply in the first surge. Since the technology is unfamiliar, the exact amount is difficult to determine. Two estimates are available.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:884`

## leviathans.2113.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2113.a&lang=en)

### 中文

按照较高的估计进行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:788`

### English

Go with the higher estimate.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:885`

## leviathans.2113.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2113.b&lang=en)

### 中文

按照较低的估计进行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:789`

### English

Go with the lower estimate.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:886`

## leviathans.2114.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2114.name&lang=en)

### 中文

堡垒：重新供能

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:790`

### English

Fortress: Repowering

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:887`

## leviathans.2114.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2114.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

注入堡垒以开启大门的高水平能量足以导致堡垒内的深层能源激活。堡垒的防御系统再次启动，关闭他们之前不必再管气闸了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:791`

### English

The high level of energy sent into the fortress to power the door was powerful enough to cause the reignition of a deeper energy source within. With its defenses online once again, the airlock will not be a concern until we've disabled them.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:888`

## leviathans.2114.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2114.a&lang=en)

### 中文

做好准备吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:792`

### English

Brace yourselves.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:889`

## leviathans.2115.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2115.name&lang=en)

### 中文

堡垒：气闸开启

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:793`

### English

Fortress: Airlock Opened

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:890`

## leviathans.2115.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2115.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一开始，注入堡垒的能量总量似乎太少，没能起到什么作用。随后，周围区域的气闸开始慢慢显示出恢复运作的迹象。靠着一些机械学的技巧和猜测，探索小队获得了控制权并成功打开气闸。虽然对接的流程不怎么规范，他们现在确实进入了堡垒内部。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:794`

### English

At first, the amount of energy supplied to the fortress seemed too small to make a difference. Then, the area surrounding the airlock slowly started to show signs of functioning. With some mechanical tricks and guesswork, the team accessed the controls and managed to open the airlock itself. Although not by the cleanest docking procedure, they are now inside the fortress.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:891`

## leviathans.2115.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2115.a&lang=en)

### 中文

希望他们能平安归来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:795`

### English

Let's hope we can also get them out.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:892`

## leviathans.2131.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2131.name&lang=en)

### 中文

堡垒：梵塔

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:796`

### English

Fortress: The Tower

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:893`

## leviathans.2131.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2131.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

外遣小队发现前面的道路被巨大的舱壁挡住了。小队报告说他们右方有一处嵌入墙壁的凹室，里面放着一个基座和置于其上的奇怪的机械装置，以和堡垒同样的金属材质铸造而成。\n\n他们相信，这个装置就是打开舱壁的钥匙。小队长将这个装置描述为在基座顶部等距排列的三个立杆，有三个环——金属圆环——大小不一，放置在左边的立杆上，最小的在顶部，而最大的在最下面。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:797`

### English

The away team find their way blocked by a massive bulkhead. The team report that there is a recessed alcove to their right, housing a pedestal and a contraption wrought in the same black metal as the fortress itself.\n\nThey believe this device is the key to opening the bulkhead. The leader of the explorers describes it as three upright poles spaced equidistant across the top of the pedestal, with three rings - metal tori - of varying sizes placed around the leftmost pole, the smallest at the top and the largest at the bottom.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:894`

## leviathans.2131.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2131.a&lang=en)

### 中文

把金属环放在另外两个立杆上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:798`

### English

Place metal rings around the remaining two poles.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:895`

## leviathans.2131.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2131.b&lang=en)

### 中文

寻找舱壁上的圆环形缺口。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:799`

### English

Search for torus-shaped indentations on the bulkhead.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:896`

## leviathans.2131.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2131.c&lang=en)

### 中文

重排立杆上的金属圆环。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:800`

### English

Rearrange the metal tori on the poles.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:897`

## leviathans.2131.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2131.d&lang=en)

### 中文

拆掉基座上中间和右边的竖杆。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:801`

### English

Remove center and rightmost poles from the pedestal.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:898`

## leviathans.2132.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2132.name&lang=en)

### 中文

堡垒：令人不安的声响

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:802`

### English

Fortress: Disturbing Noises

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:899`

## leviathans.2132.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2132.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

行动指令发布后，随之而来的是一阵寂静，随着小队试图执行命令，又传来了模糊不清的刮擦噪音。又是一阵寂静后，模糊不清的曳步声和越来越尖锐的声音持续了五秒。接着是一阵长长的寂静。我们花了几分钟才确定，这次探索已经结束。没有尖叫声，也没有人呼救。我们至少可以希冀，这意味着他们没有承受太多痛苦。\n\n一刻钟后，一阵缓慢而沉重的敲击声暗示着，无论是何种陷阱被激活，现在其已被重置。一些在场者声称，这听起来就像是异星人的笑声。\n\n最后，堡垒中响起了更加令人不安的声音：堡垒的能源重启了防御系统。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:803`

### English

After the course of action was announced, a moment of silence followed, then vague scraping noises as the team attempted to carry out their plan. After another pause, faint shuffling and a draw-out, sharp sound increasing in pitch over five seconds. Then a long silence. It took us several minutes to accept that this was the end of the expedition. No screaming, no calls for aid. We can at least hope this means they met a quick end.\n\nAfter a quarter, a slow, heavy rasp suggests whatever trap sprung on them reset itself. Some present claim it sounded like alien laughter.\n\nFinally, the fortress managed an even more disturbing sound: that of the power returning to its defense systems.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:900`

## leviathans.2132.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2132.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这鬼地方就是个可怕的机关。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:804`

### English

This place is a hellish contraption.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:901`

## leviathans.2133.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2133.name&lang=en)

### 中文

堡垒：梵塔重建

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:805`

### English

Fortress: Tower Rebuilt

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:902`

## leviathans.2133.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2133.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

行动方案宣布后，先是出现了片刻的寂静，随后正当队伍尝试执行计划时，出现了隐隐约约的刮擦声。然后响起了巨大的拍击声。一时间，你还以为你的队伍像虫子一样被拍死了。紧接着，你听到了探索队的欢呼，他们兴奋地报告说通道打开了。\n\n隔板迅速化成了紫色水银般的难闻液体。想起之前那个无聊的解谜机关，你不禁觉得你的探索队被数千年前精心设下的一个玩笑给耍了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:806`

### English

After the course of action was announced, a moment of silence followed, then vague scraping noises as the team attempted to carry out their plan. A splashing sound, so loud that at first you think the team has been swatted like small insects, is followed by cheers from the team as they report that a passage has opened.\n\nThe bulkhead appears to have rapidly transformed into an unpleasant-smelling liquid reminiscent of purple quicksilver. Combined with the relatively mundane puzzle mechanism, you have a feeling the team has been victims of an elaborate prank set up millennia ago.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:903`

## leviathans.2133.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2133.a&lang=en)

### 中文

至少我们可以开怀大笑了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:807`

### English

We can laugh about it now, at least.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:904`

## leviathans.2150.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2150.name&lang=en)

### 中文

堡垒：中枢

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:808`

### English

Fortress: The Pivot

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:905`

## leviathans.2150.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2150.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

探索队发来报告，称他们似乎进入了堡垒的几何中心。只不过堡垒的内部结构还存有一些疑点，而且也不能确切地说中心这个概念适用于这里。\n\n但有一点是毫无争议的，那就是堡垒的能源供给方式，这使得探索队的新发现更加耐人寻味了。暗物质在堡垒周围流动，然后回到核心，这有点类似血液循环系统。虽然流动已经停止了，但小队发现有重新启动的迹象。我们确实可以花费大把的时间研究这套系统，但眼下我们得赶紧将它关闭。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:809`

### English

The team reports that they have reached what appears to be the geometric center of the fortress, even if the internal geometry leaves some doubt if the concept of a center is even relevant in there.\n\nWhat is indisputably relevant is how the fortress is powered, which makes the team's new discovery all the more interesting. Dark matter is funneled around the fortress and then back to the core, like a cardiovascular system. Although the flow has stopped, the team has found signs that it's starting again. We can take all the time we want to study it, but it needs to be stopped now.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:906`

## leviathans.2150.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2150.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会找到方法的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:810`

### English

We'll find a way.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:907`

## leviathans.2151.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2151.name&lang=en)

### 中文

堡垒：暗物质核心

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:811`

### English

Fortress: The Heart of Dark Matter

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:908`

## leviathans.2151.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2151.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

依靠着我们所掌握关于暗物质的最新知识对黑洞进行分析后发现，这个堡垒的建造者竟然从黑洞中习得了不少关于控制物质与输送系统的科技。幸好这一切都处于精密的主系统之外，我们的探索队得以安全地改换了核心的连接线路，阻止堡垒的重新启动。\n\n虽然这个过程已让我们的团队受益良多，但相对于整座已经敞开在我们面前的堡垒来说，那简直是小巫见大巫。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:812`

### English

Examining a black hole with our new knowledge of how dark matter is used in the ancient fortress, we have discovered that its builders must have learned many of the manipulation and funneling techniques from black holes. Seeing them outside of the strict system they created allowed the team of explorers to change connections around the core safely, stopping the repowering process.\n\nWhile this process alone has taught us much about the physics of black holes, it is nothing compared to the treasure that the now safely shut-down fortress presents.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:909`

## leviathans.2151.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2151.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们一定要多加珍惜才行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:813`

### English

We shall cherish it.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:910`

## leviathans.2152.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2152.name&lang=en)

### 中文

堡垒：星海见证者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:814`

### English

Fortress: Our Star Witness

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:911`

## leviathans.2152.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2152.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在附近一带的恒星中搜寻堡垒是如何建成的线索时，无意中发现一个秘密。该恒星附近有一簇纳米摄像头，担当着堡垒的眼睛，这就难怪它的防御能力那么高效了。\n\n只要将这些摄像头中的数据记录跟堡垒中的线索连接起来，我们应该就能查看这个堡垒被建造之初的相关历史。但因为时间无多，我们该优先查看哪一部分的建造过程呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:815`

### English

Scouring the local star for clues to how the fortress was built, we discovered a surprising secret. A fine mist of nanotechnological cameras have been found in close proximity to the star, acting as the eyes of the fortress and explaining the efficiency of its defenses.\n\nAccessing these with data from the fortress, we can view the history of the system all the way back to its construction. However, we are running out of time. Which part of the construction process should we review for answers?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:912`

开端。

## leviathans.2153.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2153.name&lang=en)

### 中文

堡垒：没用的开端

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:819`

### English

Fortress: Fruitless Beginnings

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:916`

## leviathans.2153.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2153.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们查看了堡垒建造之初时的录像以期看到堡垒心脏部分的建造过程，可惜结果让人很失望。最初建造的是堡垒的躯壳，然而这些数据对我们毫无意义。这时候堡垒开始了重启，摄像头和录像的使用权也被其重夺回去了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:820`

### English

Hoping to see the heart of the fortress built first, we reviewed the beginning of the construction. We hoped in vain. The hull was built first, making the data useless. Our team are reporting that the fortress is repowering, and with it its control of the cameras and their footage.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:917`

## leviathans.2153.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2153.a&lang=en)

### 中文

没时间了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:821`

### English

No more time.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:918`

## leviathans.2154.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2154.name&lang=en)

### 中文

堡垒：中途

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:822`

### English

Fortress: The Middle Path

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:919`

## leviathans.2154.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2154.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

镜头的中段，虽然没有展示核心自身的建设，但却揭示了科学家们认为用于建造核心的材料的运输。这一定是结构需要动力来继续建设的关键时刻。\n\n有了这些材料的知识，堡垒内团队设法阻止了堡垒的重启。我们可以收获成功的果实了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:823`

### English

The middle section of the footage, although not showing the construction of the core itself, reveals the shipping of materials to the fortress which scientists conclude must have been used for the core. This must have been the critical time at which the structure needed power for construction to continue.\n\nWith knowledge of these materials, the team inside the fortress has managed to stop it from repowering. We can now reap the fruits of our success.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:920`

## leviathans.2154.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2154.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们当之无愧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:824`

### English

Well deserved.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:921`

## leviathans.2155.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2155.name&lang=en)

### 中文

堡垒：结尾

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:825`

### English

Fortress: The End

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:922`

## leviathans.2155.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2155.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

查看建造过程的最终部分时，我们才发现原来这部分是需要堡垒启动后才可以观看的。我们开始观看的时候一定是触发了核心的重新启动。我们的团队汇报堡垒正在重新启动，摄像头和录像的使用权也被夺回了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:826`

### English

Reviewing the final stages of the construction, we discovered too late that they must have required the fortress to already be powered. The core must have been functional at the point where we started our viewing. Our team are reporting that the fortress is repowering, and with it its control of the cameras and their footage.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:923`

## leviathans.2155.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2155.a&lang=en)

### 中文

没时间了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:827`

### English

No more time.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:924`

## leviathans.2156.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2156.name&lang=en)

### 中文

堡垒：暗物质策略

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:828`

### English

Fortress: Dark Matter Gambit

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:925`

## leviathans.2156.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2156.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

完成堡垒的人一定比我们更博学，但他们并不更聪明。我们向堡垒动力系统循环注入自己的暗物质的努力是有用的，有效地切断了核心对其系统的供能。\n\n我们终于有时间仔细看看这些博学的人完成了什么东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:829`

### English

Whoever built the fortress might have been more knowledgeable than we, but not more clever. Supplying our own dark matter to close the loop of the power system in the fortress worked, and the core is now effectively cut off from providing power to its systems.\n\nFinally, we can take the time to have a closer look at what these knowledgeable people have accomplished.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:926`

## leviathans.2156.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2156.a&lang=en)

### 中文

终于成功了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:830`

### English

Finally.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:927`

## leviathans.2157.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2157.name&lang=en)

### 中文

堡垒：一箭穿心

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:831`

### English

Fortress: Shot Through the Heart

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:928`

## leviathans.2157.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2157.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一位飞行员以前所未有的勇气和精准度，向堡垒的心脏发射了一枚鱼雷，终止了它的重启。我们的计划必然被誉为伟大的成功，因为堡垒永远不会再启动了。\n\n鱼雷命中的时刻，整个结构都爆炸了，系统中的古代技术痕迹和所有恒星系中的一切物体都被摧毁。考古学家称之为“[Root.GetAdj]政府出资的最昂贵的烟花秀”。看来这一纪录短期内不会被挑战了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:832`

### English

In a feat of unprecedented bravery and precision, a pilot fired a torpedo into the heart of the fortress to stop it from repowering. The plan must be hailed as a great success, since the fortress will never be powered again.\n\nThe moment the torpedo hit, the entire structure exploded, irreversibly destroying any trace of the ancient technology in it as well as everything else in the system. Archaeologists have dubbed it "the most expensive fireworks show ever funded by the [Root.GetAdj] government." It is unlikely to be beaten anytime soon.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:929`

## leviathans.2157.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2157.a&lang=en)

### 中文

有本事他们自己来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:833`

### English

Everyone's a critic.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:930`

恒星系在燃烧。

### English

## leviathans.2160.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2160.name&lang=en)

### 中文

堡垒：重新供能

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:835`

### English

Fortress: Repowering

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:932`

## leviathans.2160.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2160.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

时间不够了。神秘的堡垒已从我们起初的攻击中恢复、重启防御。与勘探队的通信被干扰了，但最后的通讯表明他们面临着他们预想中的命运。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:836`

### English

Time has run out. The enigmatic fortress has recovered from our initial assault and is once again starting up its defenses. Transmissions from the exploration team have already been scrambled, but their last transmission indicates that they met the fate they were prepared to risk.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:933`

## leviathans.2160.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.2160.a&lang=en)

### 中文

但并非是我们希望的那一种。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:837`

### English

But not the one we hoped for.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:934`

## leviathans.3001

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3001&lang=en)

### 中文

虚空中的梦魇

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:839`

### English

Horror in the Void

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:937`

## leviathans.3001.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3001.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[horror_system.GetName]附近遇到巨型实体，它似乎只是部分地存在于我们的宇宙，这个生物的身体大部分还困在它所来自的噩梦般的位面。\n\n有一个恶质的智能在此工作，古老程度远远超出我们的时间概念，设计也极其异类。我们只能猜测它的目的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:840`

### English

We have encountered some kind of vast entity near [horror_system.GetName]. It appears to exist only partially inside our own universe, with the bulk of the creature still trapped in whatever nightmarish realm it spawned from.\n\nThere is a malevolent intelligence at work here, ancient beyond our concept of time and utterly alien in its design. We can only guess as to its purpose.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:938`

## leviathans.3001.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3001.a&lang=en)

### 中文

来者不善，善者不来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:841`

### English

It can't be good.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:939`

## leviathans.3003

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3003&lang=en)

### 中文

逐出

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:842`

### English

Banished

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:940`

## leviathans.3003.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3003.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[guardians_horror_system.GetName]附近试图突入我们宇宙的恶质实体被赶回原位面。它的影响是否已被削弱到能被妥善地封印，只有时间能告诉我们。\n\n我们监测到某种反常的残余能量，一定是生物打开空间裂隙时留下的。深入研究这种能量可能带来科学突破。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:843`

### English

The malignant entity which attempted to breach our universe near [guardians_horror_system.GetName] has been banished back into its own realm. Only time will tell if its influence has been sufficiently weakened to keep it there for good.\n\nWe are detecting some kind of anomalous residual energy that must have been left behind by the dimensional rifts the creature opened. Studying this energy more closely could potentially lead to scientific breakthroughs.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:941`

## leviathans.3003.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3003.a&lang=en)

### 中文

不妨一试。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:844`

### English

Let us see what will come of it.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:942`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:845`

### English

$leviathans.3004.intro$\n\n$leviathans.3004.jumpdrive$

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:943`

## leviathans.3004b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3004b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$leviathans.3004.intro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:846`

### English

$leviathans.3004.intro$

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:944`

## leviathans.3004.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3004.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对[guardians_horror_system.GetName]次元裂缝所产生的残留能源的研究终于有结果了。产生这些传送门的能源可是我们闻所未闻的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:847`

### English

Our study of the energy anomalies produced by the [guardians_horror_system.GetName] dimensional rifts has finally yielded results. The energies involved in the creation of these portals are unlike any we have ever encountered.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:945`

## leviathans.3004.jumpdrive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3004.jumpdrive&lang=en)

### 中文

最关键之处在于，我们的科学家认为这些能源可以用来开发出一种全新的星际旅行方式。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:848`

### English

Most intriguing of all, our scientists theorize that it may be possible to devise a new method of interstellar travel using these exotic energies.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:946`

## leviathans.3100

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3100&lang=en)

### 中文

自动无畏战舰

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:850`

### English

Automated Dreadnought

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:949`

## leviathans.3100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[dreadnought_system.GetName]恒星系发现一艘形单影只的巨型战舰在巡航。船只上未发现生命迹象，但发出强烈能量特征，可能是装备有武器系统。该舰没有回应任何问候信息。\n\n建议小心为上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:851`

### English

A single, massive warship has been discovered patrolling the [dreadnought_system.GetName] system. No life signs can be detected on the vessel, but it is emitting a very strong power signature and appears to be bristling with weapon systems. It does not respond to hails.\n\nCaution is advised.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:950`

## leviathans.3100.desc.mach

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3100.desc.mach&lang=en)

### 中文

[dreadnought_system.GetName]恒星系发现一艘形单影只的巨型战舰在巡航。船只上未发现有机生命迹象，但发出强烈能量特征，可能是装备有武器系统。该舰没有回应任何问候信息或数据传输。\n\n建议小心为上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:852`

### English

A single, massive warship has been discovered patrolling the [dreadnought_system.GetName] system. No organic life signs can be detected on the vessel, but it is emitting a very strong power signature and appears to be bristling with weapon systems. It does not respond to hails or data transmission.\n\nCaution is advised.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:951`

## leviathans.3100.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3100.a&lang=en)

### 中文

好一个大家伙！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:853`

### English

Look at the size of that thing!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:952`

## leviathans.3102

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3102&lang=en)

### 中文

无畏战舰已被瘫痪

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:854`

### English

Dreadnought Disabled

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:953`

## leviathans.3102.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3102.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们成功在[guardians_dreadnought_system.GetName]星系使这艘自动化无畏舰丧失了行动力。虽然我方炮火猛烈，但是这艘战舰的舰体却大部分完好无损。\n\n我们派出的特遣队进入该舰后发现里面的外星种群船员早已身亡多年，他们在临终前启动了某种自动巡逻模式，而这艘战舰的自动巡逻模式竟然已经运行了780万年。\n\n虽然会耗费甚多资源，一些工程师相信该舰可以修复并服役于我军。它一定会成为强大的旗舰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:855`

### English

We have managed to disable the automated dreadnought in the [guardians_dreadnought_system.GetName] system. Despite the punishment it took from our ships, the structural integrity of the warship's massive hull remains largely intact.\n\nOur boarding parties found the mummified remains of the dreadnought's alien crew still manning their stations. Before expiring, they activated some kind of automated patrol mode which the limited shipboard AI has been locked in for the past 7.8 million years.\n\nAlthough it would take an incredible amount of resources, some of our engineers believe that the dreadnought can be restored and put back into service with our fleet. It would certainly make for a powerful flagship.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:954`

## leviathans.3102.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3102.a&lang=en)

### 中文

让该舰悬挂着我们的旗帜征战星辰大海吧！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:856`

### English

That ship will sail the stars again under our flag!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:955`

## leviathans.3102.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3102.b&lang=en)

### 中文

那样做太浪费资源了。把它拆除掉回收利用吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:857`

### English

It would be a waste of resources. Salvage it for parts.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:956`

## leviathans.3103

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3103&lang=en)

### 中文

无畏舰修复成功

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:858`

### English

Dreadnought Repaired

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:957`

## leviathans.3103.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3103.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这是值得载入史册的一天！我们训练有素的维修组和工程组成功完成了[guardians_dreadnought_system.GetName]星系的那艘古代无畏舰的维修工程。现在这艘无畏舰已经满载着我们的机组人员开始试航了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:859`

### English

This is a momentous day! Our skilled repair crews and engineers have finished their work on the ancient dreadnought we disabled in the [guardians_dreadnought_system.GetName] system. The massive warship has received its full crew complement and been declared ready for service after conducting space trials.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:958`

## leviathans.3103.desc.mach

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3103.desc.mach&lang=en)

### 中文

今天是意义非凡的一天！我们娴熟的修理机器人和工程单位修好了我们在[guardians_dreadnought_system.GetName]恒星系击败的远古无畏舰。这艘巨型战舰的船员已经齐备，经过航空测试后开始准备服役。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:860`

### English

This is a day of significance! Our skilled repair bots and engineering units have finished their work on the ancient dreadnought we disabled in the [guardians_dreadnought_system.GetName] system. The massive warship has received its full crew complement and been declared ready for service after conducting space trials.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:959`

## leviathans.3103.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3103.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我军如虎添翼。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:861`

### English

A powerful addition to our fleet.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:960`

## leviathans.3201

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3201&lang=en)

### 中文

大群蜂巢攻击机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:863`

### English

Swarming Hive Craft

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:963`

## leviathans.3201.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3201.base&lang=en)

### 中文

快速移动的攻击机正在袭击我们位于[hiver_system.GetName]星系内的开采站！这些身份不明的攻击者从一些此前在环带中休眠着的小行星中蜂拥而出，这些小行星现在正散发出强烈的能量信号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:864`

### English

Fast-moving attack craft are hitting our mining stations in the [hiver_system.GetName] system! These unidentified aggressors swarmed out of several previously dormant asteroids in the belt that are now displaying huge power signatures.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:964`

## leviathans.3201.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3201.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$leviathans.3201.base$\n\n我们只能推测，这些小行星是一种未知的太空生命形式的蜂巢。不管它们是什么，我们不断扩大的采矿活动似乎已经激怒了它们！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:865`

### English

$leviathans.3201.base$\n\nWe can only surmise that these asteroids serve as some kind of hive for a previously unknown spaceborne life form. Whatever they are, our expanding mining operations seem to have provoked them into action!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:965`

## leviathans.3201.desc.void_hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3201.desc.void_hive&lang=en)

### 中文

$leviathans.3201.base$\n\n由于我们最近的开采行动，小行星内的巢穴受到了刺激。似乎我们和它在这个星系是一山不容二虎了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:866`

### English

$leviathans.3201.base$\n\nThe hive inside of the asteroid has become agitated by our recent mining. It seems this system isn't big enough for the both of us.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:966`

## leviathans.3201.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3201.a&lang=en)

### 中文

它们在快速逼近！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:867`

### English

They are coming in fast!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:967`

## leviathans.3202

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3202&lang=en)

### 中文

晶态小行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:868`

### English

Crystalline Asteroids

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:968`

## leviathans.3202.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3202.base&lang=en)

### 中文

[hiver_system.GetName]恒星系是一种具有独特性质的异常密集小行星环带的家乡。一些大型的小行星表面覆盖着突出的结晶，似乎是非常丰富的矿物矿藏。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:869`

### English

The [hiver_system.GetName] system is home to an unusually dense asteroid belt with unique properties. Several of the larger asteroids are covered in crystalline outcroppings that appear to be extremely rich mineral deposits.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:969`

## leviathans.3202.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3202.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$leviathans.3202.base$\n\n然而，远程传感器正在追踪几个小型的、无法识别的可能是宇宙飞船的物体的零星运动，它们位于环带中的密度较大的区域。我们无法准确对其定位，但我们已经确定了至少两个被摧毁的采矿站的碎片，它们漂浮在一些较大小行星附近的太空中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:870`

### English

$leviathans.3202.base$\n\nHowever, long-range sensors are tracking sporadic movement from several small, unidentifiable objects - possibly spacecraft - in the denser regions of the belt. We are unable to get a fix on them, but we have identified debris from at least two destroyed mining stations, floating in space near some of the larger asteroids.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:970`

## leviathans.3202.desc.void_hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3202.desc.void_hive&lang=en)

### 中文

$leviathans.3202.base$\n\n我们探测到了环带中的异动：一个蜂巢思维的初级形式正在享用其矿物储备。我们可以吸收他们的意识，在我们的引导下他们将会是更强大的战力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:871`

### English

$leviathans.3202.base$\n\nWe have detected movement within the belt: a form of rudimentary hive mind, feasting on its mineral hoard. We could subsume their consciousness and under our guidance they would be a powerful deterrent.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:971`

## leviathans.3202.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3202.a&lang=en)

### 中文

有点不对劲……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:872`

### English

Something isn't right...

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:972`

## leviathans.3202.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3202.b&lang=en)

### 中文

他们与我们相似，让他们为我们服务。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:873`

### English

They are similar. Let them serve.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:973`

## leviathans.3202.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3202.c&lang=en)

### 中文

让我们看看他们会对我们作何反应。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:874`

### English

Let's see how they react to us.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:974`

## leviathans.3204

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3204&lang=en)

### 中文

小行星蜂群被击败

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:875`

### English

Asteroid Hives Defeated

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:976`

## leviathans.3204.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3204.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的部队成功摧毁了[hive_system.GetName]星系的外星蜂巢小行星。该地区没有再出现任何攻击机了。\n\n其中一个小行星虽然受到猛烈攻击但却大致完好无损。我们可以考虑派出一支科研队上去查看下。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:876`

### English

Our forces have managed to smash the alien hive asteroids in the [hive_system.GetName] system. They are once again dormant, and we are not detecting any more of those vicious attack craft.\n\nOne of the asteroids is still reasonably intact, despite the damage inflicted upon it. We should consider dispatching a science party to see what we can find inside it.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:977`

## leviathans.3204.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3204.a&lang=en)

### 中文

准备一支特遣队。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:877`

### English

Prepare an away team.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:978`

## leviathans.3205.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3205.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的科研队完成了对[hive_system.GetName]星系的蜂巢小行星内部的庞大隧道网络的探索。科学家们没有遇到之前袭击我们的那些太空生命体，但是却发现了类似孵化室的残骸。\n\n这些发现可以让我们进一步研究该生命体的生命周期，增强我们对宇宙生物学的认知。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:878`

### English

Our science team has finished exploring the massive network of tunnels and chambers running through the hive asteroid in the [hive_system.GetName] system. No live specimens of the spaceborne creatures that attacked us were encountered, but the scientists did find the remains of some kind of birthing chamber.\n\nThis has given us a great deal of insight into the life cycle of these entities, further expanding our understanding of galactic biology.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:980`

## leviathans.3230.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3230.name&lang=en)

### 中文

召集陨石

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:879`

### English

Meteorites Mustered

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:981`

## leviathans.3230.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleviathans.3230.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[from.solar_system.GetName]恒星系的原住民被惊醒了。尽管仍对多数的太空船保持敌意，但我们大可以放心，在这些全新的原始无人机的保护下，[from.solar_system.GetName]将会是一座坚不可摧的堡垒。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:880`

### English

The inhabitants of the [from.solar_system.GetName] system have awoken. While still hostile to most spacecraft, we can rest easy knowing that [from.solar_system.GetName] will be a bastion under the protection of our new primitive drones.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:982`

## INVESTIGATE_ENCLAVE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AINVESTIGATE_ENCLAVE&lang=en)

### 中文

调查[target_enclave.GetName]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:882`

### English

Investigate [target_enclave.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:985`

## INVESTIGATE_ENCLAVE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AINVESTIGATE_ENCLAVE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们需要多了解一些居住在§Y[Prev.From.GetName]星系§!的神秘外星人。如果他们拥有语言，我们必须进行破译并建立交流。\n\n奖励：£influence£ §G影响力§!或 §S特殊项目§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:883`

### English

We need to learn more about the mysterious aliens residing in the §Y[Prev.From.GetName] System§!. If they possess a language, we must decipher it and establish communications.\n\nReward: £influence£ §GInfluence§! or §SSpecial Project§!

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:986`

## STELLARITE_DEAD_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTELLARITE_DEAD_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

噬星者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:884`

### English

Stellar Devourer

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:987`

## STELLARITE_DEAD_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTELLARITE_DEAD_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

吞食恒星的怪物被击杀了，它的尸体依然散发着最近被它所吞噬的恒星的能源。对它进行解剖也许能够让我们揭秘它是如何消化宇宙中最热的物体的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:885`

### English

The star consumer has been slain, its body still radiating the energy from the star it last fed on. Studying it might reveal the secrets of how it is capable of digesting the warmest objects in the universe.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:988`

## STELLARITE_STAR_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTELLARITE_STAR_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

物归原主

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:886`

### English

Back From Whence It Came

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:989`

## STELLARITE_STAR_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTELLARITE_STAR_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

彻底研究过这个噬星者后，我们找到了一个方法可以将被吞噬的恒星恢复成初始形态，让这个陷入了寒冬的恒星系重新感受到恒星的温暖。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:887`

### English

After having studied the stellar devourer we've found a way to maybe return the consumed star mass to its point of origin, restoring the warmth to the system now stuck in an eternal winter.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:990`

## ENIGMATIC_FORTRESS_1A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AENIGMATIC_FORTRESS_1A&lang=en)

### 中文

爆破小队

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:888`

### English

Demolitions Team

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:991`

## ENIGMATIC_FORTRESS_1A_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AENIGMATIC_FORTRESS_1A_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

派一支拆除专家小组登陆堡垒的侧面尝试炸开气闸。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:889`

### English

Send a squad of demolition experts to land on the side of the fortress and attempt to blast the airlock open.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:992`

## ENIGMATIC_FORTRESS_1B

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AENIGMATIC_FORTRESS_1B&lang=en)

### 中文

局部能源供应

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:890`

### English

Local Power Surge

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:993`

## ENIGMATIC_FORTRESS_1B_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AENIGMATIC_FORTRESS_1B_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

派出一支科研船提供局部能源，尝试将能源局限于堡垒的气闸部分。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:891`

### English

Send a science ship supplying energy locally, attempting to restrict power to the portion of the fortress that powers the airlock.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:994`

## ENIGMATIC_FORTRESS_5A

### 中文

研究黑洞

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:892`

### English

Black Hole Research

## ENIGMATIC_FORTRESS_5A_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AENIGMATIC_FORTRESS_5A_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

或许对黑洞的运作原理的研究可以帮助我们了解堡垒中的暗物质是如何被利用的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:893`

### English

Perhaps the answers to how dark matter is used in the fortress can be found in the workings of a black hole.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:996`

## ENIGMATIC_FORTRESS_5B

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AENIGMATIC_FORTRESS_5B&lang=en)

### 中文

研究母星系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:894`

### English

Home System Research

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:997`

## ENIGMATIC_FORTRESS_5B_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AENIGMATIC_FORTRESS_5B_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

研究附近的恒星也许能够让我们找到一些关于这座堡垒建造过程的线索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:895`

### English

Studying the local star might give some insight into the conditions the builders of the fortress started their work in.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:998`

## ENIGMATIC_FORTRESS_5C

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AENIGMATIC_FORTRESS_5C&lang=en)

### 中文

提供暗物质

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:896`

### English

Supply Dark Matter

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:999`

## ENIGMATIC_FORTRESS_5C_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AENIGMATIC_FORTRESS_5C_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

如果能够将我们自己拥有的暗物质提供给堡垒的核心，也许能够让堡垒系统误以为自己已经在运行，从而停止输送。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:897`

### English

If we provide our own supply of dark matter to the heart of the fortress, we might be able to trick the system that it is already running and stop the flow.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1000`

## ENIGMATIC_FORTRESS_5D

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AENIGMATIC_FORTRESS_5D&lang=en)

### 中文

使用武力

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:898`

### English

Use Force

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1001`

## ENIGMATIC_FORTRESS_5D_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AENIGMATIC_FORTRESS_5D_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的团队成功通过某处被遗弃的管道打开了一条通往堡垒核心的通道。对着核心发射一枚鱼雷也许可以阻止它重启。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:899`

### English

Our team has managed to open up a passage to the core through an empty duct. Firing a torpedo into the core might stop it from restarting.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1002`

## DIMENSIONAL_HORROR_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADIMENSIONAL_HORROR_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

研究位面裂隙

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:900`

### English

Study Dimensional Rifts

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1003`

## DIMENSIONAL_HORROR_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADIMENSIONAL_HORROR_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们最近在[guardians_horror_system.GetName]星系附近驱逐的敌对个体使用了某种强大的维度裂隙连接了我们的世界和他们的扭曲国度。在最大的位面裂隙我们观测到了异常的能量读数。我们应该更接近地去研究这些异常读数。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:901`

### English

The malevolent entity that we recently banished near [guardians_horror_system.GetName] used some kind of powerful dimensional rifts to bridge the gap between our world and its own twisted realm. Anomalous energy readings have been detected where the largest rift was opened. We would do well to study these readings more closely.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1004`

## DISABLED_DREADNOUGHT_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADISABLED_DREADNOUGHT_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

修复无畏舰

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:902`

### English

Repair Dreadnought

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1005`

## DISABLED_DREADNOUGHT_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADISABLED_DREADNOUGHT_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[guardians_dreadnought_system.GetName]星系所击败的巨无霸无畏舰基本上完好无损。虽然需要庞大数量的资源来修复它，但是一旦修复成功我们的舰队就能如虎添翼。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:903`

### English

The hull of the massive dreadnought that we disabled in the [guardians_dreadnought_system.GetName] system is largely intact. Though it will require an enormous amount of resources, we should be able to repair the warship and press it into service with our own fleet.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1006`

## HIVE_ASTEROID_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AHIVE_ASTEROID_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

调查蜂巢小行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:904`

### English

Investigate Hive Asteroid

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1007`

## HIVE_ASTEROID_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AHIVE_ASTEROID_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的部队成功破坏了[hive_system.GetName]星系的其中一个蜂巢小行星。我们最好派出一支科研队前往探索该小行星的内部。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:905`

### English

Our forces have managed to pacify one of the hive asteroids in the [hive_system.GetName] system. We should dispatch a science team to explore the asteroid's interior.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1008`

## DRAGON_INCUBATION_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADRAGON_INCUBATION_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

孵化龙蛋

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:906`

### English

Drake Egg Incubation

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1009`

## DRAGON_INCUBATION_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADRAGON_INCUBATION_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗在龙巢中发现的蛋仍然显示出微弱的生命迹象。我们的科学家认为，模拟龙蛋的孵化环境并最终成功孵化是可行的。然而，我们就算成功孵化了龙蛋，得到的以太龙几乎不可能拥有与其先祖相同的大小和力量，但是如果我们对其进行合理的驯养，这仍然会是我们不可估量资产。\n\n这颗龙蛋脱离了龙巢不能存活太久，我们必须加快行动了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:907`

### English

The egg recovered from the Dragon's Hoard still shows faint signs of life. It should be possible to deduce and emulate an ideal incubation environment for the egg, with the goal of eventually hatching it. Should we be successful in hatching the egg, the resulting Ether Drake is unlikely to match its ancestors in size or power, but with proper domestication will undoubtedly prove to be an incredible asset.\n\nThe egg will not last long outside of the environment it was found in. We must act quickly.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1010`

## TECHNOSPHERE_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATECHNOSPHERE_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

进入球体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:908`

### English

Reaching for the Orb

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1012`

## TECHNOSPHERE_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATECHNOSPHERE_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们现在可以安全地研究环绕着卡冈都亚黑洞旋转的那个金属球体了。不知道在它的核心处能够发现些什么呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:909`

### English

The metallic orb hovering around the Gargantua black hole is now open for study. What will we find once we reach its processing center?

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1013`

## TECHNOSPHERE_BLACKHOLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATECHNOSPHERE_BLACKHOLE&lang=en)

### 中文

理解无限

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:910`

### English

Understanding Infinity

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1014`

## TECHNOSPHERE_BLACKHOLE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATECHNOSPHERE_BLACKHOLE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们需要辅助无限神机扫描卡冈都亚黑洞。虽说想要从黑洞中获取信息是不可能的，但是无限神机却似乎拥有某种查看黑洞的引力数据并回收这些数据的方法。只不过这个过程会§Y非常§!耗时。\n\n我们需要做的就是把运算能力借给它使用，以期稍微减短这段漫长过程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:911`

### English

In order to assist the Infinity Machine, we need to aid it in its scans of the black hole Gargantua. Receiving information from a black hole is impossible, but the Infinity Machine appears to have a method of accessing its gravitational data and getting information out of it. It simply takes a §Yvery§! long time.\n\nWe only need to lend it our processing power, easing its computational burden by a tiny fraction.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1015`

## TECHNOSPHERE_HACKING

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATECHNOSPHERE_HACKING&lang=en)

### 中文

骇入计算机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:912`

### English

Hacking the Calculator

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1016`

## TECHNOSPHERE_HACKING_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATECHNOSPHERE_HACKING_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

骇入这个金属球体的核心程序也许能让我们进一步了解它到底是什么，以及它正在做什么事情。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:913`

### English

Hacking into the metallic sphere's central programming might yield some further insight into what it is and what it is doing.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1017`

## TECHNOSPHERE_DESTRUCTION

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATECHNOSPHERE_DESTRUCTION&lang=en)

### 中文

球体破拆

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:914`

### English

Sphere Fracturing

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1018`

## TECHNOSPHERE_DESTRUCTION_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATECHNOSPHERE_DESTRUCTION_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

派一队拆除专家应该就可以发现这个神秘球形外壳的结构弱点，并借此终结这个无声的威胁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:915`

### English

A team of demolitions experts should be able to identify and exploit points of failure in the integrity of the mysterious sphere's hull, ending the silent menace.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1019`

## EXPLOIT_HIVERS

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AEXPLOIT_HIVERS&lang=en)

### 中文

相似思维

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:916`

### English

Like Minds

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1020`

## EXPLOIT_HIVERS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AEXPLOIT_HIVERS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们与[hiver_system.GetName]小行星中的生物在生理学上颇为相似。他们和我们一样能在真空中茁壮生长。若将我们的意志加至其上他们会变为非常有用的工具。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:917`

### English

We appear to be physiologically quite similar to the creatures inside the asteroids of [hiver_system.GetName]. Just like us, they thrive in the vacuum of space. If we exert our will upon them they could become a useful instrument.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1021`

### English

## infinity_calculations_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainfinity_calculations_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

帮助无限神机后，它给予了我们它的本源秘辛。原来它是个可以自我扩展的处理器单元。这将进一步增强我们的科研能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:959`

### English

Assisting the Infinity Machine made it grant us the secret behind its origin. A processing unit capable of expanding upon itself. This will further enhance our research capabilities.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1070`

## infinity_calculations_hacked

## infinity_calculations_hacked_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainfinity_calculations_hacked_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

骇入这个球形机器后，我们成功揭开了它的运算能力的秘密。这将大幅增强我们的科研能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:961`

### English

Hacking into the spherical machine allowed us to learn the secret behind its processing power. This greatly enhances our research potential.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1072`

## black_hole_pantagruel

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ablack_hole_pantagruel&lang=en)

### 中文

知识之镜

### English

The Mirror of Knowledge

## black_hole_pantagruel_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ablack_hole_pantagruel_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

庞大古埃黑洞成为了我们观察新形成宇宙的魔镜。如果能在其轨道上建造空间站，则可以为我们提供巨大的研究潜力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:963`

### English

The Pantagruel black hole has become a mirror into a newly created universe, opening up tremendous potential for research if we build a station in its orbit.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1074`

## black_hole_pantagruel_research

## black_hole_pantagruel_research_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ablack_hole_pantagruel_research_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们窥视了庞大古埃黑洞中的镜像宇宙，增强了我们的科研能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:965`

### English

We have gazed into the mirror universe of the Pantagruel black hole, enhancing our research capabilities.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1076`

## building_artist

### English

Art Monument

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1083`

## building_artist_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_artist_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

打造这个装饰性艺术纪念碑的人也许是在质问存在的本质以及道德的意义，也许是在琢磨文明的脆弱……也可能是在表达造物的过程必然充满折磨……嗯……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:971`

### English

A decorative art monument questioning the nature of existence and morality. Or maybe it is a pondering on the frailty of civilization... or how trauma is unavoidable in the construction of character... Hmm...

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1084`

## building_artist_patron

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_artist_patron&lang=en)

## building_artist_patron_desc

### 中文

时尚感十足的文化中心，用于散播知识、娱乐以及艺术给普罗大众。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:973`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1086`

## building_artist_patron_gestalt_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_artist_patron_gestalt_desc&lang=en)

一个据称时尚感十足的文化中心，其形状挑战了我们对单一心智的集体理解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:974`

### English

A supposedly fashionable headquarters for the Ministry of Culture, the shape challenges the collective understanding of the Single-Minded ones.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1087`

参加星球艺术节的群众相互践踏，导致整个星球陷入了恐慌混乱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:982`

### English

The Festival of Worlds brought about chaos on this planet as the crowds trampled their fellow citizens.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1095`

## draconic_beats_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adraconic_beats_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的舰队接到指令，当攻击以太巨龙时要以特定的节奏开火。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:984`

### English

Military fleets have been instructed to fire at the Ether Drake in a specific rhythm when engaging the dragon.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1098`

## dimensional_horror_weak_points

### English

## dimensional_horror_weak_points_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adimensional_horror_weak_points_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

如果我们对位面之魇产生的传送门的弱点进行精确打击，它将会很快失去稳定性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:986`

### English

The portal spawned by the Dimensional Horror can be destabilized if we launch pinpoint attacks against certain weak areas.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1101`

## automated_dreadnought_weak_points

## automated_dreadnought_weak_points_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aautomated_dreadnought_weak_points_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

舰队指挥官收到命令，要求集中攻击这艘自动无畏战舰的相关子系统，以关闭它的自动化程序。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:988`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1103`

## asteroid_hive_weak_points_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acurator_buff_stellarite_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

策展人建议我们对噬星者的调节活栓开火，以分散其内部的热能。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:995`

### English

The Curators have instructed us to fire at the Stellar Devourer's regulatory valves in order to disperse its inner heat.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1111`

## curator_buff_sphere

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acurator_buff_sphere&lang=en)

### English

## curator_buff_sphere_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acurator_buff_sphere_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

无限神机的实时威胁更新系统有一个弱点。它不能跟上持续的变化。今后我们要为每艘舰船安排一名船员负责不断调校舰载武器。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:997`

### English

The Infinity Machine's real-time threat update system has one weak spot. It cannot keep up with constant change. We will henceforth assign a crew member on each ship to constantly make calibrations to the ship guns.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1113`

## curator_poi_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acurator_poi_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

知道许多知识是策展人的本业。我们可以买到信息，但是要用他们的知识做些什么则取决于我们自己。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:1001`

### English

It is the Curator's business to know things. We may buy information, but it is up to us to make something of their knowledge.

Source: `localisation/english/leviathans_l_english.yml:1118`

### 中文

一个不断发出神秘光线的金属球体正在环绕§Y卡冈都亚黑洞§!。它现在毫无动静，但会一直保持毫无动静么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/leviathans_l_simp_chinese.yml:1005`

### English
